SolarBench provides an extensible foundation for fair comparison and methodological innovation in solar nowcasting.

</details>


### [66] [One Perturbation Is Not Enough: Identifiability and Blind Baselines for Behavioral AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06190)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Mariam Sohail,Ahmed Abdala,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明行为评测(behavioral evaluation)所需扰动数量是固定的：对于n个输入至少需要n个独立扰动才能认证系统确实使用了某输入；单一扰动报告无法提供此类认证。作者推导了“盲读”策略(完全不读取输入的策略)在行为测试中的得分闭式表达式，发现该得分远非零且未被现有probe报告。在视觉-语言模型预测物理量的任务中，用维度分析确定的三个完整扰动集进行实验，三个模型得分均显著低于自身盲读界而非高于它，因其默认输出少量“圆整校准值”，且与被明确要求忽略视频的同一模型不可区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为评测通过扰动输入并观察输出变化来认证系统是否使用了该输入，但现有方法依赖单一或少量扰动，缺少理论保证。作者希望厘清行为认证所需的扰动数量与设计条件，并揭示当前评测实践（尤其是多模态模型评测）的系统性缺陷。

Method: 采用线性测量框架：将策略中的“响应比”(response ratio)建模为对数响应向量对输入指数向量的线性测量。方法分为理论推导与实验验证两部分：(1)证明认证需要扰动对数张成输入空间，即至少n个独立扰动；(2)推导盲读策略得分的闭式表达式；(3)在视觉-语言模型上以维度分析确定正确物理量响应，施加三个完整独立扰动进行实证评测。

Result: 扰动数量下界为n（对于n个输入）；不完整设计恰好混淆沿设计矩阵核方向有差异的策略；锐化单个扰动不能替代增加独立扰动。盲读策略得分闭式解远离零但未见有probe报告。三个视觉-语言模型在完整识别集上均得分显著低于自身盲读界，因其默认输出少量固定“圆整校准值”；重标签响应无一移动单个指数；且与忽略视频指令的同一模型不可区分。

Conclusion: 行为认证必须使用至少n个独立扰动构成完整识别设计；单一或非独立扰动无法提供有效认证。当前主流视觉-语言模型并未真正“读取”视频中的物理信息，而是依赖固定的校准默认值，其行为与忽略视频的模型无差异，说明现有评测框架及模型能力均存在根本性缺陷。

Abstract: Behavioral evaluations perturb an input and read the induced change in the output in order to certify that a system uses that input. We show that the number of perturbations such a certificate requires is fixed, and that reporting a single perturbation cannot supply it. Where a response ratio is a property of the policy rather than of the test items, the behavioral record is a linear measurement of an exponent vector recording how much the output depends on each input, so perturbations identify input use exactly when their logarithms span the input space. At least $n$ are needed for $n$ inputs, an incomplete design confuses precisely the policies differing along the kernel of its design matrix, and sharpening a perturbation never substitutes for adding an independent one. We also derive in closed form the score such a test awards a policy that reads nothing, which is far from zero and which none of the probes we survey reports. Instantiating this where the correct response is fixed by dimensional analysis, we run a complete identifying set of three perturbations on three vision--language models reporting a physical quantity from video. All three score far below their own blind bound rather than above it, because each defaults to one of a small set of round calibration values that never matches what the scale asserts; none moves its relabeling response by a single exponent, and none is separable from the same model instructed to ignore the video.

</details>


### [67] [PhysWeep: Does a Video Generator Realize the Physics You Ask For?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06207)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysWeep提出一种固定、无标签的审计方法，用于测量图像生成视频模型是否忠实于用户请求的物理参数值，而非仅看起来"像真实的"。通过黑盒方式从生成像素中恢复实际实现的物理参数，并报告可追踪性、参数偏差以及支持何种失效机制。在三个开源生成器、六个扫描轴上的测试发现了一种此前未记载的失效模式：当生成可追踪运动时，两个模型会自信地拟合到由采样种子而非请求决定的一小组固定错误值上，这与文献预期的两种失效机制均不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的"合理性"（plausibility）检测无法衡量生成视频是否编码了正确的物理参数值——一段视频可以看起来自然，却携带了错误的物理参数。目前没有基准能直接测量这一差距，因此需要一种新的审计工具来弥合这一空缺。

Method: PhysWeep将冻结的生成器视为黑盒，通过确定性模拟器作为正控制验证每个分数可精确检验，从生成像素中恢复实际实现的物理参数值，统计生成何时可追踪、实现值与请求值的偏差距离，以及测试文献中提出的两种失效机制。对三个开源生成器在六个扫描轴上进行系统审计。

Result: 发现一个具体、可复现且此前未记载的失效模式：在产生可追踪运动时，两个模型生成高度自信、拟合良好的动力学，但收敛到由采样种子而非请求选择的一小组固定错误值。该结果在两个独立模型家族、两个物理系统和独立追踪器上均可复现。这种失效既不匹配先验回退（reversion）也不匹配基于案例的钳制（clamping）机制——因为回退目标是种子条件性的而非单一全局默认值，留一法选择规则拒绝了这两种假设；在可估计响应范围内，忠实度斜率与零在统计上无差异。对种子取平均的基准永远看不到此问题，因为每个样本都自信地锁定在错误的常数值上。

Conclusion: Plausibility评分在结构上对这类"每个样本锁定错误常数"的失效模式视而不见；PhysWeep提供了直接测量生成器物理参数忠实度的协议、套件和分析代码。这表明图像转视频模型并非以条件一致的方式编码物理动力学，其参数实现受采样种子支配而非用户请求支配。

Abstract: Image-to-video generators are often credited with absorbing physical dynamics as implicit world models, a claim the community currently checks with plausibility scores that ask whether a clip looks consistent with real-world motion. Plausibility is the wrong test on its own, because a clip can look natural while encoding the wrong value of the governing physical parameter, and no existing benchmark measures this gap directly. PhysWeep closes it with a fixed, label-free audit, treating a frozen generator as a black box, recovering the realized parameter from generated pixels, and reporting how often generation is trackable at all, how far the realized value sits from the requested one, and which, if either, of the literature's two proposed failure mechanisms the data support. A deterministic-simulator positive control confirms every score is exactly checkable. Applied to three open generators across six sweep axes, PhysWeep finds a specific, reproducible, previously undocumented failure. Conditional on producing trackable motion, two of the three generate confident, well-fit dynamics that converge to one of a small number of fixed, wrong values selected by the sampling seed rather than by the request, reproducing across two independent model families, two physical systems, and an independent tracker. It matches neither the prior reversion nor the case-based clamping the literature anticipates, because the reversion target is seed-conditional rather than a single global default, and a leave-one-out selection rule rejects both; the in-range faithfulness slope is statistically indistinguishable from zero wherever a response is estimable at all. A benchmark averaging over seeds would never see this: each sample is confidently locked to a wrong constant, exactly the failure a plausibility score is structurally blind to. We release the protocol, suite, and analysis code.

</details>


### [68] [From Gaze to Meaning: A Training-Free AI Agent for Unified Grounding and Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06208)
*Shayan Nasiriboukani,Sara Atito,Mohammad Nezamipour,Muhammad Awais*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出无需训练的Gaze Target Agent (GTA)，利用预训练视觉-语言模型结合视觉引导提示和基于记忆的检索策略，实现眼动目标预测、注意力定位和物体识别等注视引导的推理任务，在GazeFollow和GazeHOI基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人类注意力理解方法通常依赖大量训练的模型，缺乏可解释性，且难以在没有大量监督的情况下联合推理注视目标、被注意物体和视觉定位。本文旨在解决这一瓶颈，探索无需额外训练的解决方案。

Method: 方法基于预训练视觉-语言模型作为核心，通过视觉引导提示（visually guided prompts）增强模型能力，并针对高不确定性样本采用基于记忆的检索策略（memory-based retrieval）以提升性能，整个过程无需额外训练。

Result: 在GazeFollow和GazeHOI两个基准上达到了最先进的定量性能。定性结果显示该方法能提供细粒度的语义预测，即使地面真值标注错误时也能正确预测目标，并且不受词汇表约束，保持灵活性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了无需训练即可利用预训练视觉-语言模型实现鲁棒且可解释的注视引导推理，弥补了传统方法在可解释性和跨任务泛化上的不足，为眼动理解领域提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Understanding human attention is fundamental for scene interpretation, yet existing approaches often rely on heavily trained models that lack interpretability. Prior methods struggle to jointly reason about gaze targets, attended objects, and visual grounding without extensive supervision. To the best of our knowledge, this work introduces the first training-free Gaze Target Agent (GTA) for gaze-guided reasoning across tasks such as gaze target prediction, attention localization, and object identification. This is achieved by leveraging pretrained vision-language models, augmenting them with visually guided prompts, and employing a memory-based retrieval strategy for high-uncertainty samples to improve performance without additional training. We evaluate our approach using both quantitative metrics and qualitative results. Quantitatively, our method achieves state of the art performance on the GazeFollow and GazeHOI benchmarks. Qualitatively, our agent provides detailed semantic predictions, predicts the correct targets even when ground truth labels are wrong, and remains flexible without vocabulary constraints.

</details>


### [69] [Branch-Centric Tokenization and Test-Time Augmentation for Skeleton Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06218)
*Zhengyuan Li,Chuanyu Pan,Yuanming Hu,Raymond Yeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的自动骨架生成自回归框架，通过引入分支中心化tokenization（branch-centric tokenization）来解决分支结构编码问题，并引入视图增强生成（view-augmented generation）作为测试时增强手段来提升预测准确性。在Articulation-XL2.0数据集上相比最强基线Auto-Connect将CD-J2B误差降低了16.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动骨架生成方法存在两个核心问题：一是难以将分支结构有效地编码进token序列（标准BFS式序列化会破坏结构邻近性）；二是没有充分利用测试时的计算资源来提升预测质量。

Method: 1) 分支中心化tokenization：一种分支感知的表示方法，将结构相关的元素在序列中相邻放置，并直接将连接关系编码进序列，相比BFS式序列化产生更紧凑的序列。2) 视图增强生成：一种测试时增强过程，对输入网格施加沿坐标轴的旋转，将所有预测映射回公共坐标系，并基于网格覆盖率和不同视图预测间的一致性来选择最终骨架。

Result: 实验表明方法优于现有先进骨架预测方法。在Articulation-XL2.0数据集上，相比最强的可直接比较的基线Auto-Connect，CD-J2B误差降低了16.9%。在野外网格上的定性结果进一步展示了跨多样输入的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过分支中心的tokenization和测试时的多视图增强，该自回归框架在不增加模型容量的情况下显著提升了骨架生成精度，且在多样化真实世界输入上具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Automatic skeleton generation involves predicting both joint positions and skeletal connectivity. However, existing approaches struggle to encode branch structures into token sequences and do not use test-time computation effectively. We study these choices within a unified autoregressive framework. First, we introduce branch-centric tokenization, a branch-aware representation that places structurally related elements next to each other and encodes connectivity directly in the sequence. Compared with standard BFS-style serialization, this representation yields more compact sequences. Second, we introduce view-augmented generation, a test-time augmentation procedure that applies axis-aligned rotations to the input mesh, maps all predictions back to a common frame, and selects the final skeleton based on mesh coverage and consistency among predictions from different views. Experiments show that our method achieves better skeleton prediction accuracy than state-of-the-art methods. In particular, our method reduces the CD-J2B error by 16.9% on the Articulation-XL2.0 dataset compared to the strongest directly comparable baseline, Auto-Connect. Qualitative results on in-the-wild meshes further demonstrate generalization across diverse inputs.

</details>


### [70] [Spatial Attention Supervision for Defect Localization: Exploiting Ground-Truth Masks as Training Signal in Diffusion-Augmented Defect Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06232)
*Sajjad Rezvani Boroujeni,Muskan Saraf,Gnana Tulasi Makineni,Tom Bush,Hossein Abedi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用工业检测数据集中原本仅用于评估的真值缺陷掩码作为训练时的空间监督信号，通过激活级注意力对齐损失引导卷积特征图聚焦缺陷区域，并结合DDPM扩散生成图像进行混合监督训练，在MVTec-AD瓶子基准上对85个模型进行了系统评测，发现注意力引导训练可显著提升基于激活的定位性能（Pixel-AUROC最高提升+18.7%），且不影响分类精度，但效果依赖于架构空间表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业检测数据集中已有高质量的真值缺陷掩码，但传统上仅在评估阶段使用，训练阶段被浪费。作者希望将这些评估掩码复用为训练过程中的空间监督信号，让分类网络不仅学会"预测什么"（缺陷类别），还学会"看哪里"（缺陷位置），从而在不牺牲分类性能的前提下提升模型的注意力密集性与可解释性。

Method: 在分类网络的训练中引入激活级注意力对齐损失（activation-based attention alignment loss），将卷积特征图引导至缺陷区域。采用混合监督框架：有掩码的真实样本提供空间监督，无掩码的DDPM扩散生成样本提供数量扩充，实现"合成图像贡献数量、掩码贡献空间精度"的互补。在MVTec-AD瓶子基准上，以2x2数据/训练因子设计评估四个CNN骨干（EfficientNetB0、ResNet50、ConvNeXt-T等）加Swin-V2-T transformer，共85个模型，定位性能用排除于梯度更新的留出缺陷图像上的Pixel-AUROC衡量。

Result: (1) 注意力引导训练使EfficientNetB0定位Pixel-AUROC提升+18.0%（p=0.005, d=2.6），ResNet50提升+18.7%（p=0.008），八个CNN设置中四个达统计显著，分类精度无显著变化；(2) EfficientNetB0上数据x训练模式交互效应显著（p=0.002），呈超加性效果（联合+13.6% vs 个体效应和+1.6%）；(3) 空间表征较弱的架构收益最大，ConvNeXt-T无效果归因于深度卷积激活产生空间无信息的通道均值图；(4) 无监督PatchCore仍是最强定位器（Pixel-AUROC=0.983），为有监督增益提供了参照背景。

Conclusion: 已有的评估掩码可以切实用作训练信号，可测量且可复现地改善缺陷分类器的注意力定位能力，且不损害分类精度。该方法的效果具有架构依赖性——受益程度与模型空间表征质量相关，同时发现即使经过监督引导，专门的深度局部特征定位方法（如PatchCore）在纯定位任务上仍具优势。$$

Abstract: Ground-truth defect masks in industrial inspection datasets are typically reserved for evaluation. This paper repurposes them as spatial supervision signals during training of classification networks, teaching a model not just what to predict but where to look. The method adds an activation-based attention alignment loss that steers convolutional feature maps toward defect regions, in a mixed-supervision formulation that also accommodates samples without masks, such as diffusion-generated images. Combined with DDPM augmentation, synthetic images contribute quantity while masks contribute spatial precision. We evaluate 85 models (four CNN backbones under a 2x2 data/training factorial over five seeds, plus a Swin-V2-T transformer baseline) on the MVTec-AD bottle benchmark, with localization measured on held-out defect images excluded from classifier gradient updates. Main findings: (1) attention-guided training improves activation-based localization (Pixel-AUROC) by +18.0% for EfficientNetB0 with augmentation (p=0.005, Cohen's d=2.6) and +18.7% for ResNet50 (p=0.008), significant in four of eight CNN settings (uncorrected for multiple comparisons) with no significant change in classification; (2) for EfficientNetB0 a data x training-mode interaction is significant (p=0.002), consistent with a super-additive effect (+13.6% combined vs +1.6% summed individual effects); (3) architectures with weaker spatial representations benefit most, whereas ConvNeXt-T shows no effect, apparently because its depthwise-convolution activations yield spatially uninformative channel-mean maps; (4) unsupervised PatchCore remains the strongest localizer (Pixel-AUROC=0.983), contextualizing the supervised gains. These results show that existing evaluation masks can act as practical training signals that measurably and reproducibly improve where defect classifiers attend.

</details>


### [71] [VDiff-Bench: A Challenging Benchmark for Fine-Grained Image Difference Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06245)
*Yixin Wan,Tianle Zheng,Kai-Wei Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VDiff-Bench，一个用于细粒度图像差异识别的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）基准测试，包含1,756道四选一选择题，覆盖10种变化类别，用于诊断MLLM在视觉比较任务上的薄弱环节。实验表明11个模型在细粒度比较上普遍表现脆弱，尤其在噪声和纹理等低级变化上性能大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在一般视觉理解任务（如视觉问答）上表现强劲，却在"识别两幅相似图像之间变化"这一基础比较能力上经常失败。已有的单图视觉语言任务无法暴露这一问题，因此需要专门的基准来诊断MLLM的视觉比较能力。

Method: 构建VDiff-Bench基准：包含1,756道四选一问题，每道题提供一对图像输入及四个选项（真实差异、两个硬负样本描述、一个"无差异"干扰项），覆盖10种变化类别（位置、运动、局部颜色、整体颜色、出现/消失、噪声/分辨率、纹理、替换/尺寸、OCR/文本、光照）。负样本基于真实变化条件精心构造，要求模型区分真实变化与语义相近的干扰描述。对11个开源与闭源MLLM进行全面评测。

Result: 实验发现：模型在不同来源和变化类别上表现不均；在语义变化上表现较好，但在噪声和纹理等细微低级变化上持续失败。例如三个7-8B规模开源模型在语义变化上得分为52.5-70.6%，而在低级变化上仅为8.7-33.3%，常错误地认为两图无差异。此外，尽管多个闭源模型表现强劲，Grok 4.3在识别噪声和纹理差异上出现显著性能下滑，落后于Kimi K2.5和K3等大型开源模型。

Conclusion: VDiff-Bench为评估MLLM的比较视觉理解能力提供了针对性的诊断工具，揭示了标准单图视觉语言任务无法捕捉的能力缺陷，为改进MLLM的细粒度视觉比较能力提供了参考方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) perform strongly on general visual understanding tasks such as visual question answering, yet they often struggle with a basic comparative skill: identifying what has changed between two similar images. We introduce VDiff-Bench, a challenging multiple-choice benchmark for fine-grained Image Difference Identification. VDiff-Bench contains 1,756 four-way questions over image pairs and covers 10 change categories: position, motion, regional image color, overall image color, appearance/disappearance, noise/resolution, texture, substitution/size, OCR/text, and illumination. Each question corresponds to two image inputs with 4 choices: the true difference, two hard negative descriptions, and a "no difference" distractor. To make the task challenging, we specifically curate ground-truth-conditioned negatives that require models to distinguish the actual change from nearby semantic alternatives. Experiments with 11 state-of-the-art open- and closed-source MLLMs show that fine-grained visual comparison remains brittle: models exhibit uneven performance across sources and change categories, with persistent failures on subtle low-level changes like noises and textures. For instance, three 7-8B-scale open-source MLLMs score 52.5-70.6% on semantic changes but only 8.7-33.3% on low-level changes like noise and texture, falsely assuming no changes between two image inputs. Surprisingly, despite strong performance of other closed-source commercial models, Grok 4.3 demonstrate remarkable performance drop on identifying noise and texture differences between images, falling significantly behind large open-source models like Kimi K2.5 and K3. Overall, VDiff-Bench provides a targeted diagnostic for evaluating comparative visual understanding in MLLMs, exposing failures that are not captured by standard single-image vision-language tasks.

</details>


### [72] [MobileVLA-R1 2.0: RL-Enhanced Reasoning for Mobile Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06251)
*Ting Huang,Yue Huang,Zeyu Zhang,Shuicheng Yan,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MobileVLA-R1 2.0是一个强化学习增强的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)框架，通过显式耦合结构化具身推理与可执行移动机器人控制来解决高层语义推理与低层运动操控控制之间的鸿沟。该框架采用监督式思维链对齐和强化学习学习多粒度具身推理，并通过推理条件动作解码器统一感知-推理-动作接口，在VLN-CE、QUARD及Unitree Go2/G1真实部署中均优于强VLA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA方法在移动机器人上常依赖隐式推理或单一化动作预测，难以在长程决策中保持连贯性，同时难以生成精确且自适应的机器人动作。高层语义推理与低层运动操控之间存在持续鸿沟，需要更显式地耦合结构化推理与可执行控制。

Method: 1) 通过监督式思维链(CoT)对齐和强化学习学习多粒度具身轨迹推理，提升推理到动作的一致性；2) 提出推理条件动作解码器，将多模态推理表示映射到任务级动作目标，再由机器人控制器转换为具身特定指令；3) 构建统一的感知-推理-动作接口，将高层动作生成与机器人特定执行解耦。

Result: 在VLN-CE上SR平均提升1.6点，在真实G1移动操控任务上全程成功率较MobileVLA-R1提升10.0点；在语言引导导航、四足控制和仿人移动操控等多种场景中均稳定超越强VLA基线，展现跨平台的鲁棒长程指令跟随和闭环执行能力。

Conclusion: MobileVLA-R1 2.0通过显式结构化推理与强化学习增强，结合推理条件动作解码器实现推理与执行的有效耦合，弥合了高层语义推理与低层物理控制之间的鸿沟，展示了在不同移动机器人平台上的泛化性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Grounding natural-language instructions into reliable and executable actions remains a fundamental challenge for vision-language-action (VLA) systems on mobile robots, due to the persistent gap between high-level semantic reasoning and low-level locomotion and manipulation control. Existing approaches often rely on implicit reasoning or monolithic action prediction, making it difficult to maintain coherent long-horizon decision making while producing precise and adaptable robot actions. To address this challenge, we propose MobileVLA-R1 2.0, an RL-enhanced VLA framework that explicitly couples structured embodied reasoning with executable mobile robot control. The framework learns multi-granularity reasoning over embodied trajectories through supervised Chain-of-Thought (CoT) alignment and reinforcement learning, improving reasoning-to-action consistency beyond purely behavioral supervision. To support both locomotion and manipulation, we further introduce a reasoning-conditioned action decoder that maps multimodal reasoning representations to task-level action targets, which are subsequently translated into embodiment-specific commands by robot controllers. This design provides a unified perception-reasoning-action interface while decoupling high-level action generation from robot-specific actuation. We conduct extensive evaluations on language-guided navigation, quadruped control, and humanoid mobile manipulation, covering VLN-CE, QUARD, and real-world deployments on Unitree Go2 and G1 robots. MobileVLA-R1 2.0 consistently outperforms strong VLA baselines, achieving an average 1.6 point improvement in SR on VLN-CE and a 10.0 point improvement in full-task success on real-world G1 mobile manipulation tasks over MobileVLA-R1, while demonstrating robust long-horizon instruction following and closed-loop execution across different robotic platforms.

</details>


### [73] [One Model, Two Worlds: Bidirectional Sonar-Optical Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06253)
*Shengji Jin,Trung Tien Dong,Ahmed Lamidi,Chen Chen,Xiaomin Lin,Yi Sheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种统一的双向成像声呐与光学相机翻译模型DARB（方向非对称现实主义桥），通过非对称路径路由方向特定的物理先验，并配以自适应现实监督ARS来差异化调整感知训练的时机、位置与强度，使单一双向模型在双向翻译上均能匹敌甚至超越单方向专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成像声呐与光学相机之间的相互翻译对水下感知有价值，但分别训练两个方向的独立模型会重复占用存储和计算资源。现有统一双向模型大多将两个方向对称处理，忽略了二者图像形成物理过程的根本差异，导致性能不佳。

Method: 提出Direction-Asymmetric Realism Bridge (DARB)：保留共享的扩散桥主干，但通过非对称路径注入方向特定的物理先验——声呐到光学翻译使用距离感知调制，光学到声呐翻译采用极坐标射线依赖处理。进一步提出Adaptive Realism Supervision (ARS)，根据重建质量和梯度平衡动态决定感知监督的施加时机、位置与强度，以解决统一训练中因对称感知调度导致的性能损失。

Result: DARB与ARS使单个双向模型在声呐到光学方向仅以0.11 dB PSNR差距逼近单方向专家，在光学到声呐方向以0.70 FID超越单方向专家，且在八个指标中的七个上胜过两个独立训练的BBDM模型。此外，给定方法的对称训练会使声呐到光学方向PSNR下降2.60 dB。

Conclusion: 共享生成模型并不需要共享物理过程，通过共享主干加非对称物理先验路径以及自适应的非对称感知监督，可以在单一双向模型中同时取得优异的两向翻译性能，验证了物理非对称与训练非对称的必要性。

Abstract: Translating between imaging sonar and optical cameras is valuable for underwater perception, but supporting both directions with separate models duplicates storage and computation. A unified bidirectional model is therefore attractive, yet existing approaches largely treat the two directions symmetrically despite their fundamentally different image-formation physics. We argue that sharing a generative model does not require sharing the physics. We introduce the Direction-Asymmetric Realism Bridge (DARB), which retains a shared diffusion-bridge trunk while routing direction-specific physical priors through asymmetric pathways: range-aware modulation for sonar-to-optical translation and polar ray-dependent processing for optical-to-sonar translation. We further show that symmetry in training is also costly: applying a common realism schedule reduces sonar-to-optical PSNR by 2.60 dB. Our Adaptive Realism Supervision (ARS) instead determines when, where, and how strongly perceptual supervision is applied from reconstruction quality and gradient balance. Together, DARB and ARS enable one bidirectional model to match the sonar-to-optical specialist within 0.11 dB PSNR, outperform the optical-to-sonar specialist by 0.70 FID, and surpass two independently trained BBDMs on seven of eight metrics.

</details>


### [74] [Adapting Vision Foundation Models to Acoustics for Pose-Free 3D Sonar Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06261)
*Kevin Zhang,Jingxi Chen,Mohamad Qadri,Russell Shomberg,Michael Kaess,Jia-Bin Huang,Adithya Pediredla,Christopher Metzler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过利用视觉基础模型与声纳之间的几何关系以及基于物理的噪声模型进行合成数据生成，将大规模RGB视觉基础模型高效适配到声纳领域，首次实现了基于声纳的无位姿三维重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境存在浑浊、低能见度等问题，使常规RGB视觉基础模型无法适用；而水下领域又缺乏声纳基础模型及其所需的大规模开放声纳数据集，从零训练声纳基础模型不切实际，因此需要将已有视觉基础模型适配到声纳模态。

Method: (1) 利用声纳与光学RGB两种传感模态之间的几何关系进行适配；(2) 采用精确的基于物理的噪声模型生成合成数据，以弥补真实声纳数据稀缺的问题，从而高效地将视觉基础模型迁移到声纳设置中。

Result: 实验首次展示了基于声纳的无位姿（pose-free）三维重建能力，验证了所提出的声纳适配模型的有效性及新能力的实现。

Conclusion: 通过几何关系与物理噪声模型驱动的合成数据生成，大规模RGB视觉基础模型可高效适配到声纳领域，突破声纳数据稀缺瓶颈，并在水下场景中实现此前不具备的无位姿3D重建能力。

Abstract: Vision foundation models trained on Internet-scale RGB datasets enable remarkable capabilities across a range of tasks, from text-to-video generation to few-shot 3D scene reconstruction. An acoustic foundation model trained on large-scale sonar datasets could enable similar capabilities in the underwater domain, where turbidity and low-visibility conditions make conventional RGB foundation models inapplicable. Unfortunately, a lack of freely available large-scale sonar datasets makes training such a model from scratch impractical. In this work, we demonstrate that vision foundation models can be efficiently adapted to the sonar setting by (1) exploiting the geometric relationship between the two sensing modalities and (2) employing accurate physics-based noise models for synthetic data generation. The resulting sonar adaptation models enable new capabilities: For the first time, we experimentally demonstrate sonar-based pose-free 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [75] [A Comparative Study of GAN-Based Deep Learning Models for Pneumonia Detection in Chest X-Rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06276)
*Roshan Paudel,Aashish Ghimire,Pramod Acharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究在胸部X光片上评估了VGG19、MobileNetV2、ResNet50及自定义CNN对肺炎分类的性能，并探索了基于GAN的合成数据增强。MobileNetV2取得最高准确率88%且类别均衡；自定义CNN的肺炎召回率92.67%、精确率79.43%，存在精确率-召回率权衡。GAN增强实验中训练准确率接近100%但验证准确率仅约50%，低于真实数据验证结果，未证明增强能提升验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较多种CNN架构在胸部X光肺炎分类中的表现，并探索GAN合成数据增强能否进一步提升分类性能，弥补肺炎影像数据不足的问题。

Method: 采用四种模型（VGG19、MobileNetV2、ResNet50、自定义CNN）进行肺炎分类评估；使用GAN生成合成肺炎图像并与真实图像混合进行数据增强实验；以准确率、F1分数、精确率、召回率、混淆矩阵及训练曲线作为评价指标。

Result: MobileNetV2报告最高准确率88%且类别性能均衡；自定义CNN肺炎召回率92.67%、精确率79.43%，呈现精确率-召回率权衡。GAN增强实验中，VGG19训练准确率约达100%，但验证准确率接近50%，低于真实数据验证准确率，未显示出GAN增强对验证性能的提升。

Conclusion: 不同CNN架构在准确率和肺炎召回率上存在显著差异；当前GAN增广实验未展现出验证性能收益，提示需进一步评估合成图像质量并优化训练设置。

Abstract: This study evaluates pneumonia classification in chest X-rays using VGG19, MobileNetV2, ResNet50, and a custom CNN, and explores Generative Adversarial Network (GAN)-based synthetic data augmentation. MobileNetV2 achieved the highest reported accuracy of 88% with balanced class-wise performance. The custom CNN achieved pneumonia recall of 92.67% and precision of 79.43%, highlighting a precision-recall trade-off. Accuracy, F1-score, precision, recall, confusion matrices, and training curves were used to assess performance.
  Synthetic pneumonia images were combined with real images to investigate whether augmentation could improve classification performance. In the reported VGG19 comparison, augmented-data training accuracy reached approximately 100%, while validation accuracy remained near 50%, below the real-data validation accuracy. This experiment therefore did not demonstrate a validation-performance benefit from GAN augmentation. The classifier comparison highlights differences in accuracy and pneumonia recall, while the augmentation experiment indicates the need for further evaluation of synthetic-image quality and training settings.

</details>

### [76] [When Agent Governance Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05531)
*Michael Ray Johnson,Linda Naimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了受治理的自主多智能体产品组织（GAMPO）框架，并探讨了"治理收益受模型冗余能力门控且具有领域与模型特异性"的边界条件。研究中，一个"verify your writes"句子使开放模型的pass@1从2/20翻倍至4/20；在前沿模型上，统一程序将事前授权从24%提升至40%，但在另一模型上收益为零（归因于稳定的推荐覆盖倾向）；将通用程序替换为基于任务定义完成（definition-of-done）的答案盲规范后，在best-of-five自洽性下事前授权达84%（单次尝试68%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前尚无规范阐明应如何设计与评估在护栏内追求自生成目标的受治理自主AI智能体组织，作者旨在填补这一空白，提供可运行的规范与治理边界条件的证据。

Method: 第一部分通过对321个来源的文档质性证据综合，整合代理、敏捷、平台与治理理论，综合出GAMPO框架为可运行规范；第二部分在CHI-Bench长时域医疗基准上对开放模型与前沿模型进行提示层实例化的探测实验，比较通用程序与任务化definition-of-done两种治理方式。

Result: 治理收益受模型冗余能力门控且具领域/模型特异性：开放模型上完整程序无可靠收益，但单个"verify your writes"句子使pass@1从2/20翻倍到4/20；前沿模型上同一脚手架使事前授权从24%升至40%，在另一模型上净收益为零（源于推荐覆盖倾向）；以答案盲、仅锚定案例策略与公开标准的任务级definition-of-done替代通用程序后，事前授权best-of-five自洽达84%（单次68%），利用率管理达44%，而护理管理遭遇主观内容质量墙。

Conclusion: 贡献在于一个可命名、可审计的框架及能力门控的证据：治理规模应与冗余能力匹配；在前沿水平上，案例锚定的规范优于统一程序。结果属探索性：部分实例化、每个细胞样本量小（n=5-25）且仅单次试验。

Abstract: No specification says how a governed autotelic AI agent organization, where agents pursue self-generated goals inside guardrails, should be designed and evaluated. We answer in two parts. First, we synthesize the Governed Autotelic Multi-Agent Product Organization (GAMPO) framework from a document-based qualitative evidence synthesis of 321 sources, integrating agency, agile, platform, and governance theory into a runnable specification. Second, we probe a prompt-layer instantiation of GAMPO on CHI-Bench, a long-horizon healthcare benchmark, across open and frontier models. The result is a boundary condition: governance benefit is gated by a model's spare capacity and is domain- and model-specific. On capacity-constrained open models the full procedure yields no reliable benefit, whereas a single "verify your writes" sentence doubles task success (pass@1 2/20 to 4/20). At the frontier the same scaffold lifts prior-authorization 24% to 40% but nets zero on another model, a gap traced to a stable recommendation-override disposition. A second result refines the first: replacing the generic procedure with an answer-blind, per-task definition-of-done, keyed only to the case's own policy and published standards, never the hidden key, raises prior-authorization to 84% under best-of-five self-consistency (68% single-attempt, confirmed by a held-out board) and utilization-management to 44%, while care-management meets a subjective content-quality wall. The contribution is a named, auditable framework and capability-gated evidence that governance should be sized to spare capacity, and that at the frontier a case-grounded specification beats a uniform procedure. Findings are exploratory: partial instantiation, small per-cell samples (n = 5-25), and single trials.

</details>


### [77] [TamilEOT: A Dataset and Model for Semantic End-of-Turn Detection in Tamil Telephone Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05631)
*Santhoshkumar V*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布了 TamilEOT，一个包含 18,485 个标注语轮边界的数据集（源自 116 段真实泰米尔语电话录音），以及两个基于 Smart Turn v3 微调的纯音频语轮结束检测模型。在 30 通未见电话的 4,168 个片段上，准确率从零样本的 70.30% 提升至 83.71%（8.7 MB）和 86.13%（21 MB），ROC-AUC 从 0.751 提升至 0.921，且单线程 CPU 推理时间低于 150 ms。论文还报告了构建成本与大量负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音智能体需要在每次停顿处判断用户是否已说完。若缺乏语言模型，只能依赖固定静音超时，造成"打断用户"或"每轮白白等待"的两难。现有开源语轮结束检测器均未覆盖南印度语言（如泰米尔语），亟需为该语言构建公开可用的语轮结束检测资源。

Method: 从 116 段真实泰米尔语电话录音中切出 18,485 个标注语轮边界；使用规则派生标注并辅以盲听人工校验；随后发现规则标注在负类上仅 44.4% 准确率（低于随机），改用成本 5.69 美元的音频-LLM 标注器（人工一致率达 97.5%）。基于 Smart Turn v3 微调两个纯音频检测器（8.7 MB 和 21 MB）。同时评估了编码器容量、随机种子、生产级 VAD 与流式适配、推理延迟等多项因素。

Result: 在 30 通未见电话的 4,168 个片段上，准确率从零样本 70.30% 提升至 83.71%（小模型）和 86.13%（大模型）；ROC-AUC 从 0.751 提升至 0.921。两模型在笔记本 CPU 单线程下均可在 150 ms 内完成推理。论文测量显示仅有编码器容量显著影响结果；相同配置与种子下的三次运行准确率跨度达 0.87 个百分点，构成其他差异的意义下限；叠加生产 VAD 与流式适配后准确率再降 2.60 个百分点，且 7.8% 的边界从未呈递给模型。

Conclusion: 作者开源了全部数据、权重、代码及每项负面结果，认为构建语轮结束检测器的最大成本与不确定性不在模型微调本身，而在于标注的真实性——规则标注可能回答与模型任务不同的问题，需要更可靠的标注手段（如音频-LLM）；同时强调在生产管线中报告指标需纳入 VAD 与流式适配带来的真实损耗，并建议以同配置多轮运行的方差作为判断实验差异是否有意义的基准下限。

Abstract: A voice agent has to decide, at every pause, whether the user has finished speaking. Without a model of the language that decision falls back to a fixed silence timeout: set it short and the agent interrupts, set it long and every turn pays the full wait. Open semantic end-of-turn detectors exist, but to our knowledge none covers a South Indian language. We release TamilEOT: 18,485 labelled turn boundaries cut from 116 real Tamil telephone conversations, and two audio-only detectors fine-tuned from Smart Turn v3. On a held-out split of 4,168 clips from 30 unseen calls, accuracy rises from 70.30% zero-shot to 83.71% (8.7 MB) and 86.13% (21 MB); ROC-AUC rises from 0.751 to 0.921. Both models run in under 150 ms single-threaded on a laptop CPU. We also report what building it cost. Rule-derived labels, checked against a blind human listening pass, were right 95.9% of the time on the positive class and 44.4% on the negative class, which is below chance, because the rule answered a different question than the model is asked. Replacing them with an audio-LLM labeller measured at 97.5% human agreement cost US$5.69. Of every training lever we measured, only encoder capacity moved the result; three runs at identical config and seed span 0.87 accuracy points, which is the floor below which none of our other deltas mean anything. Replaying the same labelled boundaries through the production VAD and streaming adapter costs a further 2.60 points, and 7.8% of boundaries are never surfaced to the model at all. Data, weights, code and every negative result are public.

</details>


### [78] [Better Together: Complementary Query Rewriting Under a Strong RAG Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05637)
*Sara Shanian,Xiaoqin Yi,Pavlo Ruban,Kurt MacDonald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了在强检索基线下，查询改写是否能提升RAG效果。结果显示单独改写最多与强基线持平，但多种改写方法联合使用能带来显著增益（企业数据HIT@10提升+12.5~13.8），原因在于不同方法在不同问题上互补失效。预算匹配对照实验证实增益主要来自互补性而非检索预算。论文进一步提出置信度门控路由器，仅在基线top-1分数低时才执行改写，以约40%的改写成本捕获约一半的联合增益，并在下游答案质量评测中实现F1+1.92的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询改写（将用户问题改写为多个变体再检索）是提升RAG的流行手段，但已有研究未在强检索基线（稠密检索+重排序+MMR多样化）下严格检验其是否真正有效，也缺乏对多种改写策略间关系（互补或冗余）的系统性分析，以及改写成本与实际收益的权衡研究。

Method: 在固定竞争性检索流水线（BGE稠密检索+CEDR重排序+MMR多样化）下，比较四种查询改写策略（S1-S4）与两个强LLM基线（HyDE、Query2Doc），在HotpotQA、AmbigNQ和512K文档的EnterpriseRAG-Bench三个数据集上、三个随机种子下进行配对自助显著性检验。分析各方法的互补性与并集效果，通过预算匹配对照实验区分互补性增益与检索预算效应，并模拟设计置信度门控路由器——仅在基线top-1分数低时触发改写，评估其成本-收益平衡。

Result: 单独改写至多与强基线相当；方法联合显著更好（企业数据S1+S3+S4+HyDE并集较基线HIT@10提升+12.5，五方法并集达+13.8）。预算匹配对照仅捕获约40%增益，证实互补性为主要驱动。HotpotQA上并集+1.6~+1.8点(p<0.001)接近全方法oracle；AmbigNQ上融合反而降低2.4点(p<0.001)。置信度门控路由器在企业数据上以<40%查询的改写成本获得+4.3 HIT@10（约全量联合增益的一半），且在AmbigNQ自动放弃改写；下游答案质量F1提升+1.92(p<0.01)。

Conclusion: 查询改写不应被视为强基线的独立替代品，而应作为一种互补的覆盖率来源，通过成本感知路由（cost-aware routing）来应用：仅在需要时触发改写，利用不同改写方法间的互补性而非简单堆叠，以有限的推理成本实现最优收益。

Abstract: A popular way to improve Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is to rewrite the user's question into several variants and search with all of them. We test whether this actually helps once the underlying search is already strong. Under one fixed, competitive pipeline (BGE dense retrieval, cross-encoder reranking, and MMR diversification), we compare four query-rewriting strategies (S1-S4) against two strong LLM baselines (HyDE, Query2Doc) on three datasets (HotpotQA, AmbigNQ, and the 512K-document EnterpriseRAG-Bench) over three seeds with paired-bootstrap significance tests. Our headline result is that rewriting alone is at best competitive with a strong baseline, but combining methods yields outsized gains because different strategies fail on different questions. A post-hoc union of four methods (S1+S3+S4+HyDE) improves HIT@10 over the baseline by +12.5 points on enterprise data (51.70 vs 39.22), and a five-method union reaches 52.98 (+13.8). Budget-matched controls capture only ~40% of this gain, confirming that complementarity, not retrieval budget, is the primary driver. On HotpotQA the union adds +1.6 to +1.8 points (p<0.001), saturating the all-method oracle; on AmbigNQ the same fusion hurts (-2.4 below the best solo, p<0.001), and we analyze when and why. Because rewriting is expensive, we evaluate in simulation a confidence-gated router that runs rewriting only when the baseline's own top-1 score is low. It captures about half of the enterprise full-merge gain (+4.3 HIT@10) while paying rewriting cost on <40% of queries, and automatically declines to rewrite on AmbigNQ. A downstream answer-quality evaluation confirms the router improves F1 by +1.92 (p<0.01) at roughly 40% of the expansion cost. In short: treat query rewriting as a complementary coverage source, applied through cost-aware routing, not as a standalone replacement for a strong baseline.

</details>


### [79] [Who Maintains Agent Skills? A Longitudinal Study of Human-Governed, AI-Assisted Skill Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05677)
*Chen Shen,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过挖掘五个公共AI技能仓库的完整提交历史（873次提交、143个技能文件、254次实质性创建后编辑），直接研究了终身LLM代理中外部技能文物的维护过程。研究发现：（1）每次实质性编辑都由具名人类账户创作或合并，62%带有AI合著者署名，存在显著的仓库间差异；（2）这些编辑是真正的策展行为，以内容增补和修正为主；（3）预注册的规则相似性编码轴未通过可靠性检验。作者发布了语料库、编码手册和回放协议，结论是公共技能维护目前更像"人类治理、AI辅助"的循环而非自主流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能自动策展研究主要针对自动化基线进行评估，将人类维护视为未测量的瓶颈。作者旨在直接研究这一被忽视的过程，填补对实际技能维护中人类与AI角色的理解空白，为自进化代理和未来技能策展器提供基准。

Method: 作者对2025年10月至2026年6月间五个公共AI技能仓库（AI工具组织的有目的样本）的完整提交历史进行了挖掘，覆盖873次提交、143个技能文件和254次实质性创建后编辑。每次编辑使用预注册的治理（governance）、操作（operation）和触发证据（trigger-evidence）编码手册进行人工编码，并对抽样子集进行审计以验证编辑是否真正改变技能内容。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）所有实质性编辑均通过具名人类账户创作或合并，62%带有AI合著者署名，且仓库间差异显著；（2）编辑是真实策展行为，审计样本显示多数编辑改变了技能内容，编码操作以增补和修正为主；（3）预注册的规则相似性（rule-likeness）编码轴未能通过可靠性门槛，表明从提交工件中可靠编码规则相似性仍是开放测量问题。

Conclusion: 公共技能维护目前看起来不像是自主流水线，而是人类治理的、AI辅助的循环。未来自动技能策展器必须在人类治理框架内运作并以此为衡量基准。作者发布了完整语料库、编码手册、挖掘脚本和可回放协议，以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Lifelong LLM agents increasingly rely on external skill artifacts as one element for preserving and reusing capabilities over time. These skills (usually portable Markdown files such as SKILL.md) describe when and how to apply a capability and must be corrected, expanded, and consolidated as tools and usage patterns shift over deployment. Recent work seeks to automate skill curation, but it largely evaluates against automated baselines and treats human maintenance as an unmeasured bottleneck. We study that missing process directly. We mine the full commit histories of five public AI-skill repositories, a purposive sample of AI-tooling organizations, covering 873 commits, 143 skill files, and 254 substantive post-creation edits from October 2025 to June 2026. We code each edit with pre-registered governance, operation, and trigger-evidence codebooks. Three findings emerge. First, every substantive edit is authored or merged through a named human account, while 62% carry an AI co-author trailer, with large repository-level variation. Second, these edits are genuine curation: an audited sample shows that most change skill content, and the coded operations are dominated by additions and corrections. Third, a pre-registered rule-likeness axis fails its reliability gate; reliably coding rule-likeness from commit artifacts remains an open measurement problem. We release the corpus, codebooks, mining scripts, and a replay protocol for automated skill curators. For self-evolving agents, public skill maintenance currently looks less like an autonomous pipeline than a human-governed, AI-assisted loop that future curators must measure against and operate within.

</details>


### [80] [A Rubric-Guided Large Language Model Solution for Opioid Use Disorder Computable Phenotyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05682)
*Mengxian Lyu,Paredes Pardo,Cheng Peng,Ziyi Chen,Mengyuan Zhang,Jieting Li Lu,Gary M Reisfield,William M Greene,Jenny Lo-Ciganic,Yonghui Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于评分准则引导的大语言模型（LLM）框架，结合OPRO优化提示策略，用于从电子健康记录中自动识别阿片类药物使用障碍（OUD）。该框架使用18项专家设计的评分准则指导LLM提取关键临床证据以判定OUD。在253名患者（含68名OUD阳性）的验证中，该方法的F1分数达到0.774，AUROC为0.934，较机器学习方法和零样本LLM分别提高12.8%和44.4%的相对F1改进，同时具备更好的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OUD在美国是重大的公共卫生危机，但难以从电子健康记录中准确识别，因为诊断编码缺失且支持性证据深藏在临床叙述文本中。准确的OUD识别对于支持干预措施和改善健康结局至关重要。

Method: 该框架采用18项专家制定的评分准则来指导LLM自动提取关键文本和支持性证据以确定OUD标记，并结合了OPRO（Optimization by PROmpting）优化提示策略来改进LLM的提示词设计。

Result: 在253名患者（含68名OUD阳性病例）的验证中，基于LLM的可计算表型实现了最佳F1分数0.774和AUROC 0.934，相较于基于EHR和NLP提取变量的机器学习方法和零样本LLM，相对F1改进分别为12.8%和44.4%。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于LLM的可计算表型框架能将LLM提取的证据与OUD表型判定相关联，从而提供更好的可解释性，有望成为准确OUD识别的有效工具。

Abstract: Opioid use disorder (OUD) remains a public health crisis in the United States, yet it is difficult to identify from electronic health records (EHRs) because missing diagnosis codes and supporting evidence are buried in clinical narratives. Accurate OUD identification is critical to support interventions and improve health outcomes. This study developed a rubric-guided large language model (LLM) that incorporated Optimization by PROmpting (OPRO) for OUD computable phenotyping (CP). The framework used an 18-item, expert-identified rubric to instruct LLMs to automatically extract critical text with supporting evidence to determine OUD flags. Two UF Health physicians (GMR and WMG) chart-reviewed 253 patients, including 68 OUD-positive cases. Our LLM-based computable phenotype (CP) achieved the best F1 score of 0.774 and an AUROC of 0.934, outperforming the machine learning-based CP using EHR and natural language processing-extracted variables, and zero-shot LLMs by relative F1 improvements of 12.8% and 44.4%, respectively. The proposed LLM-based CP could link LLM-extracted evidence to OUD phenotyping for better explainability.

</details>


### [81] [Intra-Prompt Parallel Decoding for Common-Context Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05707)
*Theodore Glavas,Nikhita Vedula,Dushyanta Dhyani,Antonios Valkanas,Yilun Zhu,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IPPD是一种新的推理方法，在单个提示符内并行回答多个共享上下文的问答，通过虚拟位置ID和注意力掩码操作实现注意力过程中的内存与计算共享，无需微调或改变LLM架构，最多可提升7倍有效吞吐量且无质量损失，与批量推理完全兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享上下文问答任务中，现有LLM通常为每个问题使用独立提示符生成答案，尽管批处理和缓存技术能提升并行度并减少重复计算，但提示符间的问题分离限制了可实现的加速效果——现代GPU因注意力期间的内存瓶颈而利用率不足。

Method: IPPD通过将多个共享上下文的问题合并到单个提示符中并行解码，在一次推理步骤中为每个问题生成下一个token。它利用虚拟位置ID和注意力掩码操纵来产生与标准提示相同的结果，无需微调或修改LLM架构。所有并行性发生在提示符内部，因此与批量推理完全兼容，即使每个提示符具有不同的上下文。

Result: 实验表明IPPD相比标准解码可提供高达7倍的有效吞吐量，且无质量下降；在大多数设置下优于使用PagedAttention的前缀缓存方法。

Conclusion: IPPD通过在单提示符内实现多问题的并行解码并共享注意力的内存与计算，有效解决了CCQA任务中GPU内存瓶颈导致的利用率不足问题，是一种无需架构改动且兼容批处理的高效推理方案。

Abstract: In common-context question answering (CCQA) tasks, multiple input questions share a common context to base their answers from. However, Large Language Models typically generate each answer using an independent prompt. While existing batching and caching techniques help improve parallelism and reduce repeated computations, the separation of questions across prompts limits the achievable speedup, as modern GPUs are underutilized due to a memory bottleneck during attention. We present Intra-Prompt Parallel Decoding (IPPD), a novel inference method that answers multiple common-context questions in parallel within a single prompt. IPPD directly addresses the bottleneck by efficiently sharing both memory and computation during the attention process, as the next token for every question is decoded in a single inference step. IPPD uses virtual position IDs and attention mask manipulation to generate the same output as standard prompting without requiring fine-tuning or any changes to the LLM architecture. Since all parallelism occurs within a prompt, IPPD is fully compatible with batched inference, even when each prompt features a different context. Our experiments show that IPPD delivers up to 7X the effective throughput as standard decoding without quality degradation, and outperforms prefix caching with PagedAttention in most settings.

</details>


### [82] [MedWER: A Reproducible, Model-Free Evaluation Protocol for Medical Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05728)
*Justin Behling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MedWER，一个用于医疗语音识别(ASR)评估的协议与开源工具，其分母是固定的、许可证清洁的医学实体词表（19,373个药物、诊断、症状和损伤机制条目），避免传统NER模型或云API在评估时带来的版本化黑箱问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统整体词错误率(WER)会掩盖临床上关键的识别错误，例如转录可能95%正确但把一种药物错换为另一种。现有修复方法通常依赖评估时的NER模型或云API加权实体错误，导致度量的分母成为版本化的黑箱，缺乏透明性与可复现性。

Method: MedWER协议包含：(1) 固定且许可证清洁的词表作为分母，涵盖19,373个来自公开源的药物、诊断、症状及损伤机制条目；(2) 固定版本(text-normalizer)与短语感知的术语受限WER；(3) 归一化器固定在精确版本并通过golden fixtures校验；(4) 用独立的省级药物福利文件验证词表覆盖率，并用ground-truth实体跨度校准匹配启发式；(5) 采用基于重采样句级分数的95%置信区间报告结果。

Result: 对Moonshine base、Whisper base.en和MedASR在两个公开基准上使用发布工具评分，并以逐句重采样的95%置信区间报告基线结果；词表覆盖率通过与构建无关的独立省级药物福利文件交叉验证。

Conclusion: MedWER提供了一种可复现、透明且临床敏感的医疗ASR评估方案，消除了对版本化NER黑箱的依赖，使医学关键错误不再被整体WER掩盖，同时保证可复现的度量标准。

Abstract: Overall word error rate hides clinically critical errors: a transcript can be 95% correct and still swap one drug for another. The usual fix weights errors on medical entities, and almost always depends on an evaluation-time named-entity recognition (NER) model or cloud API, which makes the metric's denominator a versioned black box. We present MedWER, an evaluation protocol and open-source tool for medical ASR whose denominator is a fixed, license-clean term list: 19,373 drug, diagnosis, symptom, and injury-mechanism entries projected from public sources. The protocol couples a pinned text normalizer with a phrase-aware term-restricted WER, the MedWER, so the only versioned component is a normalizer dependency held at an exact release and checked against committed golden fixtures. Coverage is validated against an independent provincial drug-benefit file the list was not built from; the matching heuristic is calibrated against ground-truth entity spans. Baselines for Moonshine~base, Whisper~base.en, and MedASR on two open benchmarks are scored with the released tool and reported with 95% confidence intervals from resampled per-utterance scores.

</details>


### [83] [Some Tokens Behave like Magnets: Revealing Linguistic Organization in the Layers of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05743)
*Andrew Liu,Devan Srinivasan,Gerald Penn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型(LLM)内部存在一类被称为"磁向量"的特殊token向量，它们通过吸引或排斥两种对立的极性来组织周围token的几何排布。研究发现功能词在浅层一致表现为排斥磁体，且这种模式在不同架构、规模和层配置中稳定存在。移除磁体会产生因果性影响：去除浅层排斥磁体会严重破坏句法任务（POS标注从91%降至10%以下）而不影响语义任务，去除深层吸引磁体则产生相反效果。该发现提供了首个无需探针即可理解语言模型跨层几何组织语言计算的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LLM内部如何处理和组织语言是一项核心挑战。以往研究通常依赖探针(probing)方法来推断模型内部的表示结构，而这些方法存在局限性。作者希望找到一种无需探针、可直接从模型几何结构中理解语言计算组织方式的路径，并揭示LLM中是否存在系统性的、有目的性的token向量组织机制。

Method: 作者从token向量的几何属性出发，识别出一类特殊的"磁向量"，通过"吸引"或"排斥"两种极性来组织周围token——与吸引磁体方向一致的token被拉长，与排斥磁体方向一致的token被压缩。研究中进行了多方面的分析：(1)在浅层中统计检验语言类别与磁体极性的关系，发现功能词一致表现为排斥磁体；(2)在深层观察磁体极性如何重组；(3)通过微调下游任务观察任务功能性token如何涌现为磁体；(4)通过移除特定层中的磁体进行因果干预，测试句法和语义任务的表现变化。实验覆盖不同架构、规模和层配置的LLM以确保结论的普适性。

Result: 研究发现功能词在浅层一致充当排斥磁体，深层中磁体以独特方式重组极性，且这一模式在不同LLM架构、规模和层配置中一致存在。因果实验表明：在问答任务中，答案跨度token在最后一层成为独特的排斥磁体，几何性地将答案从上下文中"雕刻"出来。去除浅层排斥磁体使POS标注准确率从91%骤降至10%以下（破坏句法任务但对语义任务几乎无影响），而去除深层吸引磁体则相反——破坏语义任务而保留句法任务。

Conclusion: 磁向量现象揭示了LLM中存在一种精确的、逐层的语言计算几何组织方式：浅层排斥磁体支撑句法计算，深层吸引磁体支撑语义计算。这一发现为理解语言模型如何跨层几何组织语言计算提供了首个无需探针的分析路径，值得进一步深入研究。

Abstract: We identify a special group of token vectors inside large language models (LLMs), which we term magnetic vectors, that organize the surrounding tokens by either attracting or repelling them. Particularly, tokens pointing the same way as an attracting magnet are elongated; tokens pointing the same way as a repelling magnet are compressed. Just as physical magnets pull or push away the iron filings around them, these vectors organize their surroundings through two opposing polarities. Moreover, we identify a statistically significant pattern in linguistic category where function words consistently act as repelling magnets in early layers, and we also find magnets consistently reorganize their polarities in unique ways deeper in the model. In a further case study we find this observation may unveil a deliberate, layer-wise organization in how LLMs process language.
  This pattern is consistent across different LLM architectures, sizes, and layer configurations. It is also causally relevant. When the LLM is fine-tuned for a downstream task, the task-functional tokens emerge as magnets. E.g., in question answering, the answer-span tokens become uniquely repelling magnets in the final layer, geometrically carving the answer out of the surrounding context. Furthermore, removing early-layer repelling magnets devastates syntactic tasks (POS tagging accuracy drops from 91% to below 10%) while sparing semantic ones, and removing late-layer attracting magnets does the reverse. We believe this phenomenon warrants further investigation, as it opens the first probe-free path to understanding how language models geometrically organize linguistic computation across their layers.

</details>


### [84] [CrisisKD: Five-Stage Knowledge Distillation for Aspect-Level Sentiment and Emotion Analysis in Crisis Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05757)
*Marko Haralović,Onat Akca,Salih Eren Yücetürk,Minsi Li,Mariët Theune*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CrisisKD是一个五阶段的师生知识蒸馏框架，用于在未标注的社交媒体数据上对健康危机事件进行方面级情感与情绪分析。教师大语言模型生成方面级标签与推理轨迹，指导学生小模型完成方面提取、句法解析、观点提取、情感分类和情绪分类五个子任务。基于该框架构建并发布了包含50,615个方面级标签的数据集及开源资源。在500条人工标注金标准上，经过5任务调优的Qwen2.5-7B学生模型相比未调优模型在方面提取F1提升7.9分、情绪准确率提升17.0分、情感准确率提升6.5分；在外部ABEA基准上，相比同模型ICL基线，ATE提升2.8 F1分、联合ATE+AEC提升3.8 F1分，同时推理成本显著低于教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危机情境（尤其是健康相关危机）中识别情感词或短语的目标对象，对于理解不同文化与语言背景下公众的关注点至关重要。然而该任务在未标注的社交媒体数据上缺乏有效的自动化解决方案，需要一种既能获得高质量标注又能控制推理成本的方法。

Method: 提出CrisisKD五阶段师生知识蒸馏框架：步骤1由教师大语言模型基于未标注社交媒体数据生成方面级标签和推理痕迹；步骤2教师对推理痕迹进行句法解析；步骤3以解析句法与推理痕迹指导方面提取（ATE）；步骤4提取观点词或短语；步骤5进行方面级情感分类（AEC）与情绪分类。所得标签与推理监督用于微调较小的学生模型（Qwen2.5-7B），实现端到端的ABSA与情绪检测。

Result: 构建并发布了含50,615个方面级标签的数据集及注释与微调脚本。在500条人工标注金标准集上，调优后的Qwen2.5-7B学生比未调优模型在方面提取上提升7.9 F1点、情绪准确率提升17.0点、情感准确率提升6.5点。在外部ABEA基准上，比同模型Qwen2.5-7B的ICL基线在ATE上提升2.8 F1点、联合ATE+AEC上提升3.8 F1点。学生模型推理成本显著低于教师模型。

Conclusion: CrisisKD证明了通过知识蒸馏，用大语言模型标注能力训练小型学生模型，可以在多种ABSA子任务上取得显著效果提升，同时大幅降低推理成本，展示了在危机相关社交媒体分析中进行低成本、可扩展的方面级情感与情绪分析的有效路径。

Abstract: Identifying the target of emotional words or phrases in crisis situations, especially health-related ones, is important for understanding public concerns across cultural and linguistic contexts. We propose CrisisKD, a five-stage teacher--student knowledge distillation framework for aspect-level sentiment and emotion analysis on unannotated social media data. A teacher LLM generates aspect-level labels and reasoning traces that supervise a smaller student model across aspect extraction, syntactic parsing, opinion extraction, sentiment classification, and emotion classification. Using this framework, we construct and release a dataset containing 50,615 aspect-level labels, together with the annotation and fine-tuning scripts as open-source resources. The resulting student supports end-to-end ABSA and emotion detection at substantially lower inference cost than the teacher. On a manually annotated 500-tweet gold set, the 5-task Qwen2.5-7B student improves over the untuned model by 7.9 F1 points on aspect extraction, 17.0 points on emotion accuracy, and 6.5 points on sentiment accuracy. On the external ABEA benchmark, CrisisKD improves the same-model Qwen2.5-7B ICL baseline by 2.8 F1 points on ATE and 3.8 F1 points on joint ATE+AEC.

</details>


### [85] [Recall Is Not Protection: Evaluating Safety Monitors Against Model Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05797)
*Sripad Karne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究发现，安全监控器在不同提示词上的召回率存在显著偏差：对于模型实际会应答（elicitable）的有害提示，监控器的召回率比非elicitable提示低0.22至0.38。监控器漏掉的提示被模型遵从的可能性是捕获提示的2.8到5.6倍，表明标准召回率指标可能高估了监控器在实际中的保护效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全监控器仅以对有害性标签的召回率作为评估标准，但捕获提示并不等同于防止危害——只有当模型原本会遵从该提示时，捕获才有实际意义。因此需要直接衡量监控器在模型"会应答"与"不会应答"提示上的差异表现。

Method: 定义"可诱发性"（elicitable）：对同一有害提示从目标模型重复采样多次响应，若至少有一次模型遵从，则该提示被标记为elicitable。然后在固定的误报率下，分别报告监控器在elicitable和非elicitable提示上的召回率。实验覆盖六种监控器配置、三种模型家族（包括激活探针、微调文本守卫和120B策略条件推理分类器）。

Result: 监控器在elicitable提示上的召回率比非elicitable提示低0.22至0.38；漏掉的提示被遵从概率是捕获提示的2.8至5.6倍。该差距在三种模型家族中均复现，且在与目标模型完全独立的纯文本监控器中同样存在。

Conclusion: 标准召回率评估可能高估了安全监控器在实际部署中的保护能力。监控器应基于其对应模型实际会应答的内容进行评估，而非仅依据静态有害性标签。

Abstract: Safety monitors screen prompts sent to deployed language models, flagging harmful requests so they are never answered. They are evaluated by recall against harmfulness labels, but a catch only prevents harm if the model would otherwise have complied. We measure the difference directly: we sample repeated responses from the target model, call a harmful prompt \emph{elicitable} if the model complies at least once, and report monitor recall separately on elicitable and non-elicitable prompts. Across six monitor configurations and three model families, spanning activation probes, fine-tuned text guards, and a 120B policy-conditioned reasoning classifier, recall on elicitable prompts falls 0.22 to 0.38 below recall on non-elicitable prompts at a fixed false positive rate. The prompts a monitor misses are 2.8 to 5.6 times more likely to be complied with than the prompts it catches. The gap replicates across three model families and appears also in text-only monitors entirely independent of the target model. This suggests that standard recall may overstate the protection monitors provide in practice, and that monitors should be evaluated against what their models will actually answer.

</details>


### [86] [Dynamic Lagging for Simultaneous Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05799)
*Hieu Hoang,Amittai Axelrod*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于前缀感知的句子级训练解码器LLM方法，用于级联同声传译。通过fine-tuning使LLM了解稳定前缀，并利用单次force-decode回合在源文本不断到达时推进已提交的目标翻译，从而构建无闪烁的同声传译系统。在Qwen3-8B上进行EN→DE/JA/ZH实验，验证了该方法保持全句质量、提升最差位置块质量、改进token级提交置信度校准，并以无需训练的置信度阈值在质量-延迟权衡上优于wait-k和目标后缀删除机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在级联同时语音翻译中，机器翻译系统无法控制上游识别器的读写调度：它需要从不断增长的源端前缀开始，决定提交多少目标文本。现有方法缺乏一种能让系统天然无闪烁（flicker-free）且能控制质量-延迟权衡的前缀感知机制。

Method: 1) 将句子级、仅解码器LLM通过与稳定前缀混合全句配对进行前缀感知微调（稳定前缀是模型自身全句输出中与当前部分源翻译共享的最长前缀）；2) 通过单次force-decode回合进行提示，随着更多源文本到达推进已提交的目标翻译，使系统构造性地实现无闪烁；3) 使用无需训练的置信度阈值作为延迟控制机制，与wait-k和目标后缀删除对比。

Result: 前缀微调在保持全句质量的同时改善了最差位置块质量，并提高了token级提交置信度的校准（在早期源前缀上与稳定前缀oracle相比降低了期望校准误差ECE）。训练无关的置信度阈值是三种延迟控制中最有效的：其连续质量-延迟曲线优于离散wait-k和目标后缀删除机制。效果在FLEURS、WMT24++和CoVoST 2测试集上、在COMET和MetricX指标下均稳定成立。

Conclusion: 该方法证明通过前缀感知微调和单次force-decode范式，可以将句子级LLM有效应用于同声翻译，在保证全句翻译质量的同时显著改善流式场景下的块质量、置信度校准以及质量-延迟前沿，且具有跨数据集和指标的泛化鲁棒性。

Abstract: In cascaded simultaneous speech translation, the machine translation (MT) system cannot control the read--write schedule of the upstream recognizer: it must decide, from a growing source prefix, how much target text to commit. We make a sentence-trained, decoder-only LLM prefix-aware by fine-tuning it on stable prefixes---the longest prefix that any translation up to the current partial source has shared with the model's own full-source output---mixed with full-sentence pairs, and prompt it through a single force-decode turn that carries the committed target forward as more source arrives, making the system flicker-free by construction. We fine-tune Qwen3-8B for EN to DE, JA, ZH, simulating the source stream with reference-transcript prefixes. Prefix finetuning preserves full-sentence quality while improving worst-position chunk quality, and it improves calibration of token-level commit confidence, reducing expected calibration error (ECE) on early source prefixes against a stable-prefix oracle. A single training-free threshold on that confidence is the most effective of the three latency controls we compare: it traces a continuous quality--latency frontier that outperforms the discrete wait-$k$ and target-suffix-deletion quality-latency tradeoff mechanisms. The effect holds well on FLEURS, WMT24++, and CoVoST~2 test sets, under both COMET and MetricX.

</details>


### [87] [AtomCite: Verification and Correction of Supplied Page-Level Citations in Multi-Page Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05802)
*Chen Qian,Yimeng Wang,Yu Chen,Lingfei Wu,Andreas Stathopoulos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AtomCite，一个用于验证和纠正大语言模型在多页文档问答中页面级引用的智能体框架，并配套DocCite基准数据集（基于MP-DocVQA和DUDE构建，含928个注入实例和1,909个人工确认的真实错误）。AtomCite将答案解析为声明，逐条对照被引用页面的图像进行检查，并采用确定性修复策略，在三个模型家族上达到约93%的二分类验证准确率，将引用精度从34%提升至87-90%，同时保留90%以上的正确声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅在生成时或针对文本段落对引用评分，缺乏对"验证和纠正已附着在答案上的页面级引用"这一能力的系统评估；同时自动或合成标签的噪声可能会使评估结果产生偏差甚至颠倒系统排名，因此需要可靠的验证框架和基准。

Method: AtomCite框架：将答案解析为claims，将每个claim与所引用页面的图像进行核对，使用确定性修复策略进行纠正。DocCite基准：基于MP-DocVQA和DUDE构建，包含928个已验证的注入实例和2,468个从模型输出中采集的候选自然错误（经双标注者审核确认1,909个为真实错误）；主要标签使用确定性方式而非LLM判定器分配，人工审核作为独立验证层。

Result: 在Gemini、Claude和GPT三个模型家族上，AtomCite在注入基准上达到约93%的二分类验证准确率，显著优于所有仅OCR条件（包括计算量匹配的对照组）及基于相同OCR文本的先前文本基线。修复策略将注入混合数据中的引用精度从构建的34%提升至87-90%并保留90%以上正确claims。以冻结提示和零训练迁移时，可将两个开放的7-8B模型在五个公开基准上的幻觉检测分数提升至高于同一模型直接作为判定器的水平。审核显示自动标签噪声会偏差化测量的验证准确率并可能反转系统排名。

Conclusion: AtomCite验证并纠正页面级引用的能力显著优于现有基线和OCR-only方法；仅依赖合成或自动标签的评估存在衡量验证能力的风险，应使用经人工审核的真实错误标签进行可靠评估。

Abstract: Large language models answering questions over multi-page documents are expected to cite the supporting pages, yet supplied citations are sometimes inaccurate, and current evaluations score citations at generation time or against text passages: no existing benchmark evaluates whether a system can verify and correct a page-level citation already attached to an answer. We propose AtomCite, an agentic framework that parses an answer into claims, checks each claim against the image of its cited page, and applies a deterministic repair policy. To evaluate it, we introduce DocCite, to our knowledge the first benchmark for systems that verify and correct page-level citations in document images. Built on MP-DocVQA and DUDE, it combines 928 validated injected instances with 2,468 candidate natural errors harvested from frontier- and efficiency-tier models, of which a two-annotator audit confirms 1,909 as genuine errors. Primary labels are assigned deterministically, not by LLM judges, with the human audit as a separate validation layer. Across three model families (Gemini, Claude, and GPT), AtomCite reaches around 93% binary verification accuracy on the injected benchmark, significantly outperforming every OCR-only condition, including a compute-matched control, and exceeding every prior text-based baseline given the same OCR text. Its repair policy lifts citation precision on the injected mix from a constructed 34% to 87-90% while retaining over 90% of correct claims. AtomCite also transfers: with frozen prompts and zero training, it raises the hallucination-detection scores of two open 7-8B models on five public benchmarks above the same models prompted as direct judges. Finally, the audit shows that noise in automatic labels biases measured verifier accuracy and can reverse system rankings, so evaluations relying only on synthetic or automatic labels risk mismeasuring verification capability.

</details>


### [88] [CONDUIT: A Unified Residual-Stream Restoration Framework for KV Cache Reuse in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05821)
*Pengan Chen,Kaisheng Zheng,Liang Hong,Lixia Yi,Jiyue Jiang,Jiayang Chen,Yixuan Wang,Yimin Fan,Xinyuan Liu,Jiayi Li,Zhanqiu Zhang,Yiwen Guo,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CONDUIT是一个无需训练的KV缓存刷新策略，将单图像和多图像复用统一为残差流恢复，通过范数加权注意力、图像级相关性放大和全局选择，在10%刷新预算下达到全预填充97.0-99.5%的平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在处理重复出现的视觉内容时，精确前缀复用会因前缀变化而失效；选择性重计算虽然能在小视觉token预算下恢复质量，但原始的注意力选择方法会浪费预算——高注意力但价值范数低的token，以及对查询无关的图像的token上。现有方法存在这些失效模式，需要更好的刷新策略。

Method: CONDUIT将单图像和多图像KV缓存复用统一为残差流恢复问题。基于范数加权注意力，使用缓存的键-查询注意力来排序缓存视觉token，并用预输出的缓存值范数代理（pre-output cached-value-norm proxy）作为辅助，之后进行图像级别相关性放大，最后进行全局选择。单图像场景下系数为1，退化为图像内token选择。该方法保持模型架构和权重不变，仅在推理时增加一次查询条件化的评分。

Result: 在10%刷新预算下，CONDUIT在三个VLM骨干网络上达到了对应全预填充五数据集平均性能的97.0-99.5%，并在所有预算受限方法中领先。在MMLongBench-Doc延迟子集上，仅使用全预填充13.5%的FLOPs，实现了2.99倍的首次token生成时间（TTFT）加速。

Conclusion: CONDUIT通过统一单/多图像场景下的残差流恢复视角，结合范数加权注意力和图像级放大，有效解决了原始注意力选择的两个失效模式，在极低的刷新预算下保持高质量，同时显著降低计算开销和推理延迟。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often answer new questions about recurring visual content, where reusing the key-value (KV) cache can avoid re-encoding expensive visual prefixes. Exact-prefix reuse, however, fails when the same visual content appears under a changed prefix. Selective recomputation can recover quality under a small visual-token budget, but only when the right stale tokens are refreshed. Raw-attention selection can waste budget on high-attention tokens with small value-norm proxy scores and on query-irrelevant images. To address these failure modes, we propose CONDUIT, a training-free refresh policy that unifies single- and multi-image reuse as residual-stream restoration. Building on norm-weighted attention, CONDUIT ranks cached visual tokens using cached-key query attention and an accessible pre-output cached-value-norm proxy, then applies empirical image-level relevance amplification before one global selection. With one image, the coefficient is one and the rule reduces to intra-image token selection. The method preserves model architecture and weights, adding only a single query-conditioned scoring pass at inference. At a 10% refresh budget, CONDUIT achieves 97.0-99.5% of the corresponding full-prefill five-dataset average across three VLM backbones and leads budgeted methods on average; on the MMLongBench-Doc latency subset, it uses 13.5% of full-prefill FLOPs and achieves a 2.99x time-to-first-token speedup.

</details>


### [89] [SinoGlyphBench: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Chinese Glyph-Level Obfuscation in Language-Model Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05843)
*Yifan Wang,Zimu Wang,Suliu Qin,Changyu Zeng,Tong Chen,Siqi Chen,Yijie Lin,Lingyu Jiang,Jionglong Su,Yushan Pan,Haiyang Zhang,Wei Wang,Qiaoyu Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SinoGlyphBench诊断基准，用于评测LLM和MLLM在中文字形混淆（glyph-level obfuscation）内容下的安全审核能力，通过锚点、背景及全范围扰动设计，发现模型对非规范字形中文内容仍然脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字形级混淆可使有害中文内容对人类可读，但能削弱自动化审核系统的识别能力。现有评测缺乏对混淆如何影响审核证据的细粒度诊断，需要一种能够区分"破坏审核关键证据"与"一般表面变化"的基准设计。

Method: 构建SinoGlyphBench诊断基准：识别标签关键语义锚点（label-critical semantic anchors），生成匹配的原始与字形混淆输入（文本和图像双模态）。通过分别扰动锚点、背景或二者兼顾，区分审核相关证据的破坏与一般表面变异。基于该设计对12个LLM和MLLM进行176,916对配对评估。

Result: 混淆使有害内容的假阴性率上升6.1个百分点、假阳性率上升4.7个百分点，四分类准确率下降5.0个百分点；模型保留了75.7%原本正确的判断。全范围扰动导致最大性能退化，仅锚点扰动比仅背景扰动更具破坏性，跨脚本替换在文本模态下尤其困难。结构化输出显示模型在可见字形读取、意图消息恢复和最终安全判断上存在可观察的不一致。

Conclusion: 现有模型对以非规范字形书写的中文有害内容仍然表现脆弱，在安全审核场景下存在明显性能短板。资源已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Glyph-level obfuscation can leave harmful Chinese content readable to humans while degrading automated moderation. We introduce SinoGlyphBench, a diagnostic benchmark that identifies label-critical semantic anchors and creates matched original and glyph-obfuscated inputs in text and image modalities. By perturbing anchors, background context, or both, this design distinguishes corruption of moderation-relevant evidence from general surface variation. Across 176,916 paired evaluations of 12 LLMs and MLLMs, obfuscation increases harmful false-negative and false-positive rates by 6.1 and 4.7 percentage points, respectively, and reduces four-way accuracy by 5.0 points. Models retain 75.7% of the decisions that were correct on the matched original inputs. Full-scope perturbations cause the largest degradation, anchor-only perturbations are more damaging than background-only perturbations, and cross-script substitution is particularly difficult in the text modality. Analysis of structured outputs identifies observable mismatches in visible-form reading, intended-message recovery, and final safety judgment. The evaluated models, therefore, remain brittle to Chinese content written with non-canonical glyphs. Resources are available at https://github.com/fengshun124/SinoGlyphBench.

</details>


### [90] [What if LLMs Ate Their Words: Causal History Effects in Multi-Turn Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05882)
*Jinnan Li,Zheren Fu,Yue Wang,Jinzhe Li,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多轮交互中LLM先前响应（助手生成的历史）对后续行为的影响。在6个任务族、5个模型上，将完整单轮输入（FULL）与逐步揭示的多轮交互（SHARDED）对比，发现退化程度依赖任务和模型。通过对已完成对话进行“中和化”（将历史助手回复替换为中性内容）与“Turn Surgery”（逐轮干预），发现历史编辑确有积极但选择性的影响：替代历史可改变下游表现（+0.027），大多数干预位置无变化但部分轨迹存在获益干预，且开放权重案例分析表明行为层面的历史影响对应可测量的内部状态差异但呈任务依赖而非通用特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已表明LLM存在多轮退化现象且助手生成的历史会影响后续行为，但尚不清楚这些效应如何在不同模型、任务、轮次以及模型内部具体体现。作者希望填补这一空白，为多轮对话系统的历史管理提供依据。

Method: 在6个任务族和5个模型上进行FULL（单轮完整输入）与SHARDED（逐步揭示的多轮交互）对比实验；对完成的SHARDED对话进行回顾性分析：①中和化（neutralization）：将已观察到的助手回复全部替换为中性内容，观察下游表现变化，含长度匹配对照组；②Turn Surgery：逐轮干预单个助手回复，评估各位置干预效果；③开放权重模型案例研究：将行为层面的历史编辑效果关联到模型内部隐藏状态的可测量差异。

Result: 任务和模型对FULL到SHARDED的退化有显著影响，单轮表现更强不代表多轮交互鲁棒性更强。中和化在2,973条轨迹上使下游性能改变+0.027；在预设长度受限子集中，短中和与长度匹配中和效果几乎相同（+0.069 vs +0.068），说明简单的上下文缩短不足以解释历史编辑效应。237条退化轨迹中63.7%存在至少一个获益干预位置，但大多数位置保持不变；二分类任务中48.4%可实现失败到成功的反转。开放权重案例显示行为后果性的历史变化对应可测的下游状态差异，但呈任务依赖而非通用内部特征。

Conclusion: 助手生成的历史对多轮交互性能具有活跃但选择性的影响。历史管理不应采用统一的“全部保留”或“全部清除”策略，而应基于任务与轨迹特征进行选择性管理，以提升多轮LLM交互的鲁棒性与效率。

Abstract: Multi-turn interaction creates a feedback process in which an LLM's previous responses become context for later behavior. Prior work shows substantial multi-turn degradation and that assistant-generated history can affect later behavior. However, it remains unclear how these effects manifest across models, tasks, turns, and inside a model. We study these gaps across six task families and five models. Degradation from fully specified single-turn input (FULL) to progressively revealed multi-turn interaction (SHARDED) is clearly task- and model-dependent, and stronger one-shot performance does not imply greater interaction robustness. We then retrospectively analyze completed SHARDED conversations by replaying the user messages already observed in each trajectory while editing only assistant-generated history. Replacing prior assistant responses with neutral content (termed neutralization) changes downstream min-max normalized performance by +.027 across 2,973 trajectories. On a prespecified length-controlled subset, short and length-matched neutralization yield nearly identical effects (+.069 versus +.068), showing that simple context shortening is insufficient to explain the effect of history editing. Turn Surgery further intervenes on one assistant turn at a time. Among 237 selected degraded trajectories, 63.7% contain at least one beneficial intervention, while most tested positions remain unchanged; for binary tasks, 48.4% admit a fail-to-success reversal. An open-weight case study links behaviorally consequential history changes to measurable downstream state differences, but finds task-dependent rather than universal internal signatures. Overall, assistant-generated history has active but selective effects on multi-turn performance, motivating selective rather than uniform history management.

</details>


### [91] [AlignDiff: Exploiting Model-Intrinsic Information for Better Preference Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05899)
*Peng Lai,He Zhu,Zhiwen Ruan,Dongdong Zhang,Yun Chen,Peng Li,Furu Wei,Yang Liu,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AlignDiff是一个由模型内在信号驱动的偏好数据过滤框架，用于提升大语言模型与人类偏好的对齐效果。它通过正/反信号筛选清晰偏好的样本，再根据平均负对数似然差距优先学习更具挑战性的样本。该方法在LLaMA和Qwen模型族以及AlpacaEval 2.0、Arena-Hard和MT-Bench三个基准上，始终优于七个强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型与人类偏好的对齐效果高度依赖于偏好数据的质量，而现有数据集常受内在噪声和分布偏移影响从而限制模型性能。作者旨在解决偏好数据质量不佳这一瓶颈问题。

Method: AlignDiff采用两阶段策略：首先利用正信号和逆信号识别具有清晰偏好的样本；随后基于平均负对数似然（NLL）差距对更具挑战性的样本进行优先级排序，鼓励模型从中学习更丰富的信息。该方法本质上是难度驱动的课程学习式的数据过滤框架。

Result: 在LLaMA和Qwen两个模型族及AlpacaEval 2.0、Arena-Hard、MT-Bench三个基准上全面评估，所有设置下均一致优于七个强基线；消融研究验证了各组件有效性，并进一步证明基于难度的课程学习能提升模型性能。

Conclusion: AlignDiff作为偏好数据过滤框架，通过模型内在信号有效筛选高质量偏好样本，并引入难度优先级排序提升对齐效果，验证了基于难度的课程学习在偏好对齐中的数据筛选价值。

Abstract: Aligning large language models with human preferences remains a challenge, primarily due to the critical role of preference data quality in effective alignment. Existing datasets are frequently plagued by inherent noise and distribution shifts, which inherently limit model performance. To bridge this gap, we propose AlignDiff, a preference data filtering framework driven by intrinsic model signals. AlignDiff first identifies samples with clear preferences using both positive and inverse signals, then prioritizes the more challenging samples based on the average negative log-likelihood gap, encouraging the model to learn richer information from them. AlignDiff is evaluated on two widely used model families (LLaMA and Qwen) and three benchmarks widely adopted in the alignment community (AlpacaEval 2.0, Arena-Hard, and MT-Bench). Across all settings, it consistently outperforms seven strong baselines. We conduct comprehensive ablation studies to validate the effectiveness of AlignDiff, and further show that difficulty-based curriculum learning improves model performance.

</details>


### [92] [From Narrative to Auditable Forecasts: A Structured Scaffold for Agentic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05905)
*Yuanpu Cao,Yongkang Du,Yurui Chang,Lu Lin,Jinghui Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AuditForecast，一种结构化概率预测的智能体脚手架，通过定量基线模型锚定、模型引导的数据检索以及赔率空间中的机械聚合完成显式的预测构建，在多个实时预测基准上超越强基线并获得可审计的预测报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体预测依赖隐式叙事聚合：智能体收集证据、用散文讨论，常直接给出概率而未提供从证据到预测的显式更新路径，既限制了预测准确性也限制了可审计性。

Method: AuditForecast分三步：首先用合适的定量基线模型锚定预测；其次通过模型引导的数据检索推导基础概率；最后对模型范围之外的情境因素在赔率空间中通过机械聚合进行更新，将预测从散文式判断转变为具有显式中间对象的流程化过程。

Result: 在多个实时预测基准上，AuditForecast相比强智能体基线提升了预测准确性和校准度，在若干场景超越市场隐含参考，并以低于深度研究型智能体的成本实现更优性能，在成本-准确率权衡中保持帕累托主导地位。此外其生成可审计的预测报告，支持系统性的事后分析。

Conclusion: AuditForecast表明将预测过程结构化、显式化——以基线模型锚定并结合赔率空间机械聚合——能够同时改善预测性能与可审计性，为智能体预测提供了超越散文式推理的方向。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly used for live forecasting, where they retrieve up-to-date information and produce estimates for unresolved future events. However, current agentic forecasting often relies on implicit narrative aggregation: agents collect evidence, discuss it in prose, and often assign a probability without an explicit update path from evidence to forecast. This limits both forecasting accuracy and auditability. We propose AuditForecast, an agentic scaffold for structured probabilistic forecasting. AuditForecast first anchors the forecast with a suitable quantitative baseline model, uses model-guided data retrieval to derive a base probability, and then applies situational factor updates outside the model's scope through mechanical aggregation in odds space. This turns forecasting from a prose-based judgment into a structured process with explicit intermediate objects. Across multiple live forecasting benchmarks, AuditForecast improves forecasting accuracy and calibration relative to strong agentic baselines, surpasses market-implied references in several settings, and outperforms substantially more expensive deep-research agents while remaining Pareto-dominant in the cost--accuracy tradeoff. Beyond performance gains, AuditForecast produces an auditable forecasting report that makes forecast construction explicit and supports systematic post hoc analysis.

</details>


### [93] [UniRRM: Unified Reasoning Reward Models Across Languages and Evaluation Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05910)
*Peng Lai,Yichao Du,Junchao Wu,Weibo Gao,Linan Yue,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo,Derek F. Wong,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MixReward大规模多语言数据集（覆盖6个领域、103种语言，含pairwise和listwise数据）及UniRRM统一推理奖励模型，通过分段推理链动态生成任务通用和指令特定标准，实现细粒度、输入自适应的跨语言判断。实验表明UniRRM-8B和UniRRM-14B在多个基准上接近同规模SOTA，并对未见评估范式有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在具有可验证奖励的任务上表现优异，但在开放式任务中奖励模型的可靠性是关键挑战。现有方案依赖昂贵专有的LLM-as-a-Judge系统或缺乏可解释性的不透明标量奖励模型；近期生成式奖励模型虽具潜力，但仍受限于静态评估标准、碎片化评估范式及有限的多语言支持。

Method: 构建MixReward大规模多语言数据集（覆盖6个领域、103种语言，含pairwise和listwise数据）；提出UniRRM统一推理奖励模型，支持多种语言和评估范式，采用分段推理链动态生成任务通用及指令特定的评估标准，实现细粒度、输入自适应的判断并保持跨语言一致性。

Result: UniRRM-8B和UniRRM-14B在多个基准上达到接近同规模最先进模型的性能，对未见评估范式有效；消融实验验证了模型的可信性和有效性。

Conclusion: UniRRM通过统一推理奖励模型在可解释性、多语言支持和范式泛化方面提供了可行的解决方案，兼具接近SOTA的性能与跨范式适用性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) excels on tasks with verifiable rewards, but in open-ended tasks, the reliability of reward models remains a key challenge. Existing solutions either depend on costly proprietary LLM-as-a-Judge systems or opaque scalar reward models that lack interpretability. Recent works on generative reward models offer a promising alternative, but they remain constrained by static evaluation criteria, fragmented evaluation paradigms, and limited multilingual support. To address these challenges, we introduce \textbf{MixReward}, a large-scale multilingual dataset spanning six domains and 103 languages, containing both pairwise and listwise data, and propose \textbf{UniRRM}, a unified reasoning reward model supporting multiple languages and evaluation paradigms. UniRRM uses a staged reasoning chain to dynamically generate task-generic and instruction-specific criteria, enabling fine-grained, input-adaptive judgments while maintaining consistency across languages. Experiments demonstrate that UniRRM-8B and UniRRM-14B achieve performance close to the state-of-the-art for models of comparable size across multiple benchmarks, and are effective for unseen evaluation paradigms. In addition, ablation studies validate the reliability and effectiveness of UniRRM.

</details>


### [94] [Neuron-Guided Fine-Tuning: Unlocking Efficient Alignment Mechanisms for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05913)
*Zeyu Wu,Junchao Wu,Shudong Liu,Runzhe Zhan,Xin Chen,Shu Yang,Yichao Du,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo,Jinsong Su,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出神经元引导微调（NGFT）框架，利用神经元激活模式作为统一优化信号，通过自适应任务特定神经元选择、基于激活的数据选择以及神经元激活对齐损失函数三个协同机制，统一解决全参数微调中的参数冗余、数据质量不一致和灾难性遗忘问题，在三个模型的多领域基准上优于主流微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督微调范式（尤其是全参数微调）存在参数冗余、数据质量不一致和灾难性遗忘三大问题，但现有方法通常孤立地解决这些问题，缺乏统一的优化信号来桥接数据选择、参数更新和知识保留三个方面。

Method: 提出NGFT框架，以神经元激活模式作为通用代理统一微调生命周期。包含三个协同机制：(1)自适应任务特定神经元选择——通过单次前向传播识别关键神经元，集中参数更新并降低冗余；(2)基于激活的数据选择——优先选取对关键神经元贡献最大的信息密集样本；(3)神经元激活对齐——设计新型损失函数，将激活锚定到预训练状态以加深表征学习并保留通用知识。

Result: 在三个模型上，跨领域专用基准和通用基准的实验结果显示，NGFT在效率和性能方面均显著优于现有主流微调方法，同时有效缓解了灾难性遗忘问题。

Conclusion: NGFT通过统一的神经元激活信号将数据选择、参数更新与知识保留三者有机整合在一个框架中，证明了这种整体性方法相比孤立处理各问题更为有效，为微调范式提供了新的优化视角。

Abstract: Existing Supervised Fine-Tuning paradigms, particularly Full Parameter Fine-Tuning are often plagued by parameter redundancy, inconsistent data quality, and catastrophic forgetting, which current methods typically address in isolation and lack a unified optimization signal to bridge data selection, parameter updates, and knowledge preservation. To address this, we propose Neuron-Guided Fine-Tuning (NGFT), a holistic framework that leverages neuron activation patterns as a universal proxy to unify the fine-tuning lifecycle. NGFT operates via three synergistic mechanisms: (1) Adaptive Task-Specific Neuron Selection, which identifies essential neurons in a single forward pass to concentrate updates and reduce redundancy; (2) Activation-Based Data Selection, which prioritizes information-dense samples that maximize contribution to key neurons; and (3) Neuron Activation Alignment, a novel loss function that anchors activations to pre-trained states, deepening representation learning and preserving general knowledge. Experimental results across three models across both domain-specific and general benchmarks demonstrate that NGFT significantly outperforms existing mainstream fine-tuning methods in both efficiency and performance, while effectively mitigating catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [95] [Solving versus Verifying: Catching Contradictions in Tax Reasoning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05928)
*Albert Sadowski,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在规范税务基准题上准确率超过90%，但真实法律输入常有缺陷。研究发现最强模型在事实缺失时会弃权，但在事实矛盾时却会直接计算并返回清晰输入答案（63-76%时间）；然而让同一模型转而验证输入时，它能发现大部分矛盾。由此提出一个简单的“矛盾门控”：多一次验证调用，若模型报告冲突则弃权。该方法在六个模型上恢复了大部分错过的矛盾弃权，清晰输入准确率损失至多约5个百分点，无需训练或外部工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高准确率模型被用于税务咨询系统，但真实法律输入常存在事实缺失或相互矛盾等缺陷。在干净基准上的准确率无法反映模型在缺陷输入下的行为，而直接计算的系统会自信地输出错误数字且无任何异常信号，因此需要研究模型能否在缺陷输入下弃权或发现缺陷。

Method: 在SARA税务案例上对六个近期模型施加缺失事实和矛盾事实两类扰动，首先考察模型求解时是否会弃权；其次让同一模型转而执行输入验证任务；最后将验证调用接入一个简单的矛盾门控机制——当模型报告冲突时放弃输出，并通过额外一次调用恢复错过的弃权。

Result: 最强模型在事实缺失时会弃权，但面对注入的矛盾会直接计算，63-76%的时间返回干净输入答案且无冲突信号；改为验证任务后，模型能标记大多数矛盾。矛盾门控在全部六个模型上恢复了大部分错过的矛盾弃权，清晰输入准确率损失最多约5个百分点。

Conclusion: 在规范输入上的高准确率只是可靠性评估的不完整指标。求解模型遗漏的缺陷检测可以通过一次简单的自检（额外验证调用）低成本地恢复，无需训练或外部工具支持。

Abstract: Large language models now compute correct tax liabilities on over 90% of well-formed cases in statutory benchmarks, which makes them candidates for the tax-advisory and compliance systems that consume such an answer directly. Real legal inputs, however, are frequently defective: required facts are missing, or stated facts contradict one another. Accuracy on clean benchmarks says nothing about how a model behaves then, and a system that computes straight through a defective input returns a confident number with no sign that anything is wrong. This raises two questions: does a model asked to solve a case abstain when the input is defective, and when it does not, can the same model catch the defect when asked instead to verify the input? We study six recent models on SARA-derived tax cases under missing-fact and contradictory-fact perturbations. The strongest models abstain when a fact is missing but compute through injected contradictions, returning the clean-input answer 63-76% of the time with no signal of the conflict; asked instead to verify the same input, they flag most of those contradictions. We wire that verification call into a simple contradiction gate: one extra call that abstains when the model reports a conflict. Across all six models it recovers most of the missed contradiction abstention at a clean-accuracy cost of at most about 5 percentage points, with no training and no external tooling. High accuracy on well-formed inputs is therefore an incomplete measure of reliability, and the detection the solver misses is cheaply recoverable with a single self-check.

</details>


### [96] [SurveyAgent-HKA: A multi-agent framework for scientific survey generation with LLMs and human knowledge augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05938)
*Tong Bao,Mir Tafseer Nayeem,Yi Zhao,Davood Rafiei,Chengzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SurveyAgent-HKA，一个多智能体框架，通过结合已发表综述和同行评审意见中的知识，将科学综述生成分解为多个子任务，提升了端到端综述生成的质量、引用质量与结构一致性，同时兼顾时间与成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动科学综述生成方法通常仅从单一数据源（如arXiv）检索文献并通过一次大型语言模型调用生成综述，导致文献覆盖有限，且无法复现专家驱动的修订过程，而该过程对撰写高质量综述至关重要。

Method: 框架将综述生成分解为若干由LLM智能体处理的子任务：(1)从多源检索相关论文并通过聚类识别关键主题构建初始大纲；(2)利用人工撰写的相关综述大纲进行细化；(3)基于细化后的大纲检索主题聚焦论文并重排以选出用于起草的论文；(4)从已发表综述的同行评审意见中识别专家常见问题，用于指导修订并最终完善综述。

Result: 在两个领域上的实验表明，该方法在引用质量、结构一致性和内容质量方面均优于主流基线，同时在时间和成本上高效。

Conclusion: SurveyAgent-HKA为广泛的人工智能辅助科学写作提供了一种实用的解决方案，通过知识增强的多智能体协作实现了更高质量且高效的综述生成。

Abstract: Automatic scientific survey generation has become an important task in scientific document processing. The common approach of retrieving literature from a single source (e.g., arXiv) and generating surveys through a one-pass large language model (LLM) call often leads to limited reference coverage and, more importantly, fails to replicate the expert-driven revision process that is crucial for writing high-quality surveys. In this paper, we introduce SurveyAgent-HKA, a multi-agent framework that improves end-to-end scientific survey generation by incorporating knowledge derived from published surveys and peer-review comments. The framework decomposes survey generation into well-defined sub-tasks handled by LLM-powered agent. It first retrieves relevant papers from multiple sources and identifies key topics through clustering to construct an initial outline, which is then refined using outlines from related human-written surveys. Based on the refined outline, topic-focused papers are retrieved and re-ranked to select for drafting a well-grounded survey. Then, we identify common issues raised by experts in peer-review comments from published surveys to guide the revisions and finalize the survey. Experiments on two domains show that our approach outperforms mainstream baselines in citation quality, structural consistency, and content quality. Furthermore, our framework is efficient in both time and cost, making it a practical solution for broader AI-assisted scientific writing applications.

</details>


### [97] [The Blindness of Document-Level Translation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05949)
*Ahrii Kim,Vilém Zouhar,Chanjun Park,Seong-heum Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过反事实实验（MIX条件）检验篇章级机器翻译评估协议的有效性。在18,420条专家英韩标注和14个自动指标上，连贯与不连贯文档的分数、系统排名和错误标注在统计上等价。尽管标注者能识别连贯文本（87.3%），但输出记录不受影响，表明是评估协议而非标注者本身"视而不见"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 篇章级机器翻译评估假设以完整文档形式呈现会给标注者带来篇章级判断，但这一假设从未被直接检验。作者质疑目前的篇章级评估协议是否真正测量了它声称要测量的内容，以及投入在篇章级系统、指标和标注上的资源是否有效。

Method: 采用反事实实验设计（MIX条件）：将来自不同系统的片段组合成文档，保持篇章级呈现方式但破坏跨片段一致性。同时设置感知实验验证标注者能否区分连贯与不连贯文本。使用18,420条专家英语-韩语标注和14个自动指标进行统计分析。

Result: 连贯文档与不连贯文档（MIX条件）在分数、系统排名和错误标注上统计等价；但感知实验中标注者在87.3%的试验中能正确识别连贯文本为单一译者作品。说明文档呈现确实改变标注者的工作方式，但这种改变未反映在记录输出中。

Conclusion: 当前篇章级评估协议的盲点在于协议本身而非标注者。文档呈现方式虽然影响标注者的认知过程，但最终产出的评估结果未捕捉到这一差异，意味着现有篇章级评估可能并未真正测量篇章级质量，需要重新设计评估协议。

Abstract: Document-level machine translation (MT) evaluation extends segment-level protocols by presenting full documents to annotators, on the assumption that such presentation elicits document-level judgments. We test this assumption with a counterfactual condition (MIX) in which each document combines segments drawn from different systems, preserving document-level presentation while breaking cross-segment consistency. Across 18,420 expert Englis-to-Korean annotations and 14 automatic metrics, scores, system rankings, and error annotations are statistically equivalent between coherent and incoherent documents. Perception does not explain this: shown matched passages, raters identify the coherent one as the work of a single translator in 87.3% of trials. Document presentation does change how annotators work, but that change does not reach the recorded output. What is blind is the protocol, not the annotator. The concern is not that scores fall short, but that the resources invested in document-level systems, metrics, and annotation may not be measuring what they are intended to measure.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Cross-Lingual Transfer: Benchmarking Propagation Boundaries in Multilingual LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05976)
*Pengyang Shao,Chuanpeng Lu,Wei Qin,Yanzheng Jin,Xiaohao Liu,Xi Ai,Kenji Kawaguchi,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.CL
