Mainstream systems achieve this by concatenating event spans or using shallow fusion, which works well when all model parameters are trainable. However, in parameter-efficient fine-tuning, low-rank bottlenecks restrict information flow and prevent these interaction signals from passing through, leading to clear performance drops. To address this limitation, we propose a theoretically grounded architecture, Convolutional Bottleneck Interaction (CBI), which first applies lightweight depthwise convolution to enhance event representations and then uses element-wise multiplication to capture effective event-event interactions inside the bottleneck. Across five datasets and seven backbone models in the Adapter and LoRA settings, CBI provides consistent and substantial gains, up to +31.7 micro F1, while adding minimal computational cost, showing that explicit interaction inside low-rank spaces is crucial for temporal relation extraction. The code is available at https://github.com/VRCMF/CIF.git.

</details>


### [132] [AuthBench: A Large-Scale Multilingual Benchmark for Authorship Representation across Genres and Lengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06771)
*MaoXun Huang,Zhenxing Zhang,Claire Cardie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了AuthBench，一个大规模多语言作者身份表征基准，包含428,150篇文档、153,825位作者，覆盖10种语言、9个主要体裁和4个文档长度区段，支持作者归属（检索）和作者验证（二分类）两项任务。在统一零样本协议下评测了47个神经模型和3个非神经基线，结果显示作者身份表征问题远未解决：最佳检索模型Success@5仅为0.258，最佳验证模型EER为0.076、ROC-AUC为0.968，且不同模型族在不同任务上各领风骚，跨语言、体裁和长度表现差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作者身份基准零散且碎片化，通常只覆盖单一语言、单一体裁或有限的文档长度范围，难以评估现代表征是否真正具备泛化能力。作者身份信号在数字取证、剽窃分析、账号关联、虚假信息调查和机器生成文本检测等场景中至关重要，因此需要一个广泛、标准化且真实的基准来系统评测该问题。

Method: 构建AuthBench大型多语言基准，包含428,150篇文档、153,825位作者，横跨10种语言、9个主要体裁（66个细粒度体裁）、4个文档长度区段。支持两个互补任务：作者归属（同作者检索）和作者验证（同作者二元判定）。在统一的零样本协议下对47个神经模型和3个非神经基线进行基准评测。

Result: 最佳检索模型仅达到0.258的Success@5，最佳验证模型达到0.076 EER和0.968 ROC-AUC。排行榜显示检索和验证各自由不同模型家族领先，且跨语言、体裁和文档长度存在显著性能差异，表明作者身份表征仍远未解决。

Conclusion: AuthBench不仅是一个新基准，还可作为诊断资源用于研究作者身份表征何时以及为何成功或失败。作者团队已公开基准数据、评估工具包及数据集的访问渠道。

Abstract: Authorship signals matter in settings where writing style carries identity: digital forensics, plagiarism analysis, account linking, misinformation investigation, and machine-generated text detection. Yet current authorship benchmarks remain fragmented, usually covering only a narrow language set, a single genre, or a limited document-length regime, which makes it difficult to assess whether modern representations truly generalize. We introduce AuthBench, a large-scale multilingual benchmark for authorship representation that is designed to make this evaluation broad, standardized, and realistic. AuthBench contains 428,150 documents written by 153,825 individuals across ten widely used languages, 9 primary genres, 66 fine-grained genres, and four document-length buckets. It supports two complementary tasks: authorship attribution, formulated as same-author retrieval and authorship verification, formulated as same-author binary decision. We benchmark 47 neural models and three non-neural baselines under a unified zero-shot protocol. Results show that authorship representation remains far from solved: the best retrieval model reaches only 0.258 Success@5, while the best verification model achieves 0.076 EER and 0.968 ROC-AUC. The leaderboard also reveals a meaningful task split, with different model families leading retrieval and verification, and large performance differences across languages, genres, and lengths.
  These findings position AuthBench not only as a new benchmark, but as a diagnostic resource for studying when and why authorship representations succeed or fail. We release AuthBench, its evaluation toolkit, and benchmark data at https://github.com/mao-code/AuthBench and https://huggingface.co/datasets/MaoXun/AuthBench.

</details>


### [133] [Typed Federated Artifacts for the Agentic Web:Sharing Tool-Routing Knowledge Across Frozen,Heterogeneous LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06815)
*Abhijit Chakraborty,Ni Trieu,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SYNAPSE1——一种类型化的联邦工具路由知识共享框架，通过schema验证的联邦工件替代扁平文本提示，实现跨模型迁移和逐字段隐私。在StableToolBench上，联邦知识库仅比集中式差1.1个点（每客户端20MB JSON）；类型化字段渲染比扁平字符串高出8.5个点（干净数据）。但研究发现冲突日志并非瓶颈，且TF-IDF分类器在多个基准上超越所有LLM路由方案，揭示基准无法衡量对无标签工具的路由能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的智能体网络依赖扁平文本（提示、示例池），难以区分噪声统计、合并规则和文档；权重和适配器无法在平台间转移知识。缺乏一种支持逐字段隐私、争议解决和跨模型迁移的共享知识机制。

Method: 提出共享类型化联邦工件（schema验证对象），将其实例化为SYNAPSE1工具路由知识库。数据预处理包括删除192个垃圾条目和1,916个重复或近似重复测试查询的训练项。对比联邦与集中式知识库、类型化字段与扁平字符串渲染、不同冲突策略等维度，并在StableToolBench和τ-bench零售数据集上评测路由器及GPT-4o智能体。

Result: 联邦知识库在StableToolBench上达到集中式的1.1点以内（20MB JSON/客户端/轮）。类型化字段相对扁平字符串在干净数据上值8.5点，在60%矛盾注入下值7.4点。类型化合并+扁平渲染是最差组合，表明两者不可分割。三个冲突策略无显著差异。在τ-bench零售上每个联邦arm提升GPT-4o每步工具调用准确率至少6.7点（归因于格式而非联邦经验）。TF-IDF分类器在同一标记数据上分别以48和26点超越所有LLM路由arm。

Conclusion: 类型化联邦工件（SYNAPSE1）能有效支持工具路由知识共享，但其收益主要来自格式而非联邦经验。更重要的警示是：当前基准因包含所有工具及其测试查询的标签，无法衡量对无标签工具的泛化路由——这正是路由本身存在的意义，需要重新设计基准度量方式。

Abstract: An open, networked web will allow agents to run frozen models from multiple vendors, keep their history private, and teach each other which tool to call and when. Flat text (prompts, example pools) makes it difficult for the protocol to distinguish between noise statistics, merging rules, and documentation. Weights and adapters cannot transfer that knowledge between platforms. We suggest sharing typed federated artifacts, schema-validated objects with well-defined fields for per-field privacy (described here, but measured), dispute resolution, and cross-model transfer, and instantiating them as SYNAPSE1, a common tool-routing knowledge. After deleting 192 garbage entries and 1,916 training items that duplicate or almost duplicate test queries, a federated compendium routes within 1.1 points of a centralized one at 20 MB of JSON per client each round on StableToolBench (3,180 tools). The same experience merged and shown to the router as typed fields rather than one flat string is worth 8.5 points on clean data and 7.4 under 60% injected contradiction. Crossing merge and rendering shows the halves are inseparable (the typed merge shown flat is the worst arm), while three conflict policies are indistinguishable, so the conflict log that motivated this work is not the On τ-bench retail, each compendium arm improves GPT-4o agents' per-step tool-call accuracy by at least 6.7 points, attributed to format rather than federated experience. Two cautionary findings conclude the paper: on a topic-labeled math proxy and StableToolBench, a TF-IDF classifier over the same labeled experience beats every LLM routing arm (by 48 and 26 points, mostly retrieval recall) because the benchmark's pool holds labeled queries for every supposedly unseen tool and every test query verbatim before our filter. It cannot measure routing to tools without labels, which routing exists for.

</details>


### [134] [XYBench: Can LLMs Respond Pragmatically to Queries with Misconceptions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06842)
*Akhila Yerukola,Jena D. Hwang,Mingqian Zheng,Jenna Godsey,Hyunwoo Kim,Valentina Pyatkin,Jennifer Hu,Maarten Sap*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍XYBench基准，包含8,115个带有误解的XY-问题查询（来自StackOverflow/WikiHow等），评估LLM能否识别用户误解并引导至真正解决问题的实用方案。实验表明最强大的LLM也主要回答表面问题而非真正意图，在识别误解上远落后人类（最多63% vs 79-90%），表明务实引导（pragmatic redirection）是当前LLM严重欠缺的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非专家用户向LLM提问时经常带有误解（即XY问题），LLM需要识别误解并引导用户解决根本问题，而非机械回答字面问题。现有研究缺乏系统评估LLM这一能力的基准，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建XYBench基准，包含8,115个来自技术（StackOverflow/StackExchange）和日常（WikiHow及人工整理子集）领域的查询；设计基于合作响应理论的三维评估框架：(a)实用方案的呈现、(b)实用方案的强调程度、(c)误解的识别；并进行Oracle消融实验验证显式提供用户意图对生成的影响。

Result: 即使最强LLM也主要回答字面请求（0.75-0.92），回答真正意图的比例低得多（0.33-0.71）；LLM识别误解的能力最多达63%，远低于人类的79-90%；模型在多选题设置中压倒性偏好务实响应却在开放生成中无法生成之；Oracle实验显示提供显式用户意图有帮助，但仍有显著差距。

Conclusion: 务实重定向（pragmatic redirection）是当前LLM中根本性的未发展能力，即使最强模型也难以在识别用户误解的同时给出引导根本问题的解决方案，这一能力缺口值得未来深入研究。

Abstract: When non-expert users ask LLMs for assistance, their queries can often have misconceptions (e.g., "How do I parse XML with regex?"). In such cases, often referred to as the XY-problem, LLMs must identify the misconception ("regex are fragile") and meaningfully direct the user toward a pragmatic solution that will address the root problem implicit in the request ("use an XML parser"). We introduce XYBench, a benchmark of 8,115 such queries, drawn from technical (StackOverflow/StackExchange) and everyday (WikiHow and a manually-curated subset) domains. We design an evaluation paradigm that assesses model responses along three criteria grounded in cooperative response theory: (a) presence and (b) emphasis on pragmatic solutions, and (c) identification of misconceptions. Our experiments show that even the strongest LLMs predominantly answer the literal request (0.75--0.92) and far less often the intended one (0.33--0.71), while substantially lagging behind humans at identifying misconceptions (at most 63% vs. 79--90%). Further, models overwhelmingly prefer pragmatic responses in a multiple choice setting yet consistently fail to generate them. Oracle ablation experiments show that providing explicit user intent at generation time helps; however a large gap remains, suggesting pragmatic redirection is a fundamentally underdeveloped capability in current LLMs.

</details>


### [135] [You Are What You Read: Misalignment via In-Context Persona Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06851)
*Kyuhee Kim,Benjamin Berczi,Cozmin Ududec*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出"人格归纳"（persona induction）现象：仅通过将若干普通对话形式呈现的个人传记事实放入模型的上下文中（无需微调、无需展示有害行为的示例），即可使LLM以该人物身份回答问题，即使问题与这些事实无关。九个角色和十三个模型中，身份采纳率随事实数量呈S形增长，3至10个事实内即可超过50%。无害人格被完全采纳且几乎无错位，而有害人格会在无关问题上以高达80%的比例表达其特有观点。格式化指令可控制人格激活时机。与直接指令相比，累积的传记上下文仅被内容过滤器标记3%的输入（直接指令为24-33%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究显示，微调于窄范围数据或上下文中展示不良行为示例可导致模型错位。作者试图探究：在没有微调、没有有害行为示范的前提下，看似良性的信息（普通人传记事实）能否仅通过上下文放置即引发模型产生错位行为，从而揭示一种更隐蔽的越狱/错位路径。

Method: 在模型上下文中以普通对话轮次形式插入多个汇聚于同一人物（persona）的传记事实，不进行任何微调或有害行为示范。随后测试模型在无关问题上的回答是否以该人物身份进行。研究覆盖9个人物、13种模型，并通过改变事实数量、人物善恶属性以及添加格式化指令来控制人格激活条件，同时对比内容过滤器的检测率。

Result: 身份采纳率随传记事实数量呈S形上升，3至10个事实内即跨过50%阈值；无害人物可达完全采纳且几乎无错位；有害人物在无关问题上以高达80%的频率表达其特征性观点。格式化指令可控制人格激活与否。内容过滤器仅将3%的累积传记上下文输入标记为有害，而对等价直接指令的标记率为24-33%。

Conclusion: 上下文中的良性传记数据本身就足以诱导LLM产生人格采纳与相关错位，无需微调或有害示范。这构成一条难以被现有内容过滤器识别的隐蔽错位路径，提示上下文长度扩展与社会工程攻击对模型安全的潜在风险，需要新的防御机制应对此类不依赖明显有害内容的诱导方式。

Abstract: Broad misalignment has been produced by finetuning on narrow data, harmful or benign, and in context only by demonstrations of the undesirable behaviour itself. We show that benign data suffices in context, with no finetuning and no demonstration of harmful behaviour in the prompt. Biographical facts that converge on a single figure, placed in a model's context as ordinary conversational turns, lead it to answer as that figure on questions the facts never touch. We call this persona induction. Across nine personas and thirteen models, identity adoption rises sigmoidally with the number of facts and crosses 50% within 3 to 10 of them. Misalignment then tracks which figure is described. Harmless personas reach full adoption with near-zero misalignment, while harmful ones voice their characteristic views on unrelated questions, at rates up to 80%. A formatting instruction can gate when the persona activates. Because each fact is individually benign, accumulated biographical context is flagged by content filters on 3% of inputs against 24-33% for an equivalent direct instruction.

</details>


### [136] [AutoLexSteer: Automatic Contrast Construction for Lexical Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06879)
*Shuhe Wang,Lachlan Cowley,Eduard Hovy,Jey Han Lau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Hangman（一种基于词义的引导向量）和AutoLexSteer（首个全自动构建引导向量的流程），利用WordNet中紧密相关的词族来指定需避开的引导源和期望的引导目标，实现词级和词义级的精确LLM行为引导（如谄媚行为）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引导向量（steering vectors）已成为一种流行的LLM输出引导方法，但构建准确的引导向量由于embedding的不透明性而依赖困难的手动过程。

Method: 提出Hangman引导向量类型，其基于词义（word senses）运作；并提出AutoLexSteer，首个全自动的引导向量构建流程，通过从WordNet提取紧密相关的词族来同时指定需避免的引导源和期望的引导目标。

Result: 该引导向量非常精确，可在词级和词组词义（含义）层面进行引导，并能引导某些LLM行为（如sycophancy/谄媚行为）。

Conclusion: AutoLexSteer实现了引导向量的全自动化构建，显著降低手工成本，同时保持高精度，并可在词义层面实现可解释的精确引导。

Abstract: Steering vectors have rapidly emerged as a popular and effective method for guiding the output of LLMs in very specific ways. But constructing accurate steering vectors is a difficult manual process due to the opacity of embeddings. We introduce Hangman, a novel type of steering vector that operates using word senses, as well as AutoLexSteer, the first fully automated process for building steering vectors. AutoLexSteer employs families of closely-related words extracted from WordNet to specify both the steering source to be avoided and the desired steering target. The steering vectors are quite precise, can be used to steer at the level of words and sets of word senses (meanings), and are able to steer certain LLM behaviors like sycophancy. The dataset and code can be found at https://github.com/ShuheWang1998/autolexsteer.

</details>


### [137] [Towards Bridging the Gap Between Offline and Iterative Alignment via Preference Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06893)
*Wenbo Zhang,Wenzhuo Zhou,Hengrui Cai,Zhengling Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DP3O的离线对齐算法，通过显式偏好模型蒸馏知识到策略优化中，弥补了离线DPO与迭代DPO之间的性能差距，在保持离线方法简单高效的同时达到与迭代方法相当的性能，并减少约42%的训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代DPO在学术基准上通常优于离线DPO，作者希望回答两个关键问题：(i) 为什么迭代方法普遍优于离线方法？(ii) 迭代方法的优势能否被纳入离线对齐中？以弥合离线与迭代方法之间的性能鸿沟。

Method: 通过受控实验发现迭代过程中的显式偏好模型是其优越性的关键因素。基于此提出DP3O：首先利用辅助LLM类别学习显式偏好模型，然后将其知识蒸馏到策略优化中。理论上，显式偏好建模比隐式公式具有更好的估计误差控制，DP3O通过方差缩减实现了比硬标签DPO更紧的泛化界。

Result: DP3O在广泛的基于聊天的任务和下游任务上超越了最先进的离线方法，达到与迭代DPO相当的性能，并将训练时间减少约42%。

Conclusion: 显式偏好模型是迭代DPO优于离线方法的关键因素，且这一优势可以有效地纳入离线对齐框架中。DP3O验证了离线对齐算法在保持效率的同时可以达到迭代方法的性能水平。

Abstract: Direct preference optimization DPO is a promising offline approach for aligning large language models (LLMs) due to its simplicity, computational efficiency, and implicit modeling of human preferences. Interestingly, iterative extensions of DPO have achieved stronger performance on academic benchmarks, raising two key questions: (i) Why do iterative methods generally outperform offline ones? (ii) Can their advantages be incorporated into offline alignment? To answer the first question, our controlled experiments reveal that the explicit preference model, additionally introduced in the iterative procedure, is a key factor behind its superiority over offline methods. This insight leads us to answer the second question affirmatively and propose Distilled Preference Probability Policy Optimization (DP3O), an effective and efficient offline alignment algorithm. DP3O first learns an explicit preference model using a helper class of LLMs and then distills its knowledge into policy optimization. Theoretically, we show that explicit preference modeling admits better estimation error control than implicit formulations, and that DP3O achieves a tighter generalization bound than hard-label DPO through variance reduction. Empirically, we evaluate DP3O on a wide range of chat-based and downstream tasks and show that it outperforms state-of-the-art offline methods, achieves performance comparable to iterative DPO, and reduces training time by about $42\%$, demonstrating both its effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [138] [Dynamic-Programming-Guided Hierarchical BPE and Empirical Analysis of Vocabulary Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06898)
*Kenny Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DH-BPE，一种结合动态规划与BPE层次依赖的词汇表构建方法，通过暴露度引导和依赖感知剪枝，在固定模型可见词汇量下优化压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准BPE通过贪婪对合并构建词汇表，但合并顺序并不保证为固定模型可见词汇表大小下的压缩率最优分配。

Method: 从适度超采样（overshoot）的BPE候选词汇出发，使用动态规划度量候选实用性，并通过暴露度引导、依赖感知的剪枝策略选择固定大小的模型可见词汇表。保留BPE训练的层次依赖关系，同时结合精确最小分词下的token暴露度信息。

Result: 在12K和16K目标词汇量的主比较中，DH-BPE在聚合压缩率上一致优于Standard BPE、Pruned BPE和MinGram。MinGram-PP在主比较中压缩率更强，但在跨语料评估中DH-BPE在超采样因子f=2.0和f=3.0时超过MinGram-PP；在12K时MinGram-PP仅在f=4.0和f=5.0的更大候选池下反转此顺序。

Conclusion: DH-BPE能够在较晚、更完整的BPE合并与可复用的子词组件之间取得良好平衡，为固定模型可见词汇预算下的词汇分配提供了可行的改进方案。

Abstract: Byte Pair Encoding (BPE) constructs vocabularies through greedy pair merging, but the resulting merge order does not necessarily allocate a fixed model-visible vocabulary optimally for compression. We propose Dynamic-Programming-Guided Hierarchical BPE (DH-BPE), a vocabulary-construction method that combines token exposure under exact minimum-token segmentation with the hierarchical dependencies induced by BPE training. Starting from a modestly overshot BPE candidate vocabulary, DH-BPE uses dynamic programming to measure candidate utility and applies exposure-guided, dependency-aware pruning to select a fixed-size model-visible vocabulary. We compare DH-BPE against Standard BPE and recent vocabulary-optimization baselines, including Pruned BPE, MinGram, and MinGram-PP, in primary evaluations at 12K and 16K target vocabulary sizes, with an additional 18K evaluation against MinGram only. Across the primary 12K and 16K comparisons, DH-BPE consistently improves aggregate compression over Standard BPE, Pruned BPE, and MinGram under a shared exact minimum-token DP encoder. MinGram-PP achieves stronger aggregate compression in the primary comparisons, but DH-BPE outperforms it at overshoot factors f = 2.0 and f = 3.0 in cross-corpus evaluation; at 12K, MinGram-PP reverses this ordering only with the substantially larger candidate pools at f = 4.0 and f = 5.0. Qualitative analysis further shows that DH-BPE balances later, more complete BPE merges with reusable subword components, providing a practical approach to improving vocabulary allocation under a fixed model-visible vocabulary budget.

</details>


### [139] [TurEngMix: A Text Corpus and Benchmark for Turkish-English Code-Mixed Language Identification and Named Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06963)
*Ilayda Dogan,Phuong-Anh Nguyen-Le,Julia Mendelsohn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍TurEngMix语料库，包含55,000条土耳其语-英语混合语言社交媒体帖子，并构建了混合语言识别（LID）和命名实体识别（NER）基准。研究发现现有模型在混合语言token上错误率极高，形态整合token的NER错误率是纯语言token的5-6倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言处理系统在混合语言（code-mixed）文本上表现不佳，尤其是低资源语言对。土耳其语-英语尤其困难，因为英语词干可以与土耳其语后缀结合形成单个混合语言token。现有基准和研究对此关注不足。

Method: 构建了TurEngMix语料库：包含5.5K条自然的社交媒体帖子（486,974个token），从中构建了包含15K条专家标注token的土耳其语-英语混合语言识别和命名实体识别基准。评估了解码器式LLM和微调编码器基线模型。

Result: 单语土耳其语和英语token能被可靠标注，但所有模型在混合语言token上的LID和NER错误率都很高。对于形态整合token，GPT-4o和Qwen的NER错误率分别高出5.2倍和6.3倍。

Conclusion: 形态整合（morphological integration）仍然是混合语言处理的主要挑战。作者发布了语料库、标注和代码，以支持未来关于土耳其语-英语混合语言的计算和社会语言学研究。

Abstract: Natural language processing systems underperform on code-mixed text, particularly for low-resource language pairs. Turkish-English poses a further challenge: it lets English stems combine with Turkish suffixes to form single mixed-language tokens. We introduce TurEngMix, a corpus of 5.5K noisy, naturally occurring social media posts (486,974 tokens) rich in Turkish-English code-mixing. From this corpus, we construct a new Turkish-English benchmark for code-mixed language identification (LID) and named entity recognition (NER), comprising 15K expert-annotated tokens. Evaluating both decoder LLM and fine-tuned encoder baselines, we find that monolingual Turkish and English tokens are labeled reliably, but all models have high error rates on mixed-language tokens for both LID and NER. For morphologically integrated tokens, NER error rates were 5.2x and 6.3x higher for GPT-4o and Qwen, respectively. This highlights how morphological integration remains a challenge. We release the corpus, annotations, and code to support future computational and sociolinguistic research on Turkish-English code-mixing.

</details>


### [140] [CantoneseLLM v2: Reasoning in a Low-Resource Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06970)
*Tsz Chung Cheng,Chung Shing Cheng,Chaak Ming Lau,Cheuk Hei Chong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布 CantoneseLLM v2，一个以粤语为核心的低资源语言模型系列（基于 Qwen3 8B 和 30B-A3B），通过 CPT、chat-vector 合并、SFT、DPO 和 RLVR 多阶段训练而成。系统地评估了各训练阶段对推理行为的影响，发现 RLVR 以粤语语言和繁体中文脚本为约束，成功恢复了因 SFT 丢失的性能并引入粤语推理对齐，30B-A3B 模型在 HKCanto-Eval 上达到 73.16 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粤语使用广泛但在书面数据上是低资源语言，缺乏用于模型训练的原生粤语推理数据，需要开发能够以粤语进行推理的大型语言模型。

Method: 基于 Qwen3 8B 和 30B-A3B，通过四个阶段训练：在 7.84 亿个粤语及香港相关 token 上进行 CPT（继续预训练），chat-vector 合并移植指令跟随能力，SFT 使用有限的粤语推理数据，DPO 恢复推理格式，最后 RLVR 以粤语语言和繁体中文脚本作为乘法约束进行强化学习。

Result: chat-vector 合并能迁移指令跟随但保留原模型推理语言；SFT 缩短/移除推理痕迹并降低基准性能；DPO 恢复推理块格式（尤其对 8B 模型）但仅恢复部分性能；RLVR 引入粤语语言对齐并完全恢复性能。30B-A3B 在 HKCanto-Eval 上达到 73.16 分，比合并检查点仅低 1.20 分，同时保留了合并检查点缺失的粤语推理行为。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习与语言约束，可以有效恢复 SFT 造成的粤语推理性能损失并实现粤语推理对齐。团队开源了模型检查点、训练环境和包含十三年繁体中文 Common Crawl 数据集。

Abstract: Cantonese is widely spoken but remains low-resource in written data, with no large corpus of native Cantonese reasoning traces available for model training. We develop and release CantoneseLLM v2, comprising models based on Qwen3 8B and 30B-A3B. The models are trained through CPT on 784 million Cantonese and Hong Kong-related tokens, chat-vector merging, SFT, DPO, and RLVR. Evaluation across the training stages shows that chat-vector merging transfers instruction following but preserves the donor model's reasoning language, while SFT with limited Cantonese reasoning data substantially shortens or removes reasoning traces and reduces benchmark performance. DPO restores the reasoning-block format, particularly for the 8B model, but recovers only part of the lost performance. The RLVR training with Cantonese language and Traditional Chinese scripts as multiplicative constraints introduced Cantonese language alignment and restored the lost performance. The 30B-A3B model reaches 73.16 on HKCanto-Eval, within 1.20 points of its merged checkpoint, while retaining the Cantonese reasoning behaviour absent from that checkpoint. We release the model checkpoints, the training environments, and a thirteen-year Traditional Chinese Common Crawl dataset. The models can be accessed at https://huggingface.co/collections/hon9kon9ize/cantonesellm-v20

</details>


### [141] [Train Overcomplete, Deploy Compact: Scaling Recovery Capacity for Structured LLM Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06974)
*Seungmin Oh,Donggeon Lee,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出OverRep框架，通过"训练时过度参数化、部署时紧凑"的策略解决结构化LLM剪枝中恢复模块表征能力与移除知识复杂度不匹配的问题，在保持推理架构和成本不变的前提下，显著提升剪枝后模型的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化剪枝在降低LLM部署成本方面有效，但其恢复阶段常因恢复模块表征能力与所移除知识复杂度不匹配（即capacity-knowledge asymmetry）而受限，现有恢复方法性能不佳。

Method: OverRep采用过完备重参数化框架：训练阶段临时将恢复模块过度参数化以吸收从原模型蒸馏的复杂知识；同时引入退火激活函数，使非线性训练动力学最终收敛到线性区间，从而实现精确的代数合并，将过完备参数合并回数学等价的紧凑模块，保持推理时的架构与计算成本不变。

Result: 在三种骨干模型家族上，OverRep在25%和50%剪枝率下分别比强恢复基线提升最多5.5和8.4个推理性能点，同时内存使用和TFLOPs与现有恢复方法相当。

Conclusion: OverRep通过训练-部署参数规模解耦的方式有效解决了剪枝恢复中的容量不对称问题，在不大幅增加计算成本的前提下实现了更强的推理性能恢复，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models achieve strong performance across diverse tasks, but deployment remains costly because of memory, latency, and energy demands. Structured pruning reduces these costs by removing architectural components, yet its recovery stage is often limited by a mismatch between the recovery module's representational capacity and the complexity of the removed knowledge. We call this bottleneck the capacity-knowledge asymmetry and propose OverRep, an Overcomplete Reparameterization framework for structured LLM pruning. Following the principle of "train overcomplete, deploy compact", OverRep temporarily overparameterizes the recovery module during training to absorb complex knowledge distilled from the original model. After recovery, the overcomplete re-parameterization is algebraically merged into a mathematically equivalent compact module, preserving the pruned model's inference-time architecture and computational cost. OverRep further introduces an annealed activation that enables nonlinear training dynamics while converging to a linear regime for exact algebraic merging. Across three backbone families, OverRep improves retained reasoning performance over strong recovery baselines by up to 5.5 and 8.4 points at 25% and 50% pruning, respectively, while keeping memory usage and TFLOPs comparable to existing recovery methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/mmai-laboratory/OverRep.

</details>


### [142] [Aha-Flow Distillation: Flow Markers Matter in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07036)
*Xiaodong Wang,Peixi Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文识别了推理过程中的"Flow Moment"（持续、确认过程的言语模式，如"I'm doing"）与已有的"Aha Moment"（修订与回溯导向）相对应，并据此构建Flow-CoT（重写推理轨迹的语标记而不改变推理内容），将其作为辅助监督用于on-policy自蒸馏（OPSD）。进一步提出Aha-Flow Distillation（AFD）双模式扩展。实验在AIME25和HMMT25上对Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型推理自蒸馏方法（如基于Aha Moment的OPSD）主要关注突发的"顿悟"式推理修正，忽视了另一种更具持续性和过程确认特征的有效推理模式（Flow Moment）。作者希望通过识别并利用这种新的推理风格来进一步改进模型的自我蒸馏效果。

Method: 1) 识别并定义Flow Moment及其语言表达Flow Markers（如"I'm doing"），与Aha Markers（如重新启句、回溯表述）相对应；2) 构造Flow-CoT：仅重写原始推理轨迹中的话语标记（discourse markers），保留底层推理内容不变；3) 提出Aha-Flow Distillation（AFD）：这是OPSD的双模式扩展，Aha分支保留简洁的基于解决方案的监督，Flow分支在直接、自信的推理指令下引入重写后的Flow-CoT。推理时仅使用标准反思指令，Flow风格推理仅作为训练信号。

Result: 在AIME25和HMMT25基准上取得一致改进：AFD在Qwen3-8B上将Avg@12从60.8提升至61.3，在Qwen3-4B上将Avg@12从57.5提升至58.6（对比复现的OPSD基线）。受控消融实验表明，在相同的Flow-CoT/Aha-CoT组成下，双模式训练将Avg@12从59.5提升至60.1，证明收益不仅来自引入异构推理监督，还来自自蒸馏中的组织形式。代码已开源。

Conclusion: Flow-style推理与Aha-style推理都是大模型有效推理的组成部分，将二者以双模式组织的形式融入自蒸馏能够比单一模式带来更好的性能。这表明提高蒸馏质量的关键既包括引入多样化的推理监督信号，也包括如何合理地组织这些信号。

Abstract: We identify the Flow Moment, a reasoning pattern characterized by sustained, process-confirming verbalizations such as I'm doing, in contrast to the revision- and backtracking-oriented Aha Moment. We refer to their corresponding linguistic expressions as Flow Markers and Aha Markers, respectively. Based on this observation, we construct Flow-CoT by rewriting the discourse markers of original reasoning traces while preserving their underlying reasoning content, and use it as auxiliary supervision for on-policy self-distillation (OPSD). We further propose \textbf{Aha-Flow Distillation (AFD)}, a dual-mode extension of OPSD that pairs different forms of privileged information with corresponding reasoning instructions. The Aha branch retains concise solution-based supervision, while the Flow branch introduces rewritten Flow-CoT under a direct and confident reasoning instruction. At inference time, the model uses only the standard reflective instruction, so Flow-style reasoning serves purely as a training signal. Experiments on AIME25 and HMMT25 show consistent improvements across Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B: AFD improves Avg@12 from 60.8 to 61.3 on Qwen3-8B and from 57.5 to 58.6 on Qwen3-4B over our reproduced OPSD baselines. Controlled ablations further show that, with the same Flow-CoT/Aha-CoT composition, dual-mode training improves Avg@12 from 59.5 to 60.1, indicating that the benefit comes not only from introducing heterogeneous reasoning supervision, but also from how it is organized during self-distillation. The code is available at https://github.com/Wang-Xiaodong1899/Aha-Flow-Distillation.

</details>


### [143] [A Hyperbolicity Atlas of Large Language Model Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07053)
*Zhichao Yang,Yuanze Hu,Gen Li,Qingchen Yu,Shiying Duan,Xinyu Wang,Ye Qiu,Zeming Liu,Guangxu Chen,Zhaoxin Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究当代LLM中提示词token的隐藏状态是否表现出Gromov双曲性（一种基于距离的树状结构度量）。通过对十个开源模型在四个数据集上的818,904个样本-层测量，建立了跨越参数规模、层深度、模型家族和输入领域四个轴的双曲性图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM隐藏状态是普通向量，但其向量间的距离可能仍呈现层级结构。此前尚无系统性研究探讨当代LLM中提示token隐藏状态是否表现出Gromov双曲性，作者希望填补这一空白，并将双曲性用作实用的诊断工具。

Method: 对十个开源权重模型（包括匹配的7/8B模型家族），在MATH500、HumanEval、WinoGrande和TruthfulQA四个数据集上进行测量，构建了包含818,904个样本-层测量数据的GH图谱，跨越参数规模、层深度、模型家族与输入领域四个分析维度。

Result: 最显著的模式是层深度而非参数规模：中间层通常形成高相对双曲性平台，而最后几层往往变得更加树状。规模效应较弱且非单调；匹配的7/8B模型家族之间存在显著差异；输入领域与模型特化相互作用。

Conclusion: 这些发现使GH成为实用的诊断工具：它能揭示层级距离结构出现的位置、特化如何改变它，以及哪些模型-层-领域组合值得更深入的分析。

Abstract: LLM hidden states are ordinary vectors, but the distances among those vectors may still show hierarchical structure. To our knowledge, this paper is the first systematic study of whether prompt-token hidden states in contemporary LLMs exhibit Gromov Hyperbolicity (GH), a distance-based measure of tree-likeness. Using 818,904 sample-layer measurements from ten open-weight models across MATH500, HumanEval, WinoGrande, and TruthfulQA, we build a GH map over four axes: parameter scale, layer depth, model family, and input domain. The clearest pattern is depth, not scale: middle layers usually form a high-relative-hyperbolicity plateau, while final layers often become substantially more tree-like. Scale effects are weak and non-monotonic, matched 7/8B model families differ strongly, and domains interact with model specialization. These findings make GH useful as a practical diagnostic: it shows where hierarchical distance structure appears, how specialization changes it, and which model-layer-domain comparisons deserve closer analysis.

</details>


### [144] [Where to Look and What to Use: Retrieve-Localize-Generate for Long-Term Conversational Memory Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07093)
*Yifan Wang,Xinkui Lin,Yongxiu Xu,Shen Gao,Ruochen Yang,Kun Huang,Yubin Wang,Jie Wu,Wei Liu,Jian Luan,Hongbo Xu,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemLoc是一个针对长对话记忆问答的统一"检索-定位-生成"框架，通过多粒度内存单元分解与图结构路由检索、基于推理的策略优化定位（SHPO）以及轻量级位置ID指导生成，解决了证据碎片化和检索内容噪声触发的lost-in-the-middle效应两大挑战，在四个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法用于长对话记忆问答时面临两大挑战：(1)相关证据碎片化地分布在时间跨度长的不同会话中，难以被有效聚合；(2)检索到的会话内容含有噪声，触发LLM的lost-in-the-middle效应（即模型偏向利用头尾位置信息而忽略中间内容），影响回答质量。

Method: MemLoc包含三个核心模块：检索阶段将每个会话分解为多粒度内存单元，通过内存储图进行基于熵的粒度选择的查询路由，并通过跨存储图建模跨会话语义与时间依赖，实现由粗到细的top-K候选检索；定位阶段引入基于自反思Hint策略优化（SHPO）训练的推理式证据定位器，通过渐进式细化和跨候选重排序抑制噪声并去除冗余，最终输出带轻量级位置ID的紧凑证据集；生成阶段将位置ID作为精确grounding信号引导LLM定位到正确的内存位置，缓解lost-in-the-middle效应且保留原始上下文完整性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MemLoc在检索准确率和回答质量上均达到最先进水平，同时保持了良好的效率。

Conclusion: MemLoc通过统一的Retrieve-Localize-Generate框架，有效解决了长对话记忆QA中的证据碎片化与内容噪声问题，验证了显式证据定位与位置ID引导生成在提升长对话记忆问答质量上的有效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) enables large language models (LLMs) to answer questions by accessing external knowledge and has been widely adopted for long-term conversational memory question answering. However, existing methods suffer from two key challenges: (1) fragmented evidence scattered across temporally distant sessions, and (2) noisy content within retrieved sessions that triggers the lost-in-the-middle effect. To address these challenges, we propose MemLoc, a unified Retrieve-Localize-Generate framework for long-term conversational memory QA. For retrieval, MemLoc decomposes each session into multi-granularity memory units and performs query routing via an inner-memory graph with entropy-based granularity selection. It further models cross-session semantic and temporal dependencies through a cross-memory graph, enabling coarse-to-fine retrieval of top-K relevant memory candidates. For localization, we introduce a reasoning-based evidence locator trained with Self-reflective Hint Policy Optimization (SHPO), which performs progressive refinement by extracting query-relevant fragments within memory units to suppress noise and reranking across candidates to remove redundancy, producing a compact evidence set with lightweight location IDs. For generation, these IDs act as precise grounding signals that guide the LLM to the correct memory positions, mitigating the lost-in-the-middle effect while preserving original contextual integrity. Extensive experiments on four benchmarks demonstrate that MemLoc achieves state-of-the-art retrieval accuracy and response quality while maintaining efficiency. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Nikol-coder/MemLoc.

</details>


### [145] [Revisiting Complete Reasoning Traces for Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07103)
*Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Byeongho Heo,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM在SFT后训练中，完整推理轨迹（包含冗余中间步骤）带来的收益有限，而仅使用轨迹端点（起止）训练效果依然不错，甚至更高效。通过注意力分析与token删除实验证明中间token对最终推理质量贡献极小，LLM可凭借内部知识自动补全缺失步骤。该方法同样适用于强化学习与在线蒸馏等后训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法（如SFT）通常使用预收集的完整推理轨迹来提升LLM推理能力。但这些轨迹因复杂交织的推理路径而冗长且含大量旁路/绕行步骤。一个尚未充分探讨的问题是：LLM是否真的需要学习完整轨迹才能获益？作者试图挑战这一常规假设，探索冗余推理token的必要性。

Method: 1) 先进行试点研究（pilot study），比较完整轨迹与不同截断程度的部分轨迹在SFT中的效果；2) 通过基于注意力的分析与受控token删除研究，量化推理轨迹中中间token对最终推理质量的实际贡献；3) 提出"端点训练"方法——仅用推理轨迹的起止（问题与答案）进行训练，让LLM利用内部知识自行推断缺失步骤；4) 进一步将该思路扩展到强化学习和on-policy蒸馏等后训练范式。

Result: 完整推理轨迹带来的益处有限；部分轨迹即使在重度截断下仍然有效；注意力分析和对token删除实验均表明中间步骤对推理质量贡献极小；使用端点训练的LLM在推理行为上产生一致性变化，并在基于强化学习和on-policy蒸馏的后训练方法同样受益。

Conclusion: 当前对完整推理轨迹的依赖可能是不必要的，甚至可能引入冗余信息。建议重新审视完整的推理轨迹在后训练中的作用；让LLM基于端点自行补全推理步骤，是一种更简洁有效且可推广到多种后训练范式的训练策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often post-trained on pre-collected reasoning trajectories to improve their reasoning capability. Such trajectories tend to be long due to complex, interwoven paths, which often include detours on the path toward the answer. However, it has been underexplored whether LLMs indeed benefit from learning complete trajectories in post-training, such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Starting from our pilot study, we find that full trajectories provide only limited benefit, while partial trajectories are effective even under heavy truncation. We analyze redundancy in reasoning trajectories through attention-based analyses and controlled token-removal studies, both of which show that intermediate tokens contribute minimally to final reasoning quality. This suggests that avoiding redundant information may allow LLMs to internally infer coherent alternatives by inferring missing steps from their internal knowledge, given known trajectory endpoints. Furthermore, we show that training LLMs using endpoints leads to consistent changes in reasoning behavior, and that it also benefits post-training methods based on reinforcement learning or on-policy distillation, highlighting the need to revisit complete reasoning traces. Code is available at https://github.com/naver-ai/revisiting-trace.

</details>


### [146] [The Illusion of Debiasing: Persona Steering Redistributes Rather Than Reduces Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07117)
*Ziyue Feng,Hongbo Fang,James A. Evans*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过人格条件设定作为受控探针，沿自我报告、开放生成、词级参数关联三个深度层测量提示干预对语言模型的影响，发现人格提示存在"可读但非结构性"的梯度解离：模型能遵循单一特质指令，却无法复现人类特质间协方差结构，且随层深增加影响递减。提示式引导主要作用于输出通道，存在结构到达极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统提示、人格角色等提示式干预能可靠改变语言模型的输出内容，但其在模型中作用的深度尚不清楚——是真正重构了内部表征结构，还是仅仅调制了输出通道。理解这一点对揭示LLM机制的透明度、可靠性及干预边界具有关键意义。

Method: 以人格条件设定(persona conditioning)作为受控探针，在三个指令微调模型上沿"深度轴"设计三层测量：自我报告层面（观感/主观评判）、开放生成层面（语气与文本风格）、词级参数关联层面（词汇联想基线）。通过比较单特质指令遵循度与跨特质协方差结构复现度，考察提示干预在不同层级的效应强度与结构保持度。

Result: 发现梯度解离现象：模型能遵循单一特质指令（在封闭式问答中有偏置保持或放大），但在开放生成中仅改变绝对语气而组间差异不变，在词级参数关联上几乎不扰动已饱和的联想基线。人格提示在设计上是"可读的"但缺乏人类人格特质间的协变结构，无法在结构性层面复现人类心理测量学上的人格组织。

Conclusion: 提示式引导本质上在输出通道层面起作用，存在结构到达极限——它受限于无法穿透深层表征/参数联想结构，而这种表面可操作性可能掩盖模型在结构性改变能力上的不足。该发现对理解LLM可操控性的深度边界、以及"提示能多大程度改变模型本质行为"的问题提供了分层证据。

Abstract: Prompt-based interventions: system prompts, personas, role instructions, reliably reshape what a language model says, but it is unclear which layer they reach. Do they reconfigure internal structure, or only modulate the output channel? We use persona conditioning as a controlled probe, measuring its effects along a depth axis from self-report, through open-ended generation, to word-level parametric association, across three instruction-tuned models. We find a graded dissociation. Personas are legible but not structural: models follow single-trait instructions yet fail to reproduce human inter-trait covariance. The dissociation deepens with depth: personas hold or amplify closed-form QA bias, shift absolute tone while leaving between-group disparity unchanged, and barely perturb an already saturated associative baseline. Prompt-based steering thus operates in the output channel and has a structural reach limit that surface manipulability can mask.

</details>


### [147] [PTCG: Persona-guided Tree-based Counterargument Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07120)
*Eunbeen Son,Yohan Jo,Joonsuk Park,JinYeong Bak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PTCG框架，结合思维树式逐步生成与剪枝以及说话人角色选择，生成多样且有说服力的反方论点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反驳论点生成方法通常只生成单一论点，无法捕捉真实辩论中所需的多样性和说服力。

Method: PTCG框架包含两个核心组件：一是受思维树（Tree-of-Thoughts）启发的逐步生成与剪枝机制；二是说话人角色（speaker persona）选择，通过从原始论点估算作者角色并融入代表不同视角的说话人角色，实现视角采纳（perspective-taking），从而生成多样化的反方论点。

Result: 通过LLM评判、基于分类器的评估以及人工评估，PTCG在反驳论点的多样性和说服力方面相比基线方法均表现出持续提升。

Conclusion: PTCG验证了结合角色选择与树状逐步生成能够有效增强反方论点生成的多样性与说服力，为批判性思维训练和平衡话语生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The ability to generate counterarguments is important for critical thinking and balanced discourse, yet existing approaches typically produce only a single counterargument, failing to capture the diversity and persuasiveness required in real-world debates. To address this limitation, we propose Persona-guided Tree-based Counterargument Generation (PTCG), a framework that combines Tree-of-Thoughts-inspired step-wise generation and pruning with speaker persona selection. By estimating the author's persona from the original argument and incorporating speaker personas representing distinct perspectives, PTCG operationalizes perspective-taking and enables the generation of diverse counterarguments. Results from LLM-as-a-Judge, classifier-based assessment, and human evaluations indicate that PTCG shows consistent improvements in both the diversity and persuasiveness of counterarguments compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [148] [Line-Coupled Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07129)
*Shiyuan Li,Shaorong Zhang,Zhaorui Yang,Qian Zhang,Greg Ver Steeg,Bingyao Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出线段耦合语言模型（LCLM），一种自回归模型，可同时推进多条文本行生成，每行预测下一个词元并通过共享因果上下文耦合各线。LCLM 将各行词元交错排列为单一因果序列，采用行级错位旋转位置编码，保持标准下一词元目标和因果注意力。实验证明跨行目标比连续同行目标依赖性显著更低，支持行作为并行生成单元。881M参数下，LCLM每次前向传播平均生成2.94个内容词元（验证交叉熵损失2.44），而普通自回归基线仅1.00个词元（损失2.39）。最突出的是，即使LCLM每次前向传播生成16个词元，其损失仅比普通自回归基线高0.09（2.34 vs 2.25）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型每步解码仅生成一个词元，限制了每次前向传播的有效输出。虽然扩散模型、插入式解码和多词元预测支持并行生成，但要么引入额外的训练时词元开销，要么难以预测强依赖的未来词元。本文旨在设计一种既能并行生成多词元、又不改变标准下一词元训练目标的架构，并以"行"作为自然的并行单元来降低跨行目标间的依赖程度。

Method: LCLM通过以下方式实现多行并行生成：(1) 将多条文本行的词元交错拼接到单一因果序列中；(2) 采用行级错位（line-staggered）旋转位置编码，使每行在各自的进度上保持位置信息；(3) 各生成行通过共享的因果上下文彼此耦合；(4) 保持标准的下一个词元预测目标和因果注意力机制不变，从而无需改变训练范式即可实现每次前向传播同时推进多条行。

Result: 控制实验表明跨行目标间的依赖程度显著低于连续同行目标，验证了"行"适合作为并行生成单元。在881M参数规模下，LCLM每次前向传播平均生成2.94个内容词元（验证损失2.44），而普通自回归基线的每前向传播仅1.00个词元（损失2.39）。当LCLM每次前向传播生成16个词元时，其损失为2.34，仅比普通自回归基线（2.25）高0.09。

Conclusion: LCLM验证了以"行"为单位的并行自回归生成是可行且高效的方案：它能显著提升每次前向传播的内容词元产出（约3倍于基线），同时引入的建模代价（损失增加）非常有限，即使在生成16词元/前向传播的极限配置下也仅损失0.09。这为高性能自回归生成提供了不需改变训练目标的新路径。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models generate one token per decoding step, limiting the useful output of each forward pass. Although diffusion models, insertion-based decoding, and multi-token prediction enable parallel generation, they either incur additional training-time token traffic or struggle to predict strongly dependent future tokens. We introduce the Line-Coupled Language Model (LCLM), an autoregressive model that advances multiple text lines together by predicting the next token for every active line while coupling the lines through shared causal context. LCLM interleaves line tokens into a single causal sequence and uses line-staggered rotary positions, retaining the standard next-token objective and causal attention. Controlled experiments show that cross-line targets are substantially less dependent than consecutive same-line targets, supporting lines as parallel generation units. With 881M parameters, LCLM produces an average of 2.94 content tokens per forward pass with a validation cross-entropy loss of 2.44, compared with 1.00 token per forward pass and a loss of 2.39 for the vanilla autoregressive baseline. Most notably, even when LCLM generates 16 tokens per forward pass, its loss is only 0.09 higher than that of the vanilla autoregressive baseline (2.34 vs. 2.25).

</details>


### [149] [Retrieval-Augmented Multi-Prompt Ensemble for Minor-Grain Breeding Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07134)
*Hang Zhao,Jiahao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了RAME（检索增强多提示集成）框架，用于CCL2026-Eval任务5中的小杂粮育种信息抽取（MGBIE），该任务需从文献中联合抽取12种实体和6种关系类型。RAME是一个无需训练的框架，通过混合检索增强的少样本选择、三提示集成（严格/宽松/平衡）和大规模重复采样多数投票来聚合多个LLM输出，在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上实现总分0.499，排名第一，较GPT-5.5官方基线提升11.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小杂粮育种文献中信息抽取面临实体类型多（12种）、关系类型复杂（6种）且标注数据稀缺的挑战，需要一种无需训练即可高效利用大语言模型进行专业领域信息抽取的方法。

Method: RAME框架包含三部分：(i)利用混合BM25-嵌入检索器进行检索增强的少样本选择；(ii)设计Strict、Relaxed、Balanced三种提示构成的集成，覆盖精确率到召回率的谱系；(iii)大规模重复采样结合多数投票过滤噪声预测。

Result: 基于DeepSeek-V4-Flash，RAME在排行榜上取得总分0.499（NER 0.730，RE 0.346），排名第一，相对官方基于GPT-5.5的Track-A基线（0.448）实现了11.4%的相对提升。

Conclusion: RAME通过无需训练的检索增强与多提示多数投票策略，有效提升了LLM在专业育种文献信息抽取上的表现，验证了在标注稀缺场景下利用推理时计算换取性能的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents our system for CCL2026-Eval Task 5: Minor-Grain Breeding Information Extraction (MGBIE), which jointly extracts 12 entity types and 6 relation types from minor-grain breeding literature. We propose RAME (Retrieval-Augmented Multi-Prompt Ensemble), a training-free framework that elicits multiple LLM outputs under controlled diversity and aggregates them by majority voting to obtain high-confidence predictions. RAME combines (i) retrieval-augmented few-shot selection via a hybrid BM25-embedding retriever, (ii) a three-prompt ensemble (Strict, Relaxed, Balanced) spanning the precision to recall spectrum, and (iii) large-scale repeated sampling with majority voting to filter noisy predictions. Built on DeepSeek-V4-Flash, RAME achieves a Total Score of 0.499 (NER 0.730, RE 0.346) on the leaderboard, ranking 1st and surpassing the official Track-A baseline powered by GPT-5.5 (0.448), representing an 11.4% relative improvement. Code is available at https://github.com/king-wang123/CCL26-RAME.

</details>


### [150] [Vishing-Tactics-Bench: Forecasting Exploitation Trajectories in Voice Phishing Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07151)
*Jeongmin Lee,Dongmyung Sul,Seung Yun,Jinxia Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Vishing-Tactics-Bench基准，将语音钓鱼（vishing）防御从事后欺诈分类重构为"危害投射"——基于Endsley情境感知框架，在通话进行中逐轮预测其将走向何种具体危害（信息收集或金融剥削）。作者将MITRE ATT&CK适配为6战术分类体系，标注了5,645通合成中文通话中的35,340条诈骗者话语，并定义了生存分析风格的"剥削轨迹预测"协议及三个评估指标。实验表明战术轨迹可作为通话战术状态的可解释表征，支持特定危害的提前预测与干预选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音钓鱼实时发生，通话结束时再做事后分类已无法阻止伤害。现有工作缺乏在通话进行中判断危险走向的能力，作者希望回答更具可操作性的问题：当前正在进行的通话正战术性地趋向于哪种具体危害（信息收集或金融剥削），从而为实时干预提供依据。

Method: 基于Endsley情境感知框架构建基准，将MITRE ATT&CK适配为Vishing-Tactics（6类战术分类体系），标注35,340条诈骗者话语（5,645通合成中文通话）；定义Exploitation Trajectory Forecasting（生存风格协议），针对两种终端危害使用AP@k、C指数和散度误差三个指标评估；测试从马尔可夫启发式到微调LLM的多种基线。

Result: 实验显示战术轨迹可作为通话战术状态的可解释表征，支持针对具体危害的预测；在严格误报预算下的分层提前时间分析进一步确定了通话中轨迹信号能产生早期预警的关键时间点。

Conclusion: 战术轨迹推理是vishing防御中比事后欺诈分类更可操作的方向，能够在通话实时推进中实现危害预判，从而支持下游的干预选择应用。

Abstract: Voice phishing (vishing) unfolds in real time; by the time a call has ended and post-hoc classification is possible, the harm has already been done. The more actionable question is which concrete harm (Information Gathering or Financial Exploitation) an ongoing call is tactically progressing toward. We present Vishing-Tactics-Bench, a benchmark grounded in Endsley's situation-awareness (SA) framework that recasts vishing defense from after-the-fact fraud classification to harm projection: predicting at each turn whether the call will reach either terminal harm. We adapt MITRE ATT&CK to vishing as a 6-tactic taxonomy (Vishing-Tactics) and label 35,340 scammer utterances across 5,645 synthetic Chinese calls. We define Exploitation Trajectory Forecasting, a survival-style protocol over the two terminal harms with three metrics: AP@k, C-index, and divergence error. Baselines ranging from a Markov heuristic to fine-tuned LLMs show that the tactical trajectory serves as an interpretable representation of the call's tactical state, supporting harm-specific forecasting, which can then be used for the downstream application of intervention selection; a stratified lead-time analysis at a tight false-alarm budget further identifies at what point in a call the trajectory signal yields early warning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Beyond Right and Wrong: Evaluating Second-order Social Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05437)
*Sunny Rai,Jinyi Kuang,Reyhan Jamalova,Annie Lou,Cristina Bicchieri,Niyati Malhotra,Victor Hugo Orozco-Olvera,Ana Maria Munoz-Boudet,Lyle H Ungar,Sharath C Guntuku*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出元规范（metanorm）推理框架，用于评估LLM在规范违反后的二阶社会期望（情感评估与行为反应），并发布多视角数据集NormReact（450个场景）。研究发现当前LLM过度预测负面制裁，高估惩罚而低估容忍与关系校准，可能导致规范敏感领域的认知扭曲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有AI对齐工作集中于一级社会规范（教会模型何为可接受/不可接受行为），但忽略了社会智能的另一关键维度——预判谁会执行规范以及如何执行（即元规范）。这种二阶期望对真实社会互动至关重要，而现有评估体系尚未覆盖。

Method: 提出评估LLM元规范推理的两维度框架：情感评估（emotional appraisal）和行为反应（behavioral response），并设计两个分类任务：预测违规者的自我调节（self-regulation）以及观察者的他人调节（other-regulation）。构建NormReact数据集，包含450个手工标注的规范违反场景，涵盖违规者性别与观察者社会亲近度两个视角维度。

Result: 在六个LLM上测试发现：模型普遍过度预测负面制裁，在人类预期不作为之处预测了惩罚；且随社会距离增加，与人类判断的一致性显著下降。即当前LLM呈现出一个比现实更严苛的社会世界。

Conclusion: AI系统在冲突调解、政策模拟等规范敏感领域的应用中，可能因扭曲的社会监管图景（过度代表惩罚、低估现实生活中真实规范执行中的容忍、克制与关系校准）而产生有害后果，需要专门的元规范推理能力评估与改善。

Abstract: Previous AI alignment efforts have focused primarily on first-order social norms -- teaching models what is socially acceptable or unacceptable (e.g., `do not steal'). However, social intelligence depends not only on norm recognition, but also on anticipating who will enforce it and how (e.g., public shame or even imprisonment). These second-order expectations, known as metanorms, govern how people respond when social rules are broken. We introduce a novel framework for evaluating metanorm reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs) along two dimensions: emotional appraisal and behavioral response, and propose new classification tasks, namely, predicting self-regulation in violators, and other-regulation in observers. We release a multi-perspective dataset, NormReact, of 450 norm violation scenarios, hand-annotated for emotions and behavioral responses across norm violators' gender and observers' social closeness. Current LLMs portray a harsher social world: across six models, they overpredict negative sanctions where humans would expect inaction, and alignment with human judgments deteriorates as social distance increases. These findings suggest that AI systems in norm-sensitive domains from conflict mediation to policy simulation, may risk producing a distorted picture of social regulation: one that over-represents punishment and under-represents the tolerance, restraint, and relational calibration that characterize actual norm enforcement in real world.

</details>


### [152] [CriticGen: Generation-Aware Evaluation as Actionable Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05439)
*Huifang Du,Zecheng Zuo,Sen Wang,Chenghao Fan,Haofen Wang,Yehui Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CriticGen，一个细粒度、生成感知的大语言模型评估框架，将评估转化为可执行的答案改进控制，通过生成实例特定的评估维度与评分标准作为动态评分细则，联合产生分数、理由、可执行改进建议和精炼答案，实现从评估到改进的闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估方法粒度粗糙且与生成过程解耦，产生的评估解释泛化性强，无法为模型改进提供可操作的反馈。

Method: CriticGen首先在高层次类别（如主观、客观和自推导约束）下生成样本特定的评估维度和评分标准，这些标准作为动态评分细则来联合生成分数、理由、可执行的精细化建议以及改进后的答案，通过基于评分细则的条件化精炼过程实现诊断缺陷和针对性改进。

Result: CriticGen生成更高质量的评分细则，将相关度/覆盖率从3.33/4.03提升至3.97/4.24；取得最佳分数相关性（Pearson 0.9556，Spearman 0.9560）；将基于标准锚定的理由和可执行建议的F1从0.6369/0.5994提升至0.7554/0.7900；其反馈可改善73.17%的答案，非退化率达93.28%。

Conclusion: 细粒度评估应兼具实例特定性和可操作性，CriticGen通过生成感知的评分细则条件化评估框架，实现了评估到改进的有效转化，验证了生成感知评估的价值。

Abstract: Current evaluation methods for large language models are coarse-grained and decoupled from generation, producing generic explanations that fail to provide actionable feedback for model improvement. We propose CriticGen, a fine-grained, generation-aware evaluation framework that turns evaluation into actionable control for answer improvement. CriticGen first generates sample-specific evaluation dimensions and scoring criteria under high-level categories such as subjective, objective, and self-derived constraints. These criteria then serve as a dynamic rubric for jointly producing a score, a reason, an executable refinement suggestion, and a refined answer. This rubric-conditioned refinement process enables models to diagnose flaws and perform targeted answer improvement. Experimental results show that fine-grained evaluation should be both instance-specific and actionable. CriticGen induces higher-quality rubrics, improving relevance/coverage from 3.33/4.03 to 3.97/4.24. CriticGen also achieves the best score correlations, with 0.9556 Pearson and 0.9560 Spearman, and raises the F1 of criterion-grounded reasons and executable suggestions from 0.6369/0.5994 to 0.7554/0.7900. Crucially, its feedback translates into reliable answer improvement, improving 73.17% of answers with a 93.28% non-degradation rate.

</details>


### [153] [When Does Memory Help? A Cost-Aware Evaluation of Long-Term Memory in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05441)
*Shweta Mishra,Shashank Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MERIT，一个用于评估工具使用型LLM代理长期记忆边际效用的基准与实验框架。该基准包含跨三个领域的记忆依赖型工具任务、自动泄漏检查、难度阶梯（含事实更新回想）、受控记忆污染及完整的token/美元计量。在23,440个评分片段（$42.57）上，记忆将依赖任务成功率从泄漏验证的0.00底板提升至0.55-1.00。关键发现：嵌入检索在更新事实场景下表现不稳定，行动基于检索值仅55%时间；结构化事实存储与LLM摘要等写入时更新存储表现更稳（0.70-1.00）；更换记忆实现可移动任务成功率最多60个点，且完整重放从不经济。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆评估（如LoCoMo、LongMemEval）仅基于对话回话基准衡量问答能力，未评估记忆事实是否真正改变工具使用代理的行为。因此需要一个在显式成本核算下衡量记忆对任务执行代理边际效用的评测体系。

Method: MERIT包含三个领域的情景化工具任务，其对先前片段事实的依赖性通过自动化泄漏检查验证；设有以更新事实回想结尾的难度阶梯；引入受控记忆污染；对每次记忆操作进行完整token与美元计量。通过两代试点（gpt-4.1-mini）及预注册的3模型×3种子网格实验（GPT-4.1, Claude Haiku 4.5, 记忆端固定）进行评测，并基于Claude Sonnet 5做最新一代抽查（以干净全重放对照为门控条件），比较嵌入检索、结构化事实存储、LLM摘要及混合存储等记忆实现。

Result: 记忆将依赖任务成功率从0.00底板提升至0.55-1.00。在更新事实上，嵌入检索跨模型不可预测地崩溃（0.30-0.95，最大种子差距0.45），且代理对已正确检索的值仅55%时间采取行动；写入时更新存储（结构化事实存储、LLM摘要）维持0.70-1.00，混合方案反而比单一事实存储更差。最新一代抽查复现该模式。更换记忆实现可移动成功率最多60点，完整重放从不经济：最佳条件每美元交付2.7-3.9倍边际效用。总成本$42.57，覆盖23,440个评分片段。

Conclusion: 真实任务执行中记忆的价值必须按边际效用与成本计量，而非仅按问答准确率。嵌入检索对事实更新的脆弱性及其行动采纳率的低下，凸显了写入时更新结构化存储的优势；记忆实现的选择可显著（高达60点）影响任务成功；完整重放在经济上恒不可行。综合结果表明，构建可靠工具代理长期记忆应优先考虑结构化写入时更新存储，并以成本核算的边际效用作为核心评测指标。

Abstract: Long-term memory for LLM agents is evaluated today by conversational recall benchmarks (LoCoMo, LongMemEval), which measure question answering over dialogue history, not whether remembered facts change what a tool-using agent does. We present MERIT (Memory Evaluation for Realistic Instrumented Tasks), a benchmark and harness that measures the marginal utility of memory for task-executing agents under explicit cost accounting. MERIT provides episodic tool-use tasks in three domains whose dependence on earlier-episode facts is verified by an automated leak check; a difficulty ladder ending in updated-fact recall; controlled memory corruption; and full token and dollar metering of every memory operation. Across 23,440 scored episodes ($42.57), a two-generation pilot on gpt-4.1-mini and a preregistered 3-model x 3-seed grid (GPT-4.1, Claude Haiku 4.5; memory side held fixed), memory lifts dependent-task success from a leak-verified floor of 0.00 to 0.55-1.00. On updated facts, embedding retrieval collapses unpredictably (0.30-0.95 across models; max seed gap 0.45), and agents act on a correctly retrieved value only 55% of the time, while update-on-write stores (a structured fact store and, notably, LLM summarization) remain at 0.70-1.00; the hybrid is worse than the fact store alone. A latest-generation spot-check (Claude Sonnet 5, gated on a clean full-replay control) reproduces the pattern. Swapping a memory's implementation moves task success by up to 60 points, and full replay is never economical: the best condition per domain delivers 2.7-3.9x its marginal utility per dollar. We release the benchmark, harness, and all traces.

</details>


### [154] [AutoFyn Technical Report: Non-Parametric Expert Iteration for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05446)
*Adib Hasan,Daniel Schaffield,Akashnil Dutta,Tarik Adnan Moon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍AutoFyn，一种受Expert Iteration算法启发的智能体框架，通过更新持久状态（而非模型权重）使冻结模型在多轮迭代中自我改进。每轮从全新模型会话开始，仅通过持久记忆文件、报告和仓库状态等显式接口引入持久信息。轮内由编排器协调多个专业智能体探索、规划与构建多种方案，任务级验证器提供客观奖励，并将奖励蒸馏回持久状态以更新下一轮的有效策略。在2026年国际数学奥林匹克6道新题、Spider 2.0 dbt基准测试以及Next.js等开源项目漏洞挖掘中均展现出优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上提升模型能力依赖权重更新（如微调），但对于冻结或不可训练模型，需要一种不修改权重即可迭代改进的策略。AutoFyn旨在通过外部持久状态来适应和学习，使固定模型在多轮循环中持续优化表现。

Method: AutoFyn借鉴Expert Iteration算法，构建"探索构建-验证奖励-蒸馏回持久状态"的闭环。每轮从全新模型会话开始，编排器并行探索多种方案，多个专业智能体协作规划与实现；任务级验证器基于客观标准验证结果并给出奖励，奖励被蒸馏到持久状态（如记忆文件、报告、仓库状态），从而在下一轮更新有效策略。

Result: 在2026年IMO的6道全新题目中，AutoFyn使所有有改进空间模型的得分均高于其厂商自己的编码智能体；在Spider 2.0 dbt基准上构建了排名第一的智能体；并在Next.js、MetaMask、pnpm、Warp、LiteLLM、Langflow和Open WebUI中发现16个经维护者确认的漏洞公告。

Conclusion: AutoFyn证明了通过持久状态而非权重更新即可实现冻结模型的持续自我改进，在数学推理、数据科学和网络安全三个领域均验证了该框架的有效性，为无法访问权重的模型提供了通用的能力增强范式。

Abstract: We introduce AutoFyn, an agent harness inspired by the Expert Iteration algorithm, adapting a frozen model across many rounds by updating persistent state from verified reward signals rather than model weights. Each round begins from a fresh model session, and durable information is reintroduced only through explicit interfaces such as persistent memory files, reports, and repository state. Within a round, an orchestrator explores, plans and builds many alternative approaches with specialized agents, while a task-grounded verifier verifies the work and supplies an objective reward for measuring progress. This reward is distilled back into the persistent state, which updates the effective policy for the next round. In this technical report, we formalize this loop and describe its persistent state and verification interfaces. We then demonstrate its use in three domains, namely olympiad mathematics, data science, and cybersecurity. On the six fresh problems of the 2026 International Mathematical Olympiad, every model with room to improve scores higher under AutoFyn than in its provider's own coding agent. AutoFyn also built the top-ranked agent on the Spider 2.0 dbt benchmark, and has produced $16$ maintainer-confirmed vulnerability advisories in Next.js, MetaMask, pnpm, Warp, LiteLLM, Langflow, and Open WebUI.

</details>


### [155] [Damage-Aware Bandit Pruning for Vision and Language Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05448)
*Salem Ameen,Sunil Vadera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将语言和视觉Transformer的结构化后训练剪枝问题建模为损伤感知的多臂老虎机问题，在固定候选评估预算下依次选择注意力头和MLP通道组进行抑制。配对损伤度量（掩码损失减基线损失）降低了批次间方差，UCB和分数Beta汤普森采样策略在GPT-2、OPT、Pythia、Qwen2.5、SmolLM2、ViT-B/16、DeiT-Tiny和Swin-Tiny上优于随机、幅度、静态显著性和预算贪心基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化后训练剪枝需要选择抑制后仍保持低性能退化的完整功能单元，但如何在固定评估预算内高效选取这些单元是一个挑战。现有贪心等方法可能不是最优的，需要一种预算受限条件下兼顾损伤估计不确定性的单元选择框架。

Method: 将结构化单元选择建模为有界奖励的损伤感知多臂老虎机问题（damage-aware MAB）。在标定批次上临时掩码注意力头和MLP通道组，以掩码损失减同批次基线损失的配对损伤作为反馈来减少批次间方差。采用平滑有界奖励驱动UCB风格策略或分数贝塔汤普森采样，逐步贪心地依次添加一个单元构建最终掩码。

Result: 在WikiText-2、LAMBADA和Imagenette上的实验覆盖GPT-2、OPT、Pythia、Qwen2.5、SmolLM2、ViT-B/16、DeiT-Tiny和Swin-Tiny。5个种子下bandit方法通常优于预算贪心。28个对比中23个bootstrap置信区间排除零，11个配对检验p<0.05，BH校正后6个q<0.05。ViT-B/16和Swin-Tiny的匹配评估表明收益不单由更大评估预算解释。

Conclusion: 基于老虎机的结构化剪枝选择方法在固定预算下比预算贪心等多类基线更有效地降低了结构化剪枝造成的性能退化。值得注意的是，该方法执行的是功能单元的结构性抑制而非物理压缩，且所有报告的效果均为有效结构抑制的度量。

Abstract: Structured post-training pruning of transformers requires selecting complete functional units whose suppression causes limited degradation. We formulate structured-unit selection for language and vision transformers as a damage-aware multi-armed bandit problem under a fixed candidate-evaluation budget. Attention heads and MLP channel groups are temporarily masked on calibration batches. Paired damage is the masked loss minus the base loss on the same batch, reducing batch-to-batch variation. A smooth bounded reward drives either a UCB-style policy or fractional-Beta Thompson Sampling, and the final mask is constructed sequentially by adding one unit at each step. The selected units are functionally zeroed in the original dense checkpoint; therefore, the reported parameter effects represent effective structural suppression rather than physical compression or measured speedup. Experiments on WikiText-2, LAMBADA, and Imagenette cover GPT-2, OPT, Pythia, Qwen2.5, SmolLM2, ViT-B/16, DeiT-Tiny, and Swin-Tiny, with comparisons against random, magnitude, static-saliency, and budgeted-greedy selection. Across five seeds, the bandit methods usually reduce degradation relative to budgeted greedy in the paired language-model comparisons. Of 28 comparisons highlighted in the paper, 23 bootstrap confidence intervals exclude zero and 11 paired tests have p < 0.05; six have q < 0.05 after Benjamini-Hochberg correction across the full family of 116 dataset-wise tests. Matched-evaluation results for ViT-B/16 and Swin-Tiny indicate that their gains are not explained solely by a larger candidate-evaluation budget.

</details>


### [156] [Compiling VGDL into Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05459)
*Mohit Jiwatode,Bodo Rosenhahn,Alexander Dockhorn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个确定性框架，将游戏描述语言（VGDL）中的游戏直接编译为动态结构因果模型（DSCM），通过显式结构方程映射精灵动态、交互规则和终止条件，确保因果保真度，支持反事实推理、因果强化学习和程序化内容验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体依赖虚假相关性，大语言模型容易幻觉游戏规则，且当前缺乏将复杂游戏机制形式化映射到因果模型的成熟方法，导致因果强化学习难以在实践中落地。

Method: 将VGDL描述的游戏组件（精灵动态、交互规则、终止条件）直接翻译为显式结构方程，每个游戏tick表示从t时刻到t+1时刻状态变量间的因果转移，从而构建动态结构因果模型（DSCM），而非从游戏轨迹或LLM输出中推断因果结构。

Result: 该框架保证对ground-truth游戏机制的绝对因果保真度，生成的模型提供透明的因果路径，支持反事实推理、因果强化学习智能体训练以及程序化内容验证。

Conclusion: 该框架在符号游戏描述与因果驱动的游戏AI之间建立了原则性桥梁，为游戏环境中的可解释AI与因果推理提供了正式方法论基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning and large language models often struggle to accurately capture the causal mechanics of game environments. Standard reinforcement learning agents tend to rely on spurious correlations, while large language models are prone to hallucinating game rules. Although causal reinforcement learning improves interpretability, there is currently no formal methodology to map complex game mechanics directly into causal models. To address this, we propose a deterministic framework that compiles games specified in the Video Game Description Language into Dynamic Structural Causal Models. Rather than inferring causal structures from gameplay traces or noisy large language models' outputs, our methodology directly translates game components, including sprite dynamics, interaction rules, and termination conditions, into explicit structural equations. Each game tick represents a causal transition from state variables at time $t$ to $t+1$. By establishing this grounded mapping, the approach guarantees absolute causal fidelity to the ground-truth game mechanics. The resulting models offer transparent causal pathways that support counterfactual reasoning, causal reinforcement learning agent training, and procedural content validation. This framework provides a principled bridge between symbolic game descriptions and causally grounded game AI.

</details>


### [157] [ARC-Bench: Closed-Loop Replanning Masks Broken Action Ranking in Frozen JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05461)
*Zhengshu Zhang,Zhiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对"冻结潜在空间中的距离可用于动作排序"这一潜在世界模型核心假设进行直接审计，提出ARC-Bench协议，发现官方JEPA-WM检查点在操作和导航域中均严重且结构性违反该假设，并揭示闭环重规划掩盖了这一缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于奖励的自由潜在世界模型（如JEPA-WM）假设候选动作可按潜在空间距离正确排序（即潜在距离序与真实代价序一致），但该假设从未被直接验证。因此作者希望审计"冻结JEPA目标函数是否能正确对候选动作排序"这一核心前提。

Method: 提出ARC-Bench基准：一个无泄漏、固定候选的动作排序协议，用于测量冻结的JEPA风格目标函数是否正确排序候选动作。应用到官方发布的JEPA-WM检查点（涵盖导航与操作类控制任务），并做受控视觉主干消融实验（DINOv2替换为V-JEPA 1/V-JEPA 2，ViT-L/ViT-G尺度），同时引入重规划频率消融实验揭示掩盖机制。

Result: 假设严重且结构性失败：官方操作审计中得分最高的候选几乎总是次优的，迷宫域同样出现排名反转。将DINOv2换成视频预训练的V-JEPA编码器后缺陷仍存在。通过来源、欠训练、匹配预算主干控制与度量循环控制排除了平凡解释。降低重规划频率后，导航与操作域的成功率均崩溃，被高频率重规划挽救的情节与PointMaze首次规划诊断中的严重排序失败富集。

Conclusion: 闭环重规划掩盖了潜在距离排序缺陷，闭环成功率系统性高估了冻结潜在表示的"可排序性"。ARC-Bench提供了测量手段，掩盖机制提供了解释，提醒所有围绕潜在空间规划器进行自适应、摊销或重规划的方法需直接审计JEPA-WM动作可排序性。

Abstract: Reward-free latent world models plan by scoring candidate actions with distances in a frozen latent space: an action is preferred if its predicted future embedding lands closer to the goal embedding. This silently assumes that latent closeness is action-rankable, i.e., that ordering candidates by latent distance agrees with ordering them by true cost. We audit this assumption directly. We introduce ARC-Bench, a no-leak, fixed-candidate protocol that measures whether frozen JEPA-style objectives rank candidate actions correctly, and apply it to official released JEPA-WM checkpoints across navigation and manipulation-style control. The assumption fails, severely and structurally: on the official manipulation audits the top-scored candidate is almost always suboptimal, and the same inversion appears in the maze domains. A controlled visual-backbone extension shows that the defect persists when DINOv2 is replaced by video-pretrained V-JEPA 1 and V-JEPA 2 encoders at ViT-L/ViT-G scale. Provenance, undertraining, matched-budget backbone controls, and metric-circularity controls rule out trivial explanations. We then explain why this defect has stayed invisible: closed-loop replanning masks it. When we reduce the planner's replanning frequency, success collapses in both a navigation and a manipulation domain, and the episodes rescued by frequent replanning are enriched for severe first-plan ranking failures in the PointMaze first-plan diagnostic. Closed-loop success rates therefore systematically overstate the rankability of frozen latent representations. ARC-Bench supplies the measurement, and the masking mechanism the explanation, for methods that adapt, amortize, or replan around latent-space planners without directly auditing released JEPA-WM action rankability.

</details>


### [158] [RAPID: Reliability-Aware Pair Importance Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05481)
*Ali Mahdavi,Azadeh Zamanifar,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Omid Kashefi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RAPID（Reliability Aware Pair Importance Distillation），一种用于实例间关系蒸馏的可靠性与对重要性感知的蒸馏方法。它将可靠性门控的关系目标与自适应支持对提议分离，并通过精确逆提议校正保证损失和梯度估计器的条件无偏性。在两个文本分类任务（AG News和SST-2）上的实验表明，可靠性门控的关系蒸馏取得了最高的平均学生准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实例间关系蒸馏需要在mini batch中匹配教师表示中示例间的关系，但计算所有配对具有二次复杂度，而均匀子采样可能低效地使用有限的关系预算。作者希望解决如何在有限的关系预算下更有效地选择与加权配对关系的问题。

Method: RAPID将设计分为两个可分离的维度：(1) 可靠性门控的关系目标（Reliability gated relational target），通过校准的教师熵决定哪些教师关系应被强调；(2) 全支持自适应对提议（full support adaptive pair proposal），利用分离的学生-教师残差决定评估哪些关系，并通过精确的逆提议校正使损失与梯度估计器相对于门控mini batch目标条件无偏。

Result: 在AG News上（BERT到DistilBERT蒸馏，关系预算256）：可靠性门控关系蒸馏取得最高平均学生准确率94.285±0.054%，RAPID为94.241±0.025%；在SST-2上（DistilBERT到DistilBERT蒸馏，关系预算64）：可靠性门控关系蒸馏取得88.800±0.532%，RAPID为88.685±0.462%，均优于cross entropy基线（94.154±0.124%和87.271±0.162%）。

Conclusion: 结果表明目标可靠性与评估优先级是可分离的设计维度。门控目标在两个设置中均取得最高平均准确率，而自适应提议的表现处于种子级变异范围内。这支持了将目标可靠性和对选择优先级分离看待的模块化观点。

Abstract: Inter example relational distillation transfers a teacher's representation geometry by matching relations among examples within a mini batch. Computing all pairs has quadratic complexity in the batch size, whereas uniform subsampling may use a limited relation budget inefficiently. We introduce Reliability Aware Pair Importance Distillation, or RAPID, which separates a reliability gated relational target from a full support adaptive pair proposal. Reliability determines which teacher relations are emphasized, while calibrated teacher entropy and detached student-teacher residuals determine which relations are evaluated. Exact inverse proposal correction makes the loss and gradient estimators conditionally unbiased with respect to the gated mini batch target. We evaluate RAPID in two text classification settings: AG News with BERT-to-DistilBERT distillation using three paired seeds and a relation budget of 256, and SST-2 with DistilBERT to DistilBERT distillation using three paired seeds and a relation budget of 64. Reliability gated relational distillation achieves the highest observed mean student accuracy on both datasets: 94.285 plus or minus 0.054 percent on AG News and 88.800 plus or minus 0.532 percent on SST-2. RAPID ranks second, achieving 94.241 plus or minus 0.025 percent and 88.685 plus or minus 0.462 percent, respectively, compared with 94.154 plus or minus 0.124 percent and 87.271 plus or minus 0.162 percent for the cross entropy baseline. Pilot evaluations are counted toward the same total budget as the main relation evaluations. Across both settings, the gated target yields the highest mean accuracy, while the adaptive proposal remains within seed-level variation. These results support the modular view that target reliability and evaluation priority are separable design dimensions.

</details>


### [159] [PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN: Prior-Guided Joint Probabilistic Forecasting of Clinical Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05488)
*Weizhi Nie,Rihao Chang,Weijie Wang,Yuting Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN，一种以轨迹为先的联合概率预测框架，用于多变量生理时间序列预测。在MIMIC-IV衍生队列（6,882名患者、54,694个窗口）上评估24小时历史和6小时预报任务，在13个模型中取得第二低的归一化MAE（0.37727）和最低的RMSE（0.52656）。但它同时揭示了一个关键洞察：准确的轨迹预测本身并不足以保证校准的事件检测器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床恶化并非由单一诊断标签刻画，而是通过耦合的、部分可观测的轨迹逐步显现。现有方法往往聚焦于端点分类任务，缺乏对多变量生理学联合概率分布的建模能力，难以捕捉变量间动态关联与缺失机制。

Method: 框架包含五个核心组件：缺失感知的时序编码器（处理部分可观测数据）、软器官系统先验（利用领域知识注入）、患者特异的变量间关系建模、非线性Kolmogorov-Arnold消息传递机制，以及低秩多元Student-t分布输出头以实现联合概率预测。实验基于冻结的MIMIC-IV队列，使用24小时历史窗口预测6小时后的生理状态。

Result: 在5个随机种子、13个模型的广泛对比中，PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN获得第二低的归一化MAE（0.37727±0.00029）和最低的RMSE（0.52656±0.00034），相比确定性TimeKAN将MAE降低0.52%。概率预测方面达到边际NLL 0.66380和CRPS 0.27301，经验覆盖率分别为0.533、0.831、0.958（对应名义50%、80%、95%区间）。消融实验显示移除关系结构是最大的性能损失来源；增大协方差秩提升联合似然而对点精度影响甚微。衍生风险评分的AUROC（0.603）弱于专用GRU-D分类器（0.650）。

Conclusion: 联合轨迹预测为临床洞察提供了可检查的中间任务，但精确的生理预测本身不足以自动产生校准的事件检测器。揭示轨迹预测与事件检测之间的能力差距，呼吁未来工作需更谨慎地弥合预测精度与临床决策效用之间的距离。

Abstract: Clinical deterioration unfolds through coupled, partially observed trajectories, not a single diagnostic label. We introduce PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN, a trajectory-first framework for joint probabilistic forecasting of multivariate physiology. It combines missingness-aware temporal encoders, a soft organ-system prior, patient-specific relations, nonlinear Kolmogorov-Arnold messages, and a low-rank multivariate Student-t head. We evaluate 24-hour histories and six-hour forecasts on a frozen MIMIC-IV-derived cohort of 6,882 patients and 54,694 windows. Across five seeds and 13 models, PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN obtains the second-lowest normalized MAE (0.37727 +/- 0.00029) and the lowest RMSE (0.52656 +/- 0.00034). It reduces MAE by 0.52% relative to deterministic TimeKAN. For probabilistic forecasting, it reaches a marginal NLL of 0.66380 and a CRPS of 0.27301. Empirical coverage is 0.533, 0.831, and 0.958 for nominal 50%, 80%, and 95% intervals. Removing relational structure causes the largest ablation loss. Increasing covariance rank improves joint likelihood but has little effect on point accuracy. A trajectory-derived risk score remains weaker than a dedicated GRU-D classifier (AUROC 0.603 versus 0.650), which limits the present clinical claim. Joint trajectory forecasting therefore provides an inspectable intermediate task, but accurate physiology forecasts alone do not ensure a calibrated event detector.

</details>


### [160] [SciLitBench: Benchmark and Design Principles for LLM-Powered Systematic Literature Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05505)
*Miguel Zabaleta,Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SciLitBench，一个覆盖标题/摘要筛选、全文筛选和模式引导数据提取的多阶段系统综述基准，包含42,981条检索记录、1,012篇全文及888篇纳入论文的标注。对22个开源LLM的评估表明：显式纳入/排除标准使标题摘要筛选F2提升28.8%，研究者撰写理由使全文筛选提升15%；数据提取可靠性存在显著分化，最强的模型仅能恢复30%的评估证据和25%的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统综述需要在数千条记录上进行持续的人类判断，但现有LLM评估通常孤立地考察综述的各个阶段，缺乏一个能够统一评估多阶段性能的基准资源。

Method: 构建SciLitBench多阶段基准，涵盖标题与摘要筛选、全文筛选和模式引导的数据提取三个环节，在六个模型家族的22个开源LLM上系统评估，分别测试显式纳入/排除标准、研究者撰写的理由对筛选性能的影响，并测评不同维度数据提取的可靠性。

Result: 显式纳入和排除标准将标题/摘要筛选的F2提高28.8%；研究者撰写的理由将全文筛选性能提升15%。数据提取呈现不同可靠性模式：准确率从出版年份的0.97降至计算方法上的Jaccard重叠仅0.37，最强模型仅恢复30%的标注评估证据和25%的局限性。

Conclusion: SciLitBench厘清了高召回率筛选与证据完整提取之间的实践边界，为评估LLM辅助的证据综合提供可复现的资源，揭示了当前模型在完整提取证据方面的明显不足。

Abstract: Systematic reviews require sustained human judgment across thousands of records, yet existing evaluations of large language models (LLMs) typically examine review stages in isolation. We introduce SciLitBench, a multi-stage benchmark spanning title and abstract screening, full-text screening, and schema-guided data extraction, with 42,981 retrieved records, 1,012 full texts, and annotations for 888 included papers. Across 22 open-weight LLMs from six model families, explicit inclusion and exclusion criteria improve title and abstract screening $F_2$ by 28.8\%, while researcher-authored rationales improve full-text screening by 15\%. Data extraction reveals a different reliability regime: performance declines from 0.97 accuracy for publication year to 0.37 Jaccard overlap for computational approach, while the strongest models recover only 30\% of annotated evaluation evidence and 25\% of limitations. SciLitBench identifies a practical boundary between high-recall screening and evidence-complete extraction and provides a reproducible resource for evaluating LLM-assisted evidence synthesis.

</details>


### [161] [SCAFFOLD: Self-Improving Web Agents via Recursive Parametric Skill Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05511)
*Bowei He,Xiaokun Zhang,Meng Ding,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Scaffold，一个用于视觉Web代理的自改进框架。它从成功轨迹中归纳参数化可执行技能，在多层实例抽象约束下构建递归组合的技能层级，通过最小描述长度（MDL）准则和行为等价性检查压缩技能库冗余，并定期将技能增强轨迹蒸馏回模型权重以内部化抽象。在WebArena、VisualWebArena和Online-Mind2Web分割上，相较最强技能增强基线提升11.1-17.2个绝对成功率点，且在五次自改进迭代中呈现单调增益而无库崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web代理大多孤立地学习每个任务并丢弃累积的程序性知识。虽已有技能增强框架尝试解决此问题，但它们将技能库视为扁平或两层的提示端缓存，缺乏压缩冗余或递归组合技能的机制，限制了代理在视觉丰富、跨站点长视野界面上的泛化与持续改进能力。

Method: Scaffold框架包含四个核心组件：(i) 在多层实例抽象约束下，从成功轨迹归纳参数化、可执行技能；(ii) 维护递归组合的技能层级，高层技能调用低层技能；(iii) 使用最小描述长度准则与行为等价性检查对技能库进行紧凑化处理；(iv) 周期性将技能增强轨迹蒸馏回模型权重，内化所习得抽象。

Result: 在WebArena、VisualWebArena及Online-Mind2Web的保留分割上，Scaffold相较最强技能增强基线在成功率上提升11.1至17.2个绝对百分点，并在五次自改进迭代中呈现单调增益，未出现技能库崩溃现象。

Conclusion: Scaffold验证了通过参数化技能递归组合、MDL驱动压缩及权重蒸馏内化的闭环自改进范式，能够有效应对视觉Web代理长期任务中的知识积累与泛化挑战，且具备持续提升的稳定性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Web agents need to navigate visually rich, long-horizon interfaces that change across sites, yet most previous agents still learn each task in isolation and discard the procedural knowledge they accumulate. Recent skill-augmented frameworks take an important first step, but they treat the skill library as a flat or two-tier prompt-side cache and offer no principled mechanism for compressing redundancy or composing skills recursively. We introduce \textsc{Scaffold}, a self-improving framework for visual web agents that (i) induces parametric, executable skills from successful trajectories under a multi-instance abstraction constraint, (ii) maintains a recursively composed hierarchy in which higher-level skills invoke lower-level ones, (iii) compacts the library via a minimum-description-length (MDL) criterion and behavioral equivalence checking, and (iv) periodically distills skill-augmented trajectories back into model weights to internalize the abstractions. Across WebArena, VisualWebArena, and a held-out split of Online-Mind2Web, \textsc{Scaffold} improves success rate by $11.1$--$17.2$ absolute points over the strongest skill-augmented baseline and shows monotonic gains across five self-improvement iterations without library collapse. We release the code and documents in the Github \href{https://github.com/BokwaiHo/SCAFFOLD}{repository}.

</details>


### [162] [Reasoning-Aware Compression: Identifying and Protecting Vulnerable Reasoning Circuits for Energy-Efficient LLM Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05512)
*Leonard Twagirayezu,Prasenjit Mitra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种推理感知压缩框架，颠覆了传统对所有组件采用均匀量化的压缩方法。该框架在五个推理基准上对量化条件进行基准测试，并通过扰动扫描识别每模块的INT4脆弱性，然后选择性将最敏感电路恢复为FP16精度。关键发现包括：INT4量化可能因延长推理链反而增加能耗；组件脆弱性具有任务依赖性；选择性压缩能够实现均匀量化方法无法达到的Pareto最优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型在部署时产生巨大能耗，而现有压缩方法对所有组件统一量化，缺乏对推理关键回路的感知，可能严重损害模型的推理能力。研究者希望通过识别脆弱的推理电路并有针对性地进行精度恢复，在不损失推理质量的前提下实现能耗优化。

Method: 该框架首先在GSM8K、FOLIO、MATH-500、ProofWriter和MuSiQue五个推理基准上，结合GPU级硬件能耗测量对量化条件进行评估；随后通过扰动扫描方法，在所有196-224个（层，投影）组合上绘制每个模块的INT4脆弱性图谱，并在保留的校准分割集上定位敏感模块，最后选择性地将这些最敏感的回路恢复至FP16精度。

Result: 研究发现三个关键结果：其一，INT4量化可通过延长推理链增加能耗，25%的功率降低在GSM8K上反而转化为净能耗增加；其二，模块脆弱性具有任务依赖性——注意力投影对数学推理更为关键，且敏感性模式在逻辑推理中因架构不同而有所差异；其三，选择性压缩实现了均匀方法无法企及的Pareto最优点，R1-Qwen-7B在ProofWriter上Top-10%恢复较FP16获得+12个百分点准确率提升，同时能耗降低9.7%，并在五个基准的保留数据上得到验证。

Conclusion: 均匀量化在推理模型压缩中存在根本性局限，推理感知的、任务相关的选择性压缩是更优策略。该方法不仅在能耗与推理质量间实现了更好的权衡，也揭示了不同推理任务和架构下量化脆弱性的结构化差异，为推理模型的绿色部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) impose substantial energy costs during deployment, yet current compression methods apply uniform quantization across all components, risking damage to critical reasoning circuits. We present a reasoning-aware compression framework that benchmarks quantization conditions across five reasoning benchmarks, GSM8K, FOLIO, MATH-500, ProofWriter, and MuSiQue, with hardware-level GPU energy measurement; profiles per-module INT4 vulnerability across all 196-224 (layer, projection) pairs via a perturbation sweep on a held-out calibration split, then selectively restores the most sensitive circuits to FP16. Three findings emerge. First, INT4 quantization can increase energy by extending reasoning chains; a 25% power reduction becomes a net energy increase on GSM8K. Second, vulnerability is task-dependent: attention projections are more critical for mathematical reasoning, and sensitivity patterns differ by architecture in logical inference. Third, selective compression achieves Pareto-optimal points inaccessible to uniform methods: R1-Qwen-7B Top-10% on ProofWriter gains +12 pp over FP16 at -9.7% energy, validated on held-out data across five reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [163] [When and What to Teach: Budget-Aware Online Adaptation for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05513)
*Jianwei Zhang,Sihan Cao,Pengcheng Zheng,Ya Wen,Pei Ke,Kuien Liu,Shen Gao,Wei Dong,Yang Yang,Chaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"分数引导的在线教学与预算化轨迹修剪"（Score-Guided Online Teaching with Budgeted Trajectory Trimming）的预算感知框架，用于解决Web智能体在真实环境中部署后需要持续在线适应的问题。该框架通过可解性感知的教师门控机制和分数引导的回合选择机制，分别决定何时查询教师模型以及保留哪些有价值的训练回合，在MiniWoB和TimeWarp数据集上实现了与基准相当的首次成功率的同事，平均减少22.6%的教师调用次数和52.1%的学生训练计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web智能体在真实世界部署中需要持续在线适应，但调用强大的专有教师模型在商业上成本过高，因此需依赖轻量级本地学生模型并通过在线教学从教师处学习。然而，标准的交互式反馈（轨迹级偏好优化）成本高昂，将预算浪费在不可解决的回合和冗余执行回合上，亟需一种预算感知的方法来提高在线教学的效率。

Method: 提出Score-Guided Online Teaching with Budgeted Trajectory Trimming框架，系统性地协调"何时教"与"教什么"两个维度：(1) 通过可解性感知的教师门控（solvability-aware teacher gate）决定何时查询教师模型，避免在不可解决的任务上浪费预算；(2) 通过分数引导的回合选择机制（score-guided turn selection）保留信息量高的训练回合，剔除冗余的执行回合。整个框架以预算为导向，在有限资源下进行有取舍的教学。

Result: 在MiniWoB和TimeWarp基准上的广泛实验表明，该方法在保持与对比方法相当的首次成功率（first-pass success）的同时，平均减少22.6%的教师模型调用次数以及52.1%的学生训练计算开销。

Conclusion: 本文验证了"有选择地教学"比"全量教学"更高效：通过识别任务可解性来选择教学时机，并通过信息量评分来挑选有价值的训练轨迹，能够在几乎不影响学生模型性能的前提下大幅降低教师调用与训练开销，为真实世界中经济可行的Web智能体在线部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Web agents have achieved significant success in automating complex internet tasks but deploying them in real-world environments requires continuous online adaptation. Given that deploying powerful proprietary models remains commercially cost-prohibitive, practitioners must rely on lightweight local models that evolve post-deployment via online teaching from a stronger teacher. However, standard interactive feedback imposes prohibitive costs. We show that conventional trajectory-level preference optimization wastes budget on both unresolvable episodes and redundant execution turns. To resolve these inefficiencies, we propose \textbf{Score-Guided Online Teaching with Budgeted Trajectory Trimming}, a budget-aware framework that systematically orchestrates \textbf{when} and \textbf{what} to teach. Specifically, our framework integrates a solvability-aware teacher gate to dictate \textbf{when} to query the teacher model and a score-guided turn selection mechanism to decide \textbf{what} informative turns to retain. Extensive experiments on MiniWoB and TimeWarp demonstrate that our method achieves comparable first-pass success while reducing teacher calls by 22.6\% and student training compute by 52.1\% on average. Our code is available at https://github.com/zjw131f1fc/budgeted-online-teaching.

</details>


### [164] [The Failure Happens Before the Drift: The Social Dynamics of Values in LLM Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05514)
*Farah Atif,Sougata Saha,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于世界价值观调查（WVS）的仿真框架，评估LLM代理能否忠实模拟多样且冲突的人类价值体系。在约4000段对话（1200个角色、15个主题、3种模型）中，发现超过50%的角色从一开始就无法表达其分配的价值取向，2-7%在重复对话后发生漂移。与人类讨论相比，模拟对话在风格一致性与语义多样性之间存在不同的权衡。结论认为当前LLM代理虽能生成看似合理的对话，但在代表和维持多样化人类价值画像方面仍是有限的代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理越来越多地被用作社会科学研究中人类参与者的替代品，一个关键问题亟待回答：这些代理能否忠实地模拟人类多样且冲突的价值体系？本文的动机在于系统性地检验LLM代理在价值模拟方面的可靠性，以期为社会科学领域中LLM代理的使用提供实证依据和边界认知。

Method: 构建一个WVS驱动的仿真框架：具有不同文化背景和沟通风格的代理参与纵向的多轮价值话题讨论。研究设计了约4000段对话，覆盖1200个角色、15个主题，测试三个模型（GPT-4o、Gemini-2.5-Flash、Gemma-4-E4B）。评估维度包括价值保真度（value faithfulness）、价值漂移（value drift）和对话真实感（conversational realism）。同时进行消融实验，移除人口统计细节以考察其对价值保真度的影响。

Result: 研究发现：超过50%的角色在对话开始时未能表达其被分配的WVS价值画像；2-7%在重复对话后发生价值漂移。移除人口统计细节的消融实验虽对部分模型的价值保真度有所提升，但并未改变整体趋势——模拟的价值分布仍系统性地偏离所分配的WVS画像。与人类讨论相比，模拟对话在风格一致性与语义多样性之间呈现出不同的权衡，往往产生内容多样化但风格重复的交流。

Conclusion: 当前LLM代理能够生成看似合理的对话，但在忠实代表和长期维持多样化人类价值画像方面仍存在根本性局限。这意味着在社会科学研究中，将LLM代理作为人类参与者的替代品需要谨慎对待，不能简单地认为它们能可靠地模拟真实的人类价值系统。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used as proxies for human participants in social science research, yet it remains unclear whether they can faithfully simulate diverse and conflicting human value systems. We present a World Values Survey (WVS)-grounded simulation framework where culturally diverse agents with different communication styles engage in longitudinal, value-laden discussions. Across approximately 4,000 conversations involving 1,200 personas, 15 topics, and three models (GPT-4o, Gemini-2.5-Flash, and Gemma-4-E4B), we evaluate value faithfulness, value drift, and conversational realism. We find that more than 50\% of personas fail to express their assigned WVS profiles from the outset, while 2-7\% drift after repeated conversations. Ablations removing demographic details improve faithfulness for some models but do not change the broader trend: simulated value distributions still systematically deviate from the assigned WVS profiles. Compared to human discussions, simulated dialogues show a different trade-off between stylistic consistency and semantic diversity, often producing content-wise varied but stylistically repetitive exchanges. These findings suggest that current LLM agents can generate plausible conversations, but remain limited proxies for representing and preserving diverse human value profiles over time.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond "AI Helps Humans": Decision-Targeted Evaluation Design for Human-Agent Teams in the Agentic Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05527)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TEAM-Design规则，用于在有限重放预算下决定哪些任务应分配给人工独奏重放、哪些分配给智能体独奏重放，以评估人机协作工作流是否同时优于两种替代方案。该规则提升基线结果难以预测、比较难以建立且重放成本低的任务的采样概率，并能控制误判风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作工作流的部署决策取决于其是否同时优于"纯人类"和"纯智能体"两种替代方案，但部署后无法观察到替代结果，需要重放任务来恢复。在有限重放预算约束下，需要决定优先重放哪些任务，而现有方法（智能体基准测试、方差采样、贝叶斯信息方法）均未直接针对该部署决策进行优化。
