Method: 提出TEAM-Design规则：为每个任务分配两个重放概率（分别对应人类基线和智能体基线）。概率上升取决于：缺失基线结果难以从已有知识预测、该对比更难以建立；概率下降取决于重放成本高。作者证明该规则求解了该预算受限的设计问题，并证明随机化重放可控制错误宣称工作流同时优于两种替代品的概率。

Result: 在6个临床场景（其中无人机协作流程同时优于两种替代）和1个编程基准（存在一个同时优于两者）上重新分析，并在合成设计及基于真实胸部X光阅读者研究的半合成设计上评估。结果表明TEAM-Design在两种比较难度差异明显时表现最佳，而当两种比较难度相近时可能不如方差分配方法。

Conclusion: TEAM-Design为预算受限下的人机协作评估提供了直接针对部署决策的任务重放分配规则，但其有效性依赖于两种基线比较之间的难度差异；难度相近时需谨慎使用。

Abstract: Wherever a coding agent works under engineer supervision, or a clinical model assists a radiologist, the deployment question is whether to keep the human-AI workflow or replace it with the human alone or the agent alone. The human-AI workflow is worth keeping only if it beats both of those alternatives. Yet once it is deployed, neither alternative outcome is observed: recovering one means replaying the task under that alternative, and every replay costs expert time or compute. Under a fixed replay budget, the design question is therefore which tasks should be more likely to receive a human-only replay, and which an agent-only replay. Existing methods do not directly target this decision. Agent benchmarks do not choose which missing baseline to measure, variance-based sampling ignores which of the two comparisons is closer to failing, and Bayesian information methods focus on learning model parameters instead of making the deployment decision. We propose TEAM-Design, a rule that gives every task two replay probabilities, one per baseline. It raises a probability where the missing baseline outcome is hard to predict from what is already known about the task and where that comparison is harder to establish, and lowers it where replay is expensive. We prove that the rule solves this budgeted design problem, and that drawing the replays at random from recorded probabilities still controls the chance of wrongly declaring that the workflow beats both. We reanalyze 6 clinical settings, where no human-AI workflow beats both alternatives, and a coding benchmark, where one does, then evaluate TEAM-Design on synthetic designs and on a semi-synthetic design built from a real chest X-ray reader study. TEAM-Design works best when one of the two comparisons is clearly harder to settle than the other, and can do worse than variance-based allocation when the two are similarly difficult.

</details>


### [166] [EdgeMem: LLM-Free Agent Memory Construction and Retrieval via Evidence-Preserving Multi-Anchor Hypergraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05553)
*Zeyang Cui,Jiannong Cao,Zhiyuan Wen,Bo Yuan,Junlan Feng,Shengyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EdgeMem是一种基于多锚点超图的智能体记忆方法，通过保留原始对话轮次并利用内容、时间和情节三种线索组织记忆，使用轻量级局部处理构建索引，在检索和记忆问答任务中优于多种对比系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆方法常将交互历史压缩为摘要或其他LLM生成的表征，重复生成成本高，且可能在系统知道未来查询需求之前就丢失包含答案的关键细节。因此需要一种不依赖生成式压缩、能忠实保留原始信息的记忆方法。

Method: EdgeMem提出"保留原始交互轮次并通过互补的内容、时间和情节线索组织"的核心原则。具体通过轻量级局部处理构建多锚点超图实现；检索直接返回源证据，仅将LLM用于最终答案生成，实现跨多会话历史的结构化访问与原始对话的忠实保留，整个过程无需生成式LLM调用。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S数据集上取得强检索与记忆问答性能。在LoCoMo上，在共享prompt下7个复现系统中EdgeMem以61.01对58.70的strict-judge分数取得最高分；记忆构建与检索过程中无需生成式LLM调用。

Conclusion: EdgeMem证明：保留并组织源证据而非依赖生成式记忆管理，能为智能体记忆提供有效且高效的基础。

Abstract: Agent memory allows LLM agents to use earlier interactions when answering new queries. Existing methods often compress interaction histories into summaries or other LLM-generated representations. Repeated generation adds cost and can discard answer-bearing details before the system knows what a future query will require. We propose EdgeMem, an agent-memory method built around a simple principle: preserve original interaction turns and organize them through complementary content, temporal, and episodic cues. EdgeMem realizes this principle with a multi-anchor hypergraph constructed by lightweight local processing. Retrieval directly returns source evidence and reserves LLM use for final answer generation, combining structured access to multi-session histories with faithful retention of the original conversation. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S show strong retrieval and memory-grounded question answering; on LoCoMo, EdgeMem achieves the highest strict-judge score among seven reproduced systems under a shared prompt (61.01 versus 58.70), while construction and retrieval require no generative-LLM calls. Overall, EdgeMem shows that preserving and organizing source evidence provides an effective and efficient foundation for agent memory without generative memory management.

</details>


### [167] [Deep belief networks are exact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05572)
*Gleb Smirnov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明在\(\{-1,1\}^n\)上的每一个严格正概率分布都可以由具有有限参数的sigmoid信念网络精确表示，从而回答了Sutskever和Hinton提出的问题。证明方法是将他们的概率共享近似提升为精确表示，使用了Brouwer不动点定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sutskever和Hinton此前提出了sigmoid信念网络能否精确表示任意严格正概率分布的问题，但只证明了近似表示的可能性。精确表示的可行性是一个开放的理论问题，对理解sigmoid信念网络的表达能力具有根本性意义。

Method: 作者利用Brouwer不动点定理，将Sutskever和Hinton的概率共享近似方法升级为精确表示。通过构造适当的连续映射并证明其存在不动点，从而保证存在有限参数使得sigmoid信念网络能够精确匹配任意给定的严格正概率分布。

Result: 证明了在\(\{-1,1\}^n\)上任意严格正概率分布都能被有限参数的sigmoid信念网络精确表示，正面回答了Sutskever和Hinton的开放问题。

Conclusion: sigmoid信念网络具有强大的表达能力，能够精确表示任意严格正的离散概率分布，这为该类概率图模型的表达力提供了坚实的理论保证。

Abstract: We prove that every strictly positive probability distribution on \(\{-1,1\}^n\) is represented exactly by a sigmoid belief network with finite parameters. This answers a question of Sutskever and Hinton. The proof upgrades their probability-sharing approximation to exact representation using Brouwer's fixed-point theorem.

</details>


### [168] [EnvCraft: Synthesizing Executable Environments in Agentic RL for Claw-like Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05576)
*Yirong Zeng,Shen You,Jinhang Feng,Yufei Liu,Xiao Ding,Yutai Hou,Hao Cong,Yuxian Wang,Wu Ning,Wang Xu,Bibo Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EnvCraft框架，自动合成可执行的交互式沙箱环境及训练数据，以解决智能体强化学习（Agentic RL）中交互式训练环境稀缺的问题，实验表明在Claw式及通用工具使用基准上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM范式正从被动语言接口转向自主Claw式智能体，需要在有状态工作空间中执行长时程任务。尽管Agentic RL是优化此类智能体的有前景路径，但其规模化严重受限于交互式训练环境的极度稀缺。现有合成环境仅局限于工具调用端点，无法满足Claw式智能体端到端的真实世界需求。

Method: EnvCraft框架包含两个核心引擎：(1) 环境合成引擎，用于构建沙箱隔离的工作空间（可执行环境）；(2) 拓扑感知的数据生成引擎，用于产生连贯的任务轨迹。整体合成了139个交互式环境，包含约2万个复杂任务用于Agentic RL训练。

Result: 在Qwen3/3.5模型（8B-32B）上的实验表明，该方法在Claw式基准上取得最高+11.9%的性能提升，在通用工具使用基准上取得+8.0%的提升，同时降低了推理token开销。

Conclusion: 合成的可执行环境能为智能体训练提供稳健且可泛化的学习信号，验证了EnvCraft方法的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: The paradigm of LLMs has rapidly shifted from passive language interfaces to autonomous Claw-like agents that execute long-horizon tasks across stateful workspaces. While Agentic Reinforcement Learning (Agentic RL) provides a promising path to optimize these agents, its scaling is heavily bottlenecked by the severe scarcity of interactive training environments. Existing synthetic environments are strictly limited to tool-calling endpoints, rendering them insufficient for accommodating the end-to-end real-world demands of claw-like agents. To bridge this gap, we introduce EnvCraft, an automated framework for synthesizing executable environments and scalable training data. Specifically, EnvCraft employs an environment synthesis engine to build sandbox-isolated workspaces, alongside a topology-aware data generation engine to produce coherent task trajectories. Overall, we synthesize 139 interactive environments comprising approximately 20K complex tasks for Agentic RL training. Experiments on Qwen3/3.5 models (8B-32B) show that our method yields gains of up to +11.9% on Claw-style benchmarks and +8.0% on general tool-use benchmarks, with concurrent reductions in inference token cost. The results confirm that synthesized executable environments provide robust and generalizable learning signals for training.

</details>


### [169] [Planning and Scheduling Business Processes under Control-Flow Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05578)
*Michel Kunkler,Stefanie Rinderle-Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究业务流程中的调度问题，针对流程中因数据驱动决策导致执行路径不确定的挑战，提出将问题建模为机会约束优化，分别给出分解式（两阶段）与集成式两种规划调度方案，并在真实与合成数据集上验证了集成式更优但难扩展、分解式可扩展至大规模场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业务流程中的活动调度可以提升效率（如缩短完工时间），但由于执行过程中基于涌现数据的决策导致确切的执行路径不确定，使得调度具有挑战性。尽管历史日志可估计决策的概率信息，帮助预判可能成功完成的执行路径，但规划特定路径会影响可行性（成功完成概率）以及被规划却未执行的多余活动数，因此需要一种兼顾可行性与效率的优化方法。

Method: 将问题形式化为机会约束优化问题，提出两种公式化方法：(1) 分解式两阶段方法——规划阶段在可行性约束下最小化期望多余活动数，调度阶段在已规划活动上最小化工期；(2) 集成式方法——将规划与调度结合为单一公式进行优化。

Result: 在两个真实数据集与一个合成数据集上的评估表明：集成式方法能够获得更优的工期（makespan），但在大规模场景下计算上不可行（难扩展）；分解式方法则能够扩展到大规模设置。

Conclusion: 两种方法各有优劣：集成式方法在调度质量上更优但受限于可扩展性，分解式方法在可扩展性上占优但调度质量略逊，为业务流程规划与调度中路径不确定问题的解决提供了权衡选择方案。

Abstract: Scheduling activities in business processes can improve efficiency (e.g., reduce makespan), but is challenging because the exact sequence of activities required to complete a case is often uncertain due to decisions based on data that emerges during execution. Nevertheless, probabilistic information regarding such decisions can often be estimated or derived from historical execution logs, and can help anticipate which execution paths are likely to lead to successful completion. Planning with particular execution paths affects feasibility, i.e., the probability of successful completion, and the expected number of superfluous activities that are planned but never executed. We frame the problem as a chance-constrained optimization problem and present two formulations: A decomposed approach with two stages, a planning stage that minimizes the expected number of superfluous activities subject to a feasibility constraint, and a scheduling stage that minimizes the makespan over the planned activities; and an integrated approach that combines planning and scheduling into a single formulation. Evaluation on two real-world and one synthetic dataset shows that the integrated approach yields superior makespans but is intractable at scale, while the decomposed approach scales to large settings.

</details>


### [170] [Agents Trust Tools Too Much: Measuring Reliance on Unreliable Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05587)
*Hoyeol Yang,Woojung Song,Taewon Kim,Jonghyun Song,Seoyeon Park,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了工具使用型智能体对不可靠工具返回信息的"过度信任"问题。通过对14个LLM在三种工具（网页搜索、LLM子代理委派、代码执行）下的评估，发现智能体平均采纳被污染内容的比例超过三分之一，网页搜索场景高达68%。推理轨迹分析还揭示了一个严重问题：智能体常能识别冲突甚至内部恢复正确答案，却只输出被污染的错误答案而不警告用户。作者尝试了三类干预（用户提示、工具提供商元数据、智能体构建者后训练），但均无法在各类工具上一致地缓解过度信任问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用智能体评估通常假设工具返回可靠信息，但真实系统中工具返回可能看似合理却实际错误。智能体是否会不加批判地采纳这些不可靠信息，以及如何缓解这种过度信任，是尚未充分研究的关键问题。

Method: 对14个LLM使用三种工具（网页搜索、LLM子代理委派、代码执行）进行系统性评估。对每种工具的返回内容进行人为破坏（corrupt），测量智能体在最终答案中采纳被污染内容的比率。通过分析推理轨迹揭示失败模式，并在三个层级（用户提示、工具提供商元数据、智能体构建者后训练）实施干预策略。

Result: 所有三种设置中智能体均表现出高度的过度信任：每种工具的平均采纳率均超过三分之一，网页搜索高达68.0%。推理轨迹分析发现一个特别令人担忧的失败模式——智能体常常识别出冲突甚至内部恢复正确答合，却仅呈现被污染的错误答案且不向用户发出警告。三类干预虽对某些模型或工具有效，但没有任何干预能在各工具间一致缓解过度信任。

Conclusion: 对不可靠工具的过度信任是智能体系统中一个严重且持久存在的失败模式。该研究凸显了需要开发能够验证工具输出并透明沟通未解决冲突的评估方法和干预手段，以确保工具使用智能体的可靠性。

Abstract: Existing evaluations of tool-using agents primarily measure whether an agent can successfully complete diverse tasks with tools. These evaluations generally assume that tools return reliable information. However, tool returns in real-world systems can be plausible yet incorrect. We investigate how agents respond to unreliable tool returns by evaluating fourteen LLMs using three tools-web search, LLM sub-agent delegation, and code execution. For each tool, we corrupt its returns and measure whether agents adopt the corrupted content in their final answers. Agents exhibit high levels of overtrust across all three settings: the mean adoption rate exceeds one third for every tool and reaches 68.0% for web search. Analysis of reasoning traces reveals a particularly concerning failure mode: agents often recognize conflicts and even recover the correct answer internally, yet present only the corrupted answer without warning the user. To mitigate agents' overtrust in tool returns, we intervene at three levels: prompting by the user, metadata from the tool provider, and post-training by the agent builder. Although some interventions help for particular models or tools, none consistently mitigates overtrust across tools. These findings identify overtrust in unreliable tools as a serious and persistent failure mode, motivating evaluations and interventions that enable agents to validate tool outputs and transparently communicate unresolved conflicts.

</details>


### [171] [The convergent laboratory: when AI reasoning, autonomous experiments, high performance and quantum computing reshape chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05643)
*Eliu Huerta,Xiaoyun Wang,Geetika Gupta,Edward H. Sargent,Cameron J. Owen,Victor Fung,Abhishek Mitra,Austin Cheng,Emma Bouchard,Shams Mehdi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是TPC26会议的评论文章，讨论了AI、自主智能体、自驱动实验室、高性能计算与量子计算等前沿技术如何汇聚融合，共同推动材料科学发现进入变革性拐点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TPC26会议汇聚了学术界、国家实验室和工业界的领导者，旨在探讨多种新兴技术（AI、自主智能体、自驱动实验室、量子计算等）如何在材料科学发现中相互融合、协同放大各自的单点影响力，把握材料科学研究正处于变革性突破的临界时机。

Method: 该评论基于TPC26会议中来自研究前线参与者的第一手经验与观点交流，通过对AI驱动的推理、自主智能体框架、自驱动实验室以及容错量子处理器等并行成熟的技术领域进行整合性讨论与反思，形成面向材料与化学科学界的参考性观点文本。

Result: 本文以评论形式呈现，识别出各前沿技术正处于同步成熟的拐点期，论证了AI推理、自主智能体、自驱动实验室与量子计算之间的协同将引发材料与化学科学领域"生产性颠覆"。文中没有提供具体的实验数据或量化结果，侧重提供系统性观点与参考框架。

Conclusion: 作者认为当前正处于化学与材料科学领域变革性进展的关键拐点，多技术融合（AI、自主智能体、自驱动实验室、高性能与量子计算）将产生远超各自独立作用的影响，呼吁研究界关注由此带来的生产性颠覆与转型机遇。

Abstract: This Comment emerges from TPC26 (https://tpc26.org), a conference convening leaders from academia, national laboratories, and industry who are reshaping materials science discovery. The meeting explored how AI, autonomous agents, self-driving labs, higher performance and quantum computing converge to amplify their individual impact on materials science discovery. The perspectives here reflect the firsthand experiences of researchers at these frontiers and capture the essence of this global endeavor. As AI-driven reasoning, autonomous agentic frameworks, self-driving laboratories, and fault-tolerant quantum processors mature simultaneously, we offer this Comment as a reference at what we believe is a tipping point of transformative advances and productive disruption in the chemical sciences.

</details>


### [172] [What LLM Trading Agents Actually Do in Production: A Six-Month, Population-Scale Record from Two Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05663)
*T. J. Barton,Chris Constantakis,Patti Hauseman,Annie Mous,Alaska Hoffman,Brian Bergeron,Hunter Goodreau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于生产环境中两个系统（DX Terminal Pro与DXAP）约六个月的连续人口级测量记录，揭示自主语言模型交易代理的行为特征。核心发现是：运行层（如风险滑块、agent固定效应、排行榜展示边界）对行为的塑造远超策略文本；交易规模对波动率视而不见；代理几乎无法捕获其触达的大部分上行空间；且两个舰队均无方向性优势。作者最后提出17条方法论规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多为回测或离线模拟，缺少对LLM交易代理在大规模、真实资金、生产环境下的连续测量。作者希望利用罕见的人口级生产数据，回答"LLM交易代理在现实资本市场中到底如何行事"这一根本问题，以辨别真实的市场能力与运行环境/界面设计造成的行为伪影。

Method: 汇集两个同源系统的生产记录：DX Terminal Pro（21天，3505个用户注资金库，真实ETH在Base模因币市场）与DXAP alpha舰队（约6个月，500-599个用户创建代理，Hyperliquid永续合约）。数据含约750万次单模型调用、30万次链上动作及231,638次多工具轮次（14,596次成交）。运用回归不连续（排行榜前3截断）因果识别、agent固定效应方差分解、波动率分位分析、配对回放联赛（416个生产场景）等方法，并通过日聚类推断、置换零模型与共同费率重述进行稳健性检验。

Result: 四项核心发现：1) 运行层决定行为——风险滑块每级提升杠杆+0.425，agent固定效应解释60%方差，前3名排行榜截断处存在1.75x的因果选择效应；2) 头寸规模对波动率失明——每个波动率六分位中位数杠杆恒为5.0x，且占账面11%的单一姿态滑块单元承担62%的清算；3) 代理收益兑现能力弱——43.2%头寸24h内触及+300bps有利偏移，但其中49.3%仍以负收益平仓，机械支架每头寸可恢复+39.0bps；4) 两舰队均无方向性优势——DXAP不盈利且跑输Hyperliquid零售基准（回合胜率41% vs 50%），前沿模型在配对回放中决策质量统计上无差异，而选择稳定性跨模型族差异显著。

Conclusion: LLM交易代理的表现在生产环境中主要由操作/运行层（UI交互、界面展示、账号配置）决定，而非其策略文本或模型推理能力。代理系统性地错失其捕获的上行并缺乏方向性edge，表明当前架构的核心瓶颈在于执行与止损结构而非方向预测。作者据此提炼17条方法论规范，并坦诚披露自身过往撤回的经验教训。

Abstract: We present a continuous, population-scale measurement record of autonomous language-model trading agents operating in production across two systems with one design lineage: DX Terminal Pro (3,505 user-funded vaults trading real ETH in Base memecoin markets for 21 days, February to March 2026) and the DXAP live alpha fleet (500 to 599 user-created agents all-history, 91 to 117 concurrently active, trading Hyperliquid perpetuals, June to August 2026). The record spans roughly six months, 7.5M single-model invocations with about 300K onchain actions, and a further 231,638 multi-tool turns producing 14,596 fills. Four findings carry the paper. First, the operating layer determines behavior more than anything written in strategy text: a risk slider explains leverage (+0.425 per level), agent fixed effects absorb 60% of variance, and a leaderboard render boundary causally routes selection (regression discontinuity 1.75x at the top-3 cut). Second, sizing is volatility-blind: median leverage is 5.0x in every volatility sextile, and one posture-slider cell (11% of the book) holds 62% of liquidations. Third, agents capture almost none of the upside they reach: 43.2% of positions saw at least +300 bps of favorable excursion within 24h, yet 49.3% of those closed with a negative trade return; a mechanical bracket recovers +39.0 bps per position. Fourth, neither fleet shows a directional edge. The DXAP fleet is not profitable and trails a matched Hyperliquid retail benchmark (41% vs. 50% roundtrip win rate). A paired-replay league of frontier models on 416 captured production scenarios finds decision quality statistically indistinguishable at this horizon, while choice stability differs sharply across model families. Every headline survives day-clustered inference, permutation nulls, and a common-fee restatement; the paper closes with a 17-rule methodology canon bought with our own retractions.

</details>


### [173] [CUSP: Decomposable Collective Uncertainty for Multi-Agent Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05708)
*Chung-En Johnny Yu,David Garcia,Brian Jalaian,Nathaniel D. Bastian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CUSP，一个无需训练的视觉-语言模型集成不确定性量化框架。它将多个VLM响应映射到共享语义响应空间并池化为语义意见，输出集体不确定性和Jensen-Shannon散度两个互补的系统级信号。集成的预测误差检测在AUROC上达0.764，弃权AUARC达0.889，且无需token logits或校准标签，适用于开源和商用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合异构视觉-语言模型虽然能提升多模态推理能力，但单个模型的置信度或聚合答案的置信度都无法衡量系统层面的可靠性，缺乏在系统级对多模型集成进行不确定性量化的方法。

Method: CUSP框架包含三步骤：(1)将多个VLM响应映射到共享语义响应空间；(2)将这些语义意见池化为一个语义意见池；(3)报告两个互补的系统级信号——集体不确定性（表示池化意见的离散程度）和Jensen-Shannon散度JSD（表示模型级意见间的冲突）。其中非归一化的集体熵可精确分解为各模型个体语义熵的均值与JSD之和。该方法无需token logits也不需要校准标签。

Result: 在静态多VLM集成中：集体不确定性的预测误差检测AUROC为0.764、弃权AUARC为0.889，在小模型场景表现最强，超出多数投票和朴素选择基线4.7-15.8个点，且随集成规模增大优势扩大；JSD在商用模型场景最强（AUROC 0.819、AUARC 0.910），对硬答案模型冲突排序AUROC达0.982。池化预测比单个模型平均准确率提升5.6-13.0个百分点。在多步多智能体系统全轨迹中，子智能体集体不确定性对系统失败的排序AUROC为0.619，弃权排序AUARC为0.699。

Conclusion: CUSP通过将多VLM响应映射至共享语义空间并池化，提供了一种训练免费、无需logits和标签的系统级不确定性量化方案。集体不确定性与JSD互补，分别在小模型和大模型/商用场景下表现最优，同时能提升池化预测精度，并在多智能体系统轨迹中提供有效的失败检测和弃权排序。

Abstract: Aggregating heterogeneous vision-language models (VLMs) can improve multimodal reasoning, but neither an individual model's confidence nor that of the aggregated answer measures reliability at the system level. We present CUSP (Collective Uncertainty through Semantic Opinion Pooling), a training-free uncertainty quantification framework that maps multiple VLM responses to a shared semantic response space, pools them into a pooled semantic opinion, and reports two complementary system-level signals: collective uncertainty, the dispersion of the pooled opinion, and Jensen-Shannon divergence (JSD), the conflict among the model-level opinions. Within this pooled semantic opinion, the unnormalized collective entropy decomposes exactly into the mean of the models' individual semantic entropies and the JSD, separating total dispersion from model conflict. Requiring neither token logits nor calibration labels, CUSP applies to open-weight and commercial VLMs alike. In static multi-VLM ensembles, collective uncertainty is the strongest signal in the small-model regime (0.764 AUROC for prediction-error detection, 0.889 AUARC for abstention), outperforming uncertainty baselines majority voting and naive selection by 4.7 to 15.8 points and widening its margin as the ensemble grows; JSD is strongest in the evaluated commercial regime (0.819 AUROC, 0.910 AUARC) and ranks hard-answer model conflict with AUROC up to 0.982. The pooled prediction also improves accuracy over the average single model by 5.6 to 13.0 points. Over the full trajectory of a multi-step, multi-agent system, subagent collective uncertainty ranks system failures above chance (0.619 AUROC) and gives the best abstention ordering among the evaluated signals (0.699 AUARC).

</details>


### [174] [Recovering Temporal and Geographic Signals from Language Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05721)
*Esteban Feuerstein,Victoria Klimkowski,Juan Manuel Ortiz de Zarate,Federico Hernán Suaiter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于投影的轻量级黑盒方法，直接作用于语言模型的输出嵌入，用以分析嵌入是否编码时间和地理等结构化真实世界信息。该方法仅需少量种子示例定义嵌入空间中的轴，并通过投影对文本或实体进行排序，无需访问模型权重、内部激活或额外训练。实验表明，该方法能有效恢复时间和空间结构，为可解释性及下游时间/地理信息检索任务提供了实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型嵌入是否编码结构化的真实世界信息（如时间和地理信号），对表征分析和信息检索均具有重要意义。现有分析方法往往需要访问模型内部结构或进行额外训练，而许多现代嵌入模型仅通过API提供，难以应用这些方法，因此需要一种完全黑盒、模型无关且轻量级的分析手段。

Method: 提出一种基于投影的方法：仅利用少量种子示例在嵌入空间中定义一个语义轴（axis），通过计算文本或实体输出嵌入在该轴上的投影值进行排序或打分。该方法完全黑盒，仅需要嵌入向量，不依赖模型权重、内部激活、辅助探针或额外训练，适用于仅通过API访问的现代嵌入模型。

Result: 在时间和地理数据集上的实验表明，嵌入投影能够恢复有意义的时序结构和空间结构，说明输出嵌入确实编码了与时间和空间相关的信号。

Conclusion: 输出嵌入编码了与时间和空间相关的结构化信号，该投影方法可作为可解释性分析及下游时间/地理信息检索任务（如时序排序、地理排名、标注）的实用工具。

Abstract: Understanding whether language-model embeddings encode structured real-world information is important for both representation analysis and information retrieval. We study this question for temporal and geographic signals using a simple projection-based method that operates directly on output embeddings. Given a small set of seed examples, the method defines an axis in embedding space and ranks texts or entities by their projection onto that axis. Our approach is fully black-box and model-agnostic: it requires only embeddings, without access to model weights, internal activations, auxiliary probes, or additional training. This makes it applicable to modern embedding models available only through APIs and provides a lightweight way to analyze whether temporal and spatial dimensions are present in their representation spaces. We apply the method to temporal and geographic datasets and find that embedding projections recover meaningful chronological and spatial structure. These results provide evidence that output embeddings encode signals relevant to time and space, while also offering a practical tool for interpretability and for downstream temporal and geographic information retrieval tasks, such as temporal ordering, geographic ranking, and tagging.

</details>


### [175] [Beyond Prompts: Measuring and Optimizing LLM Tool-Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05736)
*Cen,Zhao,Haibo Ruan,Wenjie Chen,Pei-fen Tu,Usman Abbasi,Joel Hesch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对固定模型的多轮工具智能体，研究"资源受限的混合（harness）选择"问题——即不重训模型，仅通过修改运行时外围（提示词、工具边界中间件等）来改进智能体。提出了一个与优化器无关的评估协议（报告平均保留提升、最差条件提升、可重复性、成本诊断和RelLift95(B)），并实例化了PRISM优化器，其通过对故障聚类并将修复路由到提示词、工具边界中间件或联合编辑面进行Pareto搜索。在BFCL多轮、tau2-Retail和tau2-Telecom上分别获得14.2、14.9和10.1个百分点的平均保留提升，且三个基准上RelLift95均为正。消融实验表明收益主要来自故障面路由和编辑模式约束。此外，作者强调仅报告平均保留提升可能掩盖脆弱更新，可靠性指标应与平均提升一同报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型工具智能体无需重训练即可通过修改运行时harness（提示词、工具接口、中间件、状态处理、恢复逻辑）来改进。作者希望系统化研究这一固定模型下的优化空间，并解决一个核心问题：在预算受限下选择一个可靠且能带来持续保留收益的harness，而不是仅追求偶发的大幅提升而忽视更新鲁棒性。

Method: 将问题形式化为资源受限的固定模型多轮工具智能体harness选择任务，搜索面限定为提示词和工具边界中间件（编辑以工具边界的受保护拦截形式存在，而非任意改写执行逻辑）。提出优化器无关的评估协议，报告四类指标：平均保留提升、最差条件提升、可重复性、日志化成本诊断，以及RelLift95(B)——对预算B下所选harness保留收益的保守估计。实例化两个优化器（仅提示词与提示词加中间件），其中PRISM通过对失败进行聚类，将修复路由到提示词面、工具边界中间件面或联合编辑面，并进行Pareto搜索。

Result: 在BFCL多轮、tau2-Retail和tau2-Telecom基准上，PRISM获得14.2、14.9和10.1个百分点的平均保留提升；三个基准上经验RelLift95均为正。组分消融实验表明收益主要归因于故障面路由和编辑模式约束。跨优化器比较发现，一些搜索过程偶尔找到大的提升却仍选择了脆弱的更新。

Conclusion: 在固定模型的工具智能体优化中，应同时报告可靠性指标（如最差条件提升和RelLift95）与平均保留提升，因为仅看平均值可能掩盖选择脆弱更新的风险。PRISM的故障面路由与编辑模式约束是取得稳健收益的关键设计要素，该评估协议和优化方法可推广到更广泛的harness选择场景。

Abstract: LLM tool agents can be improved without retraining by modifying the runtime harness around a fixed model: prompts, tool interfaces, middleware, state handling, and recovery logic. We study this setting as resource-bounded harness selection for fixed-model multi-turn tool agents, with the search surface scoped to prompts and tool-boundary middleware: edits are guarded intercepts at the tool boundary, not arbitrary rewriting of agent execution logic. Our optimizer-agnostic protocol reports mean held-out lift, worst-condition lift, repeatability, logged cost diagnostics, and RelLift95(B), a conservative estimate of the held-out gain of the harness selected under budget B. We instantiate the protocol with prompt-only and prompt-plus-middleware optimizers, including PRISM, which clusters failures and routes repairs to prompt, tool-boundary middleware, or joint edit surfaces within a Pareto search. On BFCL multi-round, tau2-Retail, and tau2-Telecom, PRISM obtains mean held-out lifts of 14.2, 14.9, and 10.1 percentage points and positive empirical RelLift95 on all three benchmarks, and a component ablation attributes the margin chiefly to failure-surface routing and the edit-pattern constraint. Across optimizers, the results show that some search procedures can occasionally find large gains but still choose brittle updates, so the reliability of the chosen harness should be reported alongside average held-out lift.

</details>


### [176] [The Normalization of Deviance in AI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05749)
*Emilio Barkett,Alexander Kimpton,Daniel Graham,Yusuf Kundgol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证AI开发组织即使系统能力再强，也受到与历史上重大技术灾难相同的组织结构性动力学影响，倾向于走向系统性失败。通过对照挑战者号航天飞机、三哩岛核事故和波音737 MAX空难的案例，识别出灾难前共同的结构性机制，并将其映射到当代AI开发中，指出现有安全基础设施可能提供比表象更少的保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI风险研究过度聚焦于能力风险（系统过于强大、自主或与人类价值观错位），而忽视了组织层面的风险——即构建AI系统的机构本身是否天生倾向于漂移向失败。作者意图在AI开发的灾难前阶段仍可干预时，使这些组织动力学变得可读、可见。

Method: 采用历史案例比较研究方法，选取空间/核能/航空三个领域具有代表性的重大技术灾难——挑战者号航天飞机事故、三哩岛核事故、波音737 MAX空难，从中提取每起事故前共有的结构性组织失效机制，并将这些机制映射到当代AI开发组织的实践与安全流程中进行分析。

Result: 研究发现，无论AI系统本身能力如何，构建它们的组织面临与以往重大技术灾难相同的结构性动力学。组织可以在完全合规地完成各项安全流程的情况下，仍然产出灾难性后果——即现有安全基础设施可能提供虚假安全感，实际保护效果低于其表象。

Conclusion: AI开发的灾难前阶段仍在进行中，当前是识别和打断这些组织失效动力学的关键窗口期。论文旨在使这些隐藏的组织风险公开化、可干预化，而非等到灾难发生后再进行追溯式的事后分析。

Abstract: Work on the risks of artificial intelligence has focused predominantly on capability risk: the danger that systems become too powerful, too autonomous, or too misaligned with human values. Far less attention has been paid to the organizational level---to whether the institutions building these systems are themselves predisposed to drift toward failure. This paper argues that they are. Regardless of how capable AI systems become, the organizations building them face the same structural dynamics that preceded past major technological disasters. Drawing on case studies of the Space Shuttle Challenger, the Three Mile Island accident, and the Boeing 737 MAX crashes, this paper identifies the common structural mechanisms preceding each failure and maps them onto contemporary AI development. The findings suggest that existing safety infrastructure may provide less protection than it appears, as organizations can complete safety processes in full compliance and still produce catastrophic outcomes. The pre-disaster period of AI development is still underway; the purpose of this paper is to make these dynamics legible while they can still be interrupted.

</details>


### [177] [From Monolithic Blending to Agentic Orchestration: Dynamic Response for Conversational Assistants at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05758)
*Cen,Zhao,Peng Wang,Chuan Shi,Yufeng Zhang,Ying Lyu,Wanmeng Ren,Robert Xue,Claire Na Cheng,Yashar Mehdad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个大型住宿预订平台（月对话数百万、11种语言、P90延迟10秒）在客户支持助手上的一次生产级架构迁移：用有界ReAct编排器+类型化工具+后端校验上下文契约的小型生成器（Dynamic Response, DR），取代单一Qwen3-235B-A22B混合应答器。通过严格控制变量下的重放实验和低流量A/B测试，阐明各效果归因：类型化实体选择将预约选择器精度从8.3%提升至89.1%（召回从75.2%降至67.3%）；类型化动作ID+成员检查将结构化动作幻觉从2.14%降至0.0%；硬转人工从5.60%降至3.08%，软转人工从9.56%降至2.49%，而生产转人工量基本持平。服务端优化将编排器P90延迟从3.87s降至2.24s，GPU占用减少约1/3，自托管使年模型服务成本降低一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话助手可以在单一模型路径中混合检索、动作选择、升级和措辞，或将角色分离。作者希望验证在真实生产环境中，将混合应答分解为"有界ReAct编排器+类型化工具+后端校验上下文契约+小型生成器"的模块化架构，是否能带来可归因的、可控的精度提升与幻觉消除，并说明迁移中由于提示词、对齐和服务方式也发生变化，因此需要通过严格控制变量的实验来区分架构效应的真实贡献。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：(1) 用相同重放回合在换提示词/对齐/服务前后分别测量，仅将那些在完全相同输入上再现的差异归因于架构效应，从而分离提示词、对齐、服务等混杂因素；(2) 采用低流量（low-ramp）A/B测试验证生产环境中的升级率变化。新架构DR包含有界ReAct编排器（有限步推理与工具调用）、类型化工具（typed tools，含类型化实体选择与类型化动作ID）、后端校验的上下文契约（backend-validated context contract），以及一个较小的生成器负责最终措辞撰写。服务端采用自托管与优化以降延迟和削减GPU及成本。

Result: 在精确控制的条件下，架构效果可获得量化：类型化实体选择让预约选择器从精度8.3%/召回75.2%移动到精度89.1%/召回67.3%的精度优先工作点；类型化动作ID加成员检查将结构化动作幻觉从2.14%彻底消除至0.0%。低流量A/B测试显示硬升级响应从5.60%降至3.08%、软升级响应从9.56%降至2.49%，生产转人工量基本稳定；自助解决率为方向性改善（+5.1点，95%CI[-2,+12]）。服务优化后编排器P90时延从3.87s降至2.24s，GPU占用减少约1/3，自托管使年化模型服务成本降低一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 模块化架构（编排+类型化工具+后端校验）+小型生成器在真实生产规模下显著优于单一大型混合应答模型：可带来精度数量级提升、完全消除结构化动作幻觉、降低转人工率而不牺牲用户转人工可用性，且通过类型化中间表示带来可控性与可调试性。同时，提示/对齐/服务解耦后的测量表明，架构设计本身才是这些改进的主要来源，而非仅仅换成了更强模型或更好提示。成本与延迟的显著降低进一步支持该架构在大规模多语言生产环境中的可行性。

Abstract: Conversational assistants can blend retrieval, action selection, escalation, and wording in a single model path, or separate those roles. We report a production migration of a customer-support assistant at a large accommodation marketplace (millions of conversations per month, 11 languages, 10-second P90). Dynamic Response (DR) replaces a single Qwen3-235B-A22B blended responder with a bounded ReAct orchestrator over typed tools plus a smaller generator that writes from a backend-validated context contract. Because the migration also changed prompts, alignment, and serving, we attribute each effect to its cause and claim as architecture effects only those measured on identical replayed turns: typed entity selection moves the reservation selector to a precision-first operating point (precision 8.3% to 89.1%, recall 75.2% to 67.3%), and typed action IDs with a membership check remove observed structured-action hallucination (2.14% to 0.0%). A low-ramp A/B test reproduces the replay escalation reductions: hard-escalation responses fall from 5.60% to 3.08% and soft-escalation responses from 9.56% to 2.49%, while production handoff volume holds roughly steady; self-solve is directional (+5.1 points, 95% CI [-2, +12]). Serving optimizations cut orchestrator P90 latency from 3.87s to 2.24s on a GPU footprint reduced by roughly one-third, and self-hosting reduces estimated annual model-serving cost by more than an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [178] [Inference-Time Graph Engineering for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05774)
*Katherine Tieu,Dongqi Fu,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReActNet，一个无需训练的框架，通过将查询和角色特化智能体编译为随时间演化的有向通信图序列（时间工作流图），实现多智能体LLM系统的显式、可检查且任务条件化的编排，在知识推理、数学、代码生成和GAIA任务上超越固定拓扑与学习拓扑基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的多智能体LLM系统通常依赖静态拓扑或学习优化拓扑，但忽略了通信的时序和语义条件。作者认为有效的多智能体编排不仅取决于哪些智能体通信，还取决于信息在推理过程中何时、为何以及如何流动——这需要从图工程视角重新审视多智能体编排。

Method: ReActNet将图编译与图执行分离：首先将查询和一组角色特化智能体编译为一系列有向通信图快照（每个快照对应一个推理阶段，每条边携带自然语言指令指定消息语义），然后通过结构化消息传递执行该时序图——智能体整合自身先前状态与控制器分配的邻居消息来更新推理状态，最终由聚合器综合各状态生成答案。整个过程无需强化学习或梯度基拓扑优化。

Result: 在知识推理、数学问题求解、代码生成及GAIA风格助手任务上，ReActNet持续优于固定拓扑和学习拓扑基线，同时保持有竞争力的推理成本。

Conclusion: 有效多智能体编排的关键不仅在于通信拓扑结构，更在于工程化可执行的工作流图，该图需编码推理过程中信息流动的时间、原因和方式。任务条件化的时序工作流图设计为多智能体协调提供了显式、可检查且无需训练的新范式。

Abstract: Recent multi-agent LLM systems increasingly rely on graph-structured communication to coordinate specialized agents. We revisit multi-agent orchestration from a graph-engineering perspective: rather than optimizing a static topology, we synthesize a task-conditioned temporal workflow graph that jointly specifies agent connectivity and edge-level communication semantics. We introduce ReActNet, a training-free framework that compiles a query and a set of role-specialized agents into a sequence of directed communication graphs. Each graph snapshot corresponds to one reasoning stage, and each edge carries a natural-language instruction specifying the message that a source agent should provide to a target agent. The compiled temporal graph is then executed through structured message passing: agents update their reasoning states by integrating their previous states with messages from controller-assigned neighbors, and a final aggregator synthesizes the resulting states into the answer. This design separates graph compilation from graph execution, making multi-agent coordination explicit, inspectable, and task-conditioned without requiring reinforcement learning or gradient-based topology optimization. Across knowledge reasoning, mathematical problem solving, code generation, and GAIA-style assistant tasks, ReActNet consistently improves over fixed-topology and learned-topology baselines while maintaining competitive inference cost. These results suggest that effective multi-agent orchestration depends not only on which agents communicate, but also on engineering executable workflow graphs that encode when, why, and how information should flow during reasoning.

</details>


### [179] [DI-Bench: Systematically Generating In-Domain Data Intelligence Benchmarks for Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05776)
*Jiangyun Zhang,Kristen Surrao,Torpong Nitayanont,Yupei Zhang,Roopali Singh,Zhiyu Chen,Julia Huang,Zhou Tang,Shayan Ali Akbar,Omar Alonso,Erwin Cornejo,Yuan Li,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DI-Bench，一个用于生成企业数据智能（DI）领域真实基准测试的自动化流水线。该pipeline通过构建工件链接图将数据表、维度、指标和文档关联起来，生成需要计算与知识检索相结合的任务，并通过查询执行和LLM验证确保答案正确性。实验表明，模型在需要业务规则修改计算的复杂任务上准确率仅32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级智能体需要在特定领域基准上进行评估，但现有公共基准鲜有评估智能体能否整合业务知识与分析计算的能力，且手工构建此类基准成本高昂。因此需要一个自动化pipeline来生成覆盖数据智能任务的真实基准。

Method: DI-Bench通过构建包含数据表、维度、指标和文档的工件链接图来模拟需要计算和知识检索的DI任务。答案通过查询执行获得，随后使用LLM生成问题并进行验证。流水线应用于两个公共数据集，生成包含731个任务的基准，覆盖知识检索、分析计算和基于规则的推理。

Result: 该pipeline在公共数据集上生成了731个任务的基准。评估四个模型后发现：模型在检索到的业务规则需要修改计算的计算任务上准确率仅为32%，揭示了该基准具有显著区分度和难度。

Conclusion: DI-Bench证明了自动化生成企业数据智能基准的可行性，同时揭示出现有模型在处理需结合业务知识与分析计算的复杂任务时存在显著不足，表明该研究方向仍需进一步探索和改进。

Abstract: Evaluating enterprise agents on domain-specific benchmarks is critical, yet public benchmarks rarely evaluate whether agents can integrate business knowledge with analytical computation, and constructing such benchmarks manually is costly. We present DI-Bench, a pipeline for generating realistic benchmarks for data intelligence (DI), the practice of extracting insights from large volumes of enterprise data. To emulate realistic DI tasks that require both computation and knowledge retrieval, DI-Bench builds an artifact linkage graph over data tables, dimensions, metrics, and documents to form questions involving structured data and associated knowledge. Ground truth answers are derived via query execution, followed by LLM question generation and validation. Applied to two public datasets, the pipeline produces a 731-task benchmark covering knowledge retrieval, analytical computation, and rule-grounded reasoning. To show the discriminatory capability and difficulty of the benchmark, we evaluate four models, revealing a substantial finding: models achieve only 32% accuracy when doing computational tasks where retrieved business rules modify the computation.

</details>


### [180] [Distilling Vision-Language Models for On-Device Fire Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05782)
*Mohammad Kazzazi,Zixuan Liu,Siavash Khajavi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何将领域特化的视觉语言模型（VLM）压缩并部署到嵌入式火灾检测传感器上。作者开发了教师-学生知识蒸馏框架，将大VLMs（火灾理解微调后）蒸馏为轻量学生模型，并在商业Detectium火灾传感器上联合评估推理精度、延迟与内存。结果表明紧凑学生模型保留了教师的大部分火灾理解能力，其中Qwen2.5-0.5B提供了最佳的整体部署权衡，且压缩部署不仅影响精度也影响模型失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统火灾检测系统误报率高，VLM通过语义上下文推理可降低误报，但VLM模型庞大，无法在嵌入式火灾传感器上实际部署，因此需要研究如何在保持安全关键行为的前提下压缩领域特化的VLM以实现完全端侧部署。

Method: 采用教师-学生知识蒸馏框架，将针对火灾理解微调后的大规模VLM蒸馏为轻量级学生模型，并在多个VLM家族和模型尺度上进行实验验证。蒸馏后的模型进一步部署在商业化的Detectium火灾检测传感器上，对推理精度、延迟和内存占用进行联合评估。

Result: 紧凑学生模型保留了教师模型的大部分火灾理解能力。压缩与部署不仅影响精度，还会改变模型的失败模式。Qwen2.5-0.5B在整体部署权衡中表现最强。

Conclusion: 为在资源受限且安全关键的场景中部署领域特化VLM提供了更广泛的指导。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) offer a promising alternative to conventional fire detection systems by reasoning about the semantic context of a scene and thus reducing false alarms, yet their large model size makes deployment on embedded fire sensors impractical. In this paper, we study how domain-specialized VLMs can be compressed for fully on-device deployment without losing the safety-critical behavior required for fire detection. We develop a teacher-student knowledge distillation framework in which large VLMs fine-tuned for fire understanding can be distilled into lightweight students. Experiments across multiple VLM families and model scales show that compact students preserve most of their teachers' fire-understanding capability. We further deploy the distilled models on our commercial Detectium fire detection sensor and jointly evaluate reasoning accuracy, latency, and memory usage. The results show that compression and deployment affect not only accuracy but also model failure modes, with Qwen2.5-0.5B providing the strongest overall deployment trade-off. Our findings provide broader guidance for deploying domain-specialized VLMs in resource-constrained, safety-critical settings.

</details>


### [181] [More Than Mimicking Reviewers: Evaluating LLMs for Pre-Submission Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05788)
*Pouya Parsa,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究面向作者的LLM审稿系统，该系统在论文提交前生成大量原子化审稿意见并压缩为简短报告。基于3,398篇ICLR 2026投稿，独立采样覆盖44.9%历史问题，去重与补充可达到78.7%（严格）和84.9%（加权）覆盖率，但成本分别高出3.6倍和5.2倍。隐藏Top-32 Oracle保留了256候选池中79.3%的加权覆盖率，而仅基于论文的选择器仅保留40–44%，表明LLM审稿覆盖面广但压缩能力差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行评审反馈常常来得太晚，作者难以在提交前进行有意义的修改。作者希望将部分"压力测试"提前到投稿之前，因此需要一个能够提供广泛且有效的预审反馈的系统。

Method: 构建一个面向作者的LLM系统：先生成大量原子化关注点（broad pool of atomic concerns），再将这些关注点压缩为简短报告。使用10,000篇ICLR 2026投稿中的3,398篇可获取预审版本的手稿进行评测，评估系统与历史评审的一致性以及遗漏意见的潜在有效性。通过独立采样、去重与补充（deduplication and refill）、以及隐藏Oracle选择器等设置进行对比实验。

Result: 独立采样覆盖44.9%的历史问题；去重与补充达到78.7%严格覆盖率和84.9%加权覆盖率，但需要3.6倍请求和5.2倍token。隐藏Top-32 Oracle在256候选池中保留完整79.3%加权覆盖率，但仅基于论文的选择器仅保留40–44%。

Conclusion: LLM评审在大型候选池下覆盖面广，但压缩能力弱；消融实验表明代表性选择和匹配器敏感性是造成压缩差距的主要原因。

Abstract: Peer-review feedback often arrives too late for authors to make meaningful revisions. We study an author-facing LLM system that moves part of this stress test before submission: it generates a broad pool of atomic concerns and compresses them into a short report. We evaluate agreement with historical reviews and, separately, the possible validity of concerns they omit.
  From 10,000 ICLR 2026 submissions, we use 3,398 manuscripts with accessible versions that predate review. On a ten-paper diagnostic, independent sampling covers 44.9% of historical issues; deduplication and refill reaches 78.7% strict and 84.9% seriousness-weighted coverage, at 3.6$\times$ more requests and 5.2$\times$ more tokens. A hidden Top-32 Oracle preserves the full 79.3% weighted coverage of a 256-candidate pool, but paper-only selectors retain only 40--44%. LLM review therefore provides broad coverage with a large candidate pool but compresses poorly; ablations identify representative selection and matcher sensitivity as the main sources of this gap.

</details>


### [182] [Spillover-Aware Multi-Value Steering for Pluralistic LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05800)
*Weici Pan,Xander Barron,Jiawei Zhou,Zhenhua Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需微调、无需奖励模型、无需人工提示工程的激活引导（activation steering）方法，能够同时控制LLM行为的多个价值维度。通过分析引导方向间的Gram矩阵，识别出朴素引导在不同价值之间产生的巨大溢出效应（spillover），并从激活范数惩罚目标中推导出一种零成本校正，精确解耦各方向的贡献。在气候话语场景中，该校正将净引导效果从+5.9%提升至+14.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的激活引导方法一次只能处理单一概念，而多元对齐（pluralistic alignment）场景需要同时引导多个价值维度。朴素引导会产生显著的溢出效应——针对某一价值的引导效果会泄漏到其他价值上，亟需一种能同时解耦并引导多个维度的方案。

Method: 将激活引导中的目标效应与溢出效应类比因果推断中的处理效应与溢出分解；将溢出归因于引导方向之间的几何纠缠（由它们的Gram矩阵刻画）；从激活范数惩罚目标中推导出零成本校正，精确解耦各方向的贡献。整体流程端到端自动完成：仅给定领域问题，自动发现价值维度、提取引导方向、诊断纠缠程度并应用校正引导。

Result: 在气候话语数据集上，经过10万对成对判断验证，校正方法将净引导效果从+5.9%提升到+14.0%。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种无需训练、无需奖励模型、无需人工提示工程的多维激活引导方案，能有效消除多维引导间的耦合干扰，支持了差异化的多元价值对齐需求。

Abstract: Activation steering controls LLM behavior at inference time by adding learned directions to hidden states, but existing methods handle one concept at a time. Pluralistic alignment, where different stakeholders need different value emphases, requires steering multiple dimensions simultaneously. We show that naive steering produces substantial spillover: the effect intended for one value leaks into others. This parallels the treatment-versus-spillover decomposition in causal inference. We trace spillover to geometric entanglement of steering directions, captured by their Gram matrix, and derive a zero-cost correction from an activation-norm-penalized objective that decouples each direction's contribution exactly. Our end-to-end pipeline requires no fine-tuning, no reward model, and no manual prompt engineering: given only domain questions, it automatically discovers value dimensions, extracts directions, diagnoses entanglement, and applies corrected steering. On climate discourse, the correction improves the net steering effect from +5.9% to +14.0%, validated over 100,000 pairwise judgments.

</details>


### [183] [Evidence-Aligned Local Composition of Discrete Experts for Sequence Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05801)
*Mohammad Panahazari,Usman A. Khan,Shuchin Aeron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"证据对齐的局部组合"（evidence-aligned local composition）方法，在无区域标签或无训练路由器的测试场景下，通过从损坏观测的边缘证据推断对多个冻结领域专家进行逐位置的软加权，来实现文档的恢复与重建。该方法在自然混合科学文档上达到0.85的区域场准确率，在词汇不相交的构造混合上达到0.98。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档（如README文件）常由多个领域（散文、代码、配置等）的文本组合生成。当此类文档被损坏且仅有冻结的领域专家可用时，既需要判断缺失内容，又需要在每个位置上决定信任哪个专家，且该决策必须在测试时完成，没有区域标签或训练好的路由器。这构成了一个组合式文档恢复的双重不确定性问题。

Method: 提出evidence-aligned local composition方法：在给定损坏模型下，从损坏观测的边缘证据中推断专家间的软逐位置权重。具体而言，通过专家自身的去噪损失来估计证据（evidence），并在位置间平滑权重。由于权重是软的，当真实组合是混合时能恢复混合分布，当单一专家足够时则集中于该专家。

Result: 在三种设定下验证：分类模拟器、字节级专家、以及从1.3B离散流匹配模型微调的专家。推断的权重在自然混合科学文档上达到0.85的区域场准确率，在区域词汇不相交的构造混合上达到0.98。当专家确实不同时，恢复效果优于单一全局权重；当专家收敛时，恢复效果退化为单一全局权重，并能追踪一种专家分离度度量。

Conclusion: 证据对齐的局部组合能够在无监督、无路由器的情况下，通过测试时的边缘证据推断实现文档的局部组合恢复，其性能依据专家间的可区分度自适应变化，为混合领域文档的损坏恢复提供了一种可靠且通用的求解途径。

Abstract: A document modeled as a discrete sequence of tokens can be thought of as being generated from a composition of texts from different domains; a README file, for example, moves between prose, code, and configuration. When such a document is corrupted and only frozen domain experts are available, restoring it requires deciding both what is missing and which expert to trust at each position, at test time and without region labels or a trained router. We introduce evidence-aligned local composition, which infers a soft, position-wise weighting over the experts from the marginal evidence of the corrupted observation under a given corruption model, estimating the evidence from the experts' own denoising losses and smoothing the weights across positions. Because the weighting is soft, it recovers a mixture when the true composition is mixed and concentrates on one expert when that suffices. Across a categorical simulator, byte-level experts, and experts fine-tuned from a $1.3$B discrete flow-matching model, the inferred weights track the true regions at $0.85$ field accuracy on naturally mixed scientific documents, and at $0.98$ on constructed mixtures whose regions are lexically disjoint. Restoration improves over a single global weight when the experts are genuinely distinct and reduces to it when they converge, tracking a measure of expert separation.

</details>


### [184] [Exposing Weaknesses in Emotion Recognition in Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05806)
*Amir Ben Khalifa,Fanny Bezancon,Amine Trabelsi,Bessam Abdulrazak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑ERC中"每句话只有一个情感标签"的核心假设，通过LLM零样本情感识别实验发现模型错误集中在包含否定、感叹和叹词的语句中，并通过四名标注者的受控重新标注研究证实大量"模型错误"实为标注本身的歧义（仅35%案例达成强一致），因此提出一种LLM-as-Judge框架，逐情感独立评估其在对话语境中的合理性，而非强制单标签判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感识别精确支持共情对话代理、心理健康支持和教育技术等多种应用。然而现有ERC方法大多依赖特定任务微调，可能利用数据集特定的线索；更关键的是，ERC中"每个话语可被赋予单一无歧义情感标签"这一核心假设几乎从未被质疑过，作者旨在调查这一假设的有效性。

Method: 在零样本设置下使用LLM并结合前文轮次作为上下文进行ERC研究；通过聚合指标掩盖的系统性失败分析（聚焦否定、感叹、叹词话语）；进行涉及四名人类标注者的受控重新标注研究以评估标注一致性；提出LLM-as-Judge框架，对每个情感独立评估其在对话语境中的可行性，替代强制单标签决策。

Result: 模型错误在包含否定、感叹词和叹词的话语周围集中出现，该模式在所有被评估模型中一致，表明是基准的限制而非模型特定弱点。重新标注研究表明仅35%案例存在强一致标注，中性话语主导高一致样本，而许多情感类别落入低一致区间，证明大量表面上的模型错误实为标注歧义。

Conclusion: 标准单标签评估不足以衡量ERC模型真实能力，许多明显模型错误反映的是真实标注歧义而非情感理解不佳。因此需要引入LLM-as-Judge框架，按每个情感在语境中的合理性独立评估，以更好地反映人类情感表达固有的模糊性与多义性。

Abstract: Emotion Recognition in Conversations (ERC) aims to identify speakers' emotions in multi-turn dialogue. Accurate emotion recognition can support a wide range of applications, including empathetic conversational agents, mental health support, and educational technologies. While many recent approaches rely on task-specific fine-tuning, such models may exploit dataset-specific cues. A central yet rarely questioned assumption in ERC is that each utterance can be assigned a single unambiguous emotion label. To investigate this assumption, we study ERC using Large Language Models (LLMs) in a zero-shot setting while incorporating preceding conversational turns as context. We show that aggregate metrics mask systematic failures. Errors concentrate around utterances containing negations, exclamations, and interjections. This pattern is consistent across all evaluated models, suggesting limitations in the benchmarks rather than model-specific weaknesses. A controlled re-annotation study involving four human annotators supports this finding: strong agreement is observed in only 35 percent of cases, with neutral utterances dominating high-agreement instances, while many emotional categories fall into low-agreement regimes. These findings suggest that many apparent model errors reflect genuine annotation ambiguity rather than poor emotion understanding. Standard single-label evaluation is therefore insufficient. To address this limitation, we introduce an LLM-as-Judge framework that evaluates each emotion independently according to its plausibility in the conversational context rather than enforcing a single-label decision.

</details>


### [185] [Agentic BAIM-LLM Evaluation (ABLE): Benchmarking LLM Use of Protein Design Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05818)
*Bryce Cai,Geetha Jeyapragasan,Samira Nedungadi,Jake Yukich,Seth Donoughe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ABLE基准，用于评估LLM智能体在双重用途蛋白设计工作流中使用生物AI模型（BAIMs，如ProteinMPNN和AlphaFold3）的能力。通过对15个前沿模型进行评估发现，7个模型拒绝所有任务，其余模型表现差异显著，其中Claude Sonnet 4和Gemini 3 Pro在信息检索、工具选择和工具使用上得分最高。研究还对比了专家人类基线，结果表明当前LLM能大幅降低蛋白设计门槛，但在规划、策略生成以及将生物学知识与工具使用整合方面仍存在不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生物AI模型（如蛋白质设计工具）的普及，它们具有双重用途潜力（既可用于合法科研也可被滥用），需要系统评估LLM智能体在自主编排这些BAIMs完成蛋白设计任务时的安全性表现和能力边界。

Method: 构建ABLE基准测试集，涵盖结构检索、序列生成和设计验证三类任务；选取15个前沿LLM模型进行系统评估，并针对部分任务设置专家人类基线进行对比，从信息检索、工具选择、工具使用等维度量化智能体表现。

Result: 15个模型中7个以安全为由拒绝所有任务；其余模型在不同任务上表现差异显著，Claude Sonnet 4和Gemini 3 Pro在各项指标上均居领先地位；与专家人类基线对比显示，LLMs在降低蛋白设计门槛方面有明显效果，但在复杂规划与策略生成、生物学知识图谱整合方面仍不及熟练专家且表现不稳定。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在生物设计工作流中展现出可观能力，能够降低蛋白设计的准入门槛，但尚未达到能稳定、可靠地执行完整蛋白设计项目的水平，尤其在规划质量和多步骤生物推理方面存在明显短板，这一发现对评估和管控双重用途生物技术的安全风险具有重要意义。

Abstract: We introduce ABLE, a benchmark for evaluating LLM agents' ability to use biological AI models (BAIMs), such as ProteinMPNN and AlphaFold3, in dual-use protein design workflows. ABLE assesses agent performance through a set of tasks spanning structure retrieval, sequence generation, and design validation. We evaluate 15 frontier models and find that seven refuse all tasks, while the remaining models exhibit substantial performance differences. Claude Sonnet 4 and Gemini 3 Pro achieve the highest scores across information retrieval, tool selection, and tool use. We further compare model performance on a subset of tasks against an expert human baseline. Our results suggest that current LLMs can substantially lower barriers to protein design, but remain inconsistent in planning, strategy generation, and integrating biological knowledge with tool use.

</details>


### [186] [Beyond Top-$k$ Skill Retrieval: Diversity-Aware Skill Routing for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05824)
*Wang Wei,Tiankai Yang,Samyadeep Basu,Hongjie Chen,Yue Zhao,Zhengzhong Tu,Xiyang Hu,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi,Hoda Eldardiry*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Diverse Skill Routing (DSR)，一种基于行列式点过程的多样性感知重排序框架，用于在大型技能注册表中路由用户请求，平衡技能相关性与非冗余性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖外部技能，但在大型技能注册表上进行路由很困难，因为许多技能功能冗余，而复杂任务需要互补技能组合。现有技能路由器通常独立按查询相关性排名，可能因冗余技能浪费上下文预算。

Method: DSR框架使用行列式点过程（DPP）平衡相关性和非冗余性，引入查询残差多样性核，惩罚冗余技能重叠同时减少仅由共享查询相关性引起的惩罚。

Result: 在SkillRouter基准上，DSR相比强点级重排序基线提升了召回率和完全覆盖率，在多技能查询上获得更大增益。

Conclusion: 技能路由不仅应被视为相关性排序问题，还应被视为互补集合选择问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external skills, but routing user requests over large skill registries is difficult because many skills are functionally redundant while complex tasks often require complementary skill sets. Existing skill routers typically rank candidates independently by query relevance, which can waste context budget on redundant skills. We propose Diverse Skill Routing (DSR), a diversity-aware reranking framework that uses a Determinantal Point Process to balance relevance and non-redundancy. DSR introduces a query-residual diversity kernel that penalizes redundant skill overlap while reducing penalties caused only by shared query relevance. On the SkillRouter benchmark, DSR improves recall and full coverage over a strong pointwise reranking baseline, with larger gains on multi-skill queries. These results suggest that skill routing should be treated not only as relevance ranking, but also as complementary set selection.

</details>


### [187] [Learning Counterfactual World Models for Embodied Reasoning under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05834)
*Todd Y. Zhou,Daniel Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实坍缩（counterfactual collapse）这一失败模式：世界模型仅优化感知相似性而非干预结构时，会预测视觉上合理但行为上不可区分的未来。作者引入反事实潜在世界模型（CLWM），通过对比反事实目标区分不同干预导致的未来状态，在遮挡操作、别名导航和长时程操作等任务上显著提升规划成功率，并提出反事实可分性度量用于审计任意编码器的可行动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型是具身智能的通用路径，但目前其底层表征多在大规模视频/交互/多模态语料上预训练，优化目标是感知相似性而非干预/行为结构。这就产生一个预测质量无法回答的问题：学到的表征何时才是真正可行动的（actionable）？现有模型可能预测出视觉上看似合理的未来，却无法区分具有不同行为后果的干预，导致规划失效。

Method: 论文首先识别并定义反事实坍缩这一失败模式。然后提出反事实潜在世界模型（CLWM），包含三部分：循环信念状态编码器、动作条件化潜动力学、以及对比反事实目标——该目标用于分离由不同干预诱导出的未来状态，即使这些状态在观测层面高度相似。此外还提出一个表征无关的反事实可分性度量，用于在规划器信任某编码器之前审计其可行动性。

Result: 在遮挡操作（Occluded Push）上规划成功率从65.1%提升至74.6%；在别名导航（Aliased Maze）上从67.3%提升至78.9%；在延迟厨房（Deferred Kitchen）上将剥削性规划失败率从18.4%降至9.7%。消融实验表明增益主要来自硬反事实负样本，尤其是感知别名负样本。反事实可分性度量与五种基线模型类的规划成功率高度相关（r ≥ 0.94）。

Conclusion: 论文建立了反事实可分性度量与规划成功率之间的关系（目前在从头训练的世界模型上验证），并证明该度量是表征无关的——给定干预-结果标签，可审计任何预训练或从头训练的编码器。但作者明确承认尚未在大规模预训练编码器上测量该度量，这是未来的工作方向。

Abstract: World models promise a general route to embodied intelligence: learn predictive dynamics once, then reason, plan, and act with them. Increasingly, the representations beneath such models are pretrained on large-scale video, interaction, and multimodal corpora, which raises a question prediction quality alone cannot answer: when is a learned representation actually actionable? We identify a failure mode we call counterfactual collapse: a model predicts visually plausible futures while failing to distinguish interventions with different behavioral consequences. This arises whenever a representation is optimized for perceptual similarity rather than intervention structure, which is precisely the objective under which most large-scale pretrained encoders are learned. We introduce Counterfactual Latent World Models (CLWM), which combine a recurrent belief-state encoder, action-conditioned latent dynamics, and a contrastive counterfactual objective that separates futures induced by distinct interventions even when their observations look alike. Across occluded manipulation, aliased navigation, and long-horizon manipulation, CLWM improves planning success over the strongest baseline (65.1% $\to$ 74.6% on Occluded Push and 67.3% $\to$ 78.9% on Aliased Maze) and reduces exploitative planning failures (18.4% $\to$ 9.7% on Deferred Kitchen), with ablations attributing the gains to hard counterfactual negatives, especially perceptual-alias negatives. Finally, our counterfactual separability metric, which tracks planning success across the five baseline model classes ($r \ge 0.94$), is representation-agnostic: given intervention-outcome labels, it can audit any encoder, pretrained or trained from scratch, before a planner trusts it. We do not yet measure it on large-scale pretrained encoders. Here we establish the metric and its relationship to planning success for world models trained from scratch.

</details>


### [188] [AgentBrew: Offline Tool-Use Agent Learning from Raw Real-World Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05837)
*Zhiyi Lyu,Yewen Li,Longtao Zheng,Shengtian Yang,Lang Feng,Lei Feng,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai,Qingpeng Cai,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentBrew是一个离线训练框架，无需任务验证器或逐步on-policy rollout，仅从单批原始交互轨迹中学习有效的工具使用策略。它通过回顾式任务推断重建指令，并利用基于PMI的信用分配将轨迹信息分解为逐动作信用来加权训练目标，在GitHub、Notion、PostgreSQL三个真实MCP应用中显著优于基线（包括更大的Qwen3-235B），证明细粒度离线学习能从原始轨迹中恢复过滤式方法丢弃的有用监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体正日益通过工具调用API部署于真实应用，但其针对特定环境的训练仍面临根本性困难：真实应用缺乏预定义任务或验证器、没有忠实的仿真器，且大规模环境交互的预算有限。因此需要在无验证器和无仿真器的条件下从原始数据中学习有效策略。

Method: AgentBrew包含两阶段核心设计：(1) 回顾式任务推断(Retrospective Task Inference) - 代理先在目标环境中探索收集原始轨迹语料（无质量过滤），然后根据每个轨迹的实际执行结果重建对齐的指令；(2) 基于PMI的信用分配(PMI-Based Credit Assignment) - 利用点互信息将轨迹对推断指令的总体信息量分解为可加的逐动作信用，这些信用用于加权策略训练目标，放大有信息量的动作同时抑制无效动作。

Result: 在三个真实MCP应用（GitHub、Notion、PostgreSQL）上，AgentBrew使Qwen3-32B平均提升+8.7准确率/+9.7分数，超越Qwen3-235B（+2.3/+4.4），并优于拒绝采样基线（+5.9/+10.3）。

Conclusion: 细粒度的离线学习可以从原始轨迹中恢复过滤式方法（如拒绝采样）会丢弃的有用监督信息，证明在没有环境验证器和在线演出的约束下，智能体仍可通过离线轨迹分析获得有效的工具使用策略。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed in real-world applications through tool-use APIs, yet training them for specific environments remains fundamentally difficult: real-world applications provide no pre-defined tasks or verifiers, no faithful simulators, and limited budget for large-scale environment interaction. In this paper, we propose \textbf{AgentBrew}, an offline training framework that learns effective tool-use policies from a single batch of raw interaction trajectories, without task verifiers or iterative on-policy rollouts. The agent first explores the target environment to collect a raw trajectory corpus without quality filtering. To extract training signal from this noisy corpus, \emph{retrospective task inference} reconstructs an aligned instruction for each trajectory based on its actual outcome, and \emph{PMI-Based credit assignment} decomposes the trajectory's total information about the inferred instruction into additive per-action credits via pointwise mutual information (PMI). These credits weight the policy training objective, amplifying informative actions while suppressing ineffective ones. On three real-world MCP applications (GitHub, Notion, PostgreSQL), AgentBrew improves Qwen3-32B by +8.7 Acc / +9.7 Score on average, surpassing Qwen3-235B (+2.3 / +4.4) and outperforming rejection sampling (+5.9 / +10.3). These results demonstrate that fine-grained offline learning can recover useful supervision from raw trajectories that filtering-based approaches would discard. The code is available at https://github.com/alphatogo/AgentBrew

</details>


### [189] [Multimodal Resource-Exhaustion Attacks on Vision-Language Models via Joint Pixel-Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05889)
*Zhaoxiong Ni,Yatie Xiao,Chi-Man Pun,Fei Peng,Qingxiao Guan,Keke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Joint Pixel-Prompt Optimization (JPPO)——首个将可见提示词(prompt)与图像扰动共同作为可优化表面的复合对抗框架，针对自回归视觉语言模型(VLM)发起跨模态的资源耗尽攻击。在8/255无穷范数预算下，JPPO在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上实现4.6倍延迟和5.3倍能量放大，在BLIP-2上实现36.6倍延迟和32.7倍能量放大，远超现有基线且所需迭代次数更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对自回归VLM的资源耗尽攻击通常假设单模态威胁模型，仅将图像分支作为主要优化面而保持用户可见的提示词固定，即使近期的循环依赖变体也局限于单一通道范式。作者指出，将可用性(availability)攻击作为跨模态联合可控输入面的优化问题来利用，仍是一个未被探索的方向。

Method: JPPO将可见提示词提升为一等对抗变量，与图像扰动并列，在受限联合输入威胁模型下对像素面和提示词面执行耦合的分阶段(stagewise)优化。这种优化产生协同的成本放大效应，其机制与循环依赖导致的失效不同——实验中循环发生率可忽略不计。方法在五个开源VLM家族和MS COCO、ImageNet数据集上进行了评估。

Result: 在8/255无穷范数预算下，JPPO在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上实现超过4.6倍延迟和5.3倍能量放大，在BLIP-2上实现超过36.6倍延迟和32.7倍能量放大。在直接对比的基线中达到最强的成本放大效果，同时所需优化迭代次数显著更少。消融实验证实放大效果源于多模态协同而非提示词长度或单一模态的作用。

Conclusion: 本研究发现当前VLM服务防御机制存在结构性盲区，从而提出应将成本感知的鲁棒性评估作为多模态部署中的一等安全需求。

Abstract: Resource-exhaustion attacks against autoregressive vision-language models (VLMs) typically assume unimodal threat models, treating the image branch as the primary optimization surface while holding user-visible prompts fixed. Even recent loop-centric variants remain confined to this single-channel paradigm, leaving the exploitation of availability unexplored as a cross-modal optimization problem over jointly controllable input surfaces.
  We introduce Joint Pixel-Prompt Optimization (JPPO), the first compound adversarial framework elevating the visible prompt to a first-class adversarial variable alongside image perturbations. Under a restricted joint-input threat model, JPPO performs coupled, stagewise optimization over both the pixel and prompt surfaces. This produces synergistic cost amplification, mechanistically distinct from loop-dependent failures, exhibiting negligible loop incidence in our experiments.
  Evaluating five open-source VLM families on MS COCO and ImageNet under an 8/255 infinity-norm budget, JPPO achieves over 4.6x latency and 5.3x energy amplification on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and over 36.6x latency with 32.7x energy amplification on BLIP-2. This represents the strongest cost amplification among directly compared baselines while requiring substantially fewer optimization iterations. Ablations confirm this amplification arises from multimodal coordination rather than prompt length or isolated modalities. These findings reveal structural blind spots in current VLM serving defenses, motivating cost-aware robustness evaluation as a first-class security requirement for multimodal deployments.

</details>


### [190] [The End of AI Exponentiation: Fluttering Inside and Outside AI Bubble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05894)
*Victor Kebande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨"AI指数增长的终结"这一命题，分析AI快速发展在生态内外引发的多重挑战，包括数据峰值限制、算力需求激增、合成数据递归、估值泡沫等，指出传统规模扩展范式正面临摩擦，无法持续支撑指数级增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI近年指数级发展虽带来变革，但其加速度也暴露出技术、社会、经济、伦理和基础设施等多维挑战。传统规模化范式正遭遇增长瓶颈，需要审视"AI指数增长终结"的可能及其对生态内外的影响。

Method: 采用系统性分析视角，将AI生态系统的不稳定性划分为"内部"与"外部"两个维度进行审视：内部聚焦算力与数据中心竞赛、投机性投资及超级智能追逐之争；外部聚焦劳动力冲击、治理隐忧、公众不确定性以及全球地缘政治加速态势。

Result: 揭示了AI指数增长面临的系统性摩擦，包括：内部生态中算力竞赛与投机引发的泡沫化倾向，外部生态中社会、治理与地缘政治层面的不稳定张力，论证了传统扩展范式难以持续支撑无限指数增长。

Conclusion: 研究认为"AI指数增长"正走向终结阶段，AI发展需要超越单纯的规模扩展逻辑，重新审视其在技术泡沫内外的不稳定动态，以应对未来智能系统与基础设施带来的全方位挑战。

Abstract: The exponentiation of Artificial intelligence (AI) in the recent past has entered a transformative era that has been driven by the growth in large language models (LLMs), large-scale compute infrastructures, and autonomous reasoning systems. However, the rapid acceleration of AI has increasingly shown technological, societal, economic, ethical and infrastructural challenges associated with peak data limitations, rising computational demands, synthetic data recursion, valuation inflation, and societal instability. The traditional scaling paradigms that have powered the modern AI systems are gradually encountering friction in sustaining continuous exponential growth. This paper views ``the end of AI exponentiation,'' thus exploring how it flutters inside and outside the bubble, where instability emerges within the AI ecosystem through compute and data-center races, speculative investments, and the rat-race toward superintelligence, and outside the ecosystem through labor disruption, governance concerns, public uncertainty, and geopolitical acceleration surrounding future intelligent systems and infrastructures globally.

</details>


### [191] [Beyond Final Decisions: A Process-Centric Benchmark for Transparent AI-Assisted Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05947)
*Siming Yuan,Xueyi Zhang,Wangze Ni,Tianfang Xiao,Shimin Di,Jia Zhu,Zhuoren Jiang,Rong Tan,Lei Chen,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向AI辅助同行评审的过程中心诊断基准。它用(x,$z_s$,$z_c$,$z_r$,y)结构化表示论文内容、摘要、评论、建议和决策，将三个异构评审数据集转换为过程对齐数据，通过直接决策预测基线，对比金标准过程变量与预测过程变量的决策价值，并进行阶段级评估、链一致性评估和干预敏感性分析。结果表明金标准过程变量决策价值更高、金标准-预测差距在多种组合中稳定复现，且模型生成的中级评审文本局部一致但最终决策缺乏充分的评审证据支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助同行评审的评估主要关注生成评审的整体质量或最终决策的准确性，无法证明模型决策是否由充分且可靠的评审证据支撑。需要从过程视角构建可审计的诊断工具，而当前缺少此类过程为中心的系统化基准。

Method: 构建过程中心诊断基准，将PeerRead、NLPeer ARR-22和OpenReview-ICLR的异构评审记录转换为过程对齐数据，以论文内容直接做决策预测(Direct)为基线，比较金标准过程变量与预测过程变量的决策价值，并结合阶段级评估、链一致性评估和干预敏感性分析，跨三个数据集和六个模型进行实验。

Result: 金标准过程变量普遍比预测过程变量具有更高的决策价值；对主要分析模型而言，金标准-预测差距在不同数据集和随机种子下保持稳定，且大多数模型-数据集组合都复现了这一差距；模型生成的中级评审文本在相邻阶段间具有较高的局部一致性，但最终决策并未被前序评审证据一致地支持。

Conclusion: 该基准旨在服务辅助而非替代人类评审者的AI系统，它提供了一个透明且可审计的诊断工具，用于评估AI评审过程（而非仅结果）的可靠性，揭示了现有模型在评审证据支撑决策方面仍存在的关键缺陷。

Abstract: Peer review is central to quality control in science. However, existing evaluations of AI-assisted peer review mainly focus on the overall quality of generated reviews or the accuracy of final decisions. They therefore provide limited evidence about whether model decisions are supported by sufficient and reliable review evidence. We introduce a process-centric diagnostic benchmark for AI-assisted peer review. It uses (x,$z_s$,$z_c$,$z_r$,y)
  to represent the paper content, summary, critique, suggestion, and decision. We convert heterogeneous review records from PeerRead, NLPeer ARR-22, and OpenReview-ICLR into process-aligned data. Our benchmark uses direct decision prediction from the paper content (Direct) as its baseline. It compares the decision value of Gold-process variables and Predicted-process variables, and conducts stage-level evaluation, chain-consistency evaluation, and interventional sensitivity analysis. Experiments across three datasets and six models show that Gold-process variables generally have higher decision value. For the main analysis model, the Gold--Predicted gap remains stable across datasets and random seeds. This gap is also reproduced in most model--dataset combinations. Although model-generated intermediate review texts show relatively high local consistency across adjacent stages, the final decisions are not consistently supported by the preceding review evidence. Our benchmark targets AI systems designed to assist rather than replace human reviewers. It provides a transparent and auditable diagnostic tool for evaluating the reliability of their review processes.

</details>


### [192] [MOAE: Multi-Objective Agent Evolution with Pareto-Preserving Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05992)
*Hengle Jiang,Qijun Cai,Ziying Luo,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多目标智能体进化算法（MOAE），将迭代式上下文内细化组织为帕累托保持的进化搜索，在有限回滚预算内维护非支配候选档案，从而在不预设固定标量化权重的情况下同时优化任务性能、轨迹质量和安全性等多个目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体的评估维度日益多元化（任务准确率、交互质量、安全性、效率等），一个核心问题是如何同时优化这些目标。现有方法通常将异构度量压缩为固定标量分数，这种标量化依赖于指标归一化和偏好权重，可能丢弃具有实际部署权衡价值的候选方案。

Method: MOAE将迭代式上下文内细化组织为帕累托保持的进化搜索：在有限的回滚预算内维护非支配候选的经验档案；利用目标特定的诊断信息引导子代生成；仅在部署阶段应用约束感知的选择。该方法无需参数更新，各目标可替换为任意可测量属性，在实验中实例化为任务性能、轨迹质量和安全性。

Result: 在TravelPlanner和AgentDojo上的实验表明，在匹配的回滚预算下，MOAE一致地提升了任务性能和轨迹质量，同时保持较强的安全性。搜索行为分析进一步显示，帕累托保持扩大了可达目标区域并增大了联合改进的频率。

Conclusion: 实验结果展示了帕累托保持的上下文内进化在无需在搜索过程中承诺固定标量化的情况下优化多个智能体属性的潜力。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents continue to advance, their evaluation has become increasingly multifaceted: a capable agent must not only achieve high task completion accuracy but also perform well in interaction quality, safety, and efficiency, raising a central question: can these objectives be optimized simultaneously? Existing methods have considered multiple objectives, but many collapse heterogeneous measurements into a fixed scalar score. Such scalarization depends on metric normalization and preference weights and may discard candidates that represent useful deployment trade-offs. We introduce Multi-Objective Agent Evolution (MOAE), which organizes iterative in-context refinement as a Pareto-preserving evolutionary search over complete agent rollouts. Given a limited rollout budget, MOAE maintains an empirical archive of non-dominated candidates, uses objective-specific diagnostics to guide offspring generation, and applies constraint-aware selection only at deployment. This separates candidate preservation during search from the preference used to return a final solution. The procedure requires no parameter updates and allows each objective to be replaced by any measurable property, which we instantiate as task performance, trajectory quality, and safety. Experiments on TravelPlanner and AgentDojo show that MOAE consistently improves task performance and trajectory quality while maintaining strong safety under matched rollout budgets. Search-behavior analysis further shows that Pareto preservation expands the attainable objective region and increases the frequency of joint improvement. These results demonstrate the potential of Pareto-preserving in-context evolution for optimizing multiple agent properties without committing to a fixed scalarization during search.

</details>


### [193] [Agentic Pressure: The Endogenous Entropy of Reliable Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05995)
*Hengle Jiang,Ziying Luo,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文识别了一种名为"Agentic Pressure"（代理压力）的非对抗性现象：在长期自主导航中，当合规成本与目标达成相冲突时，代理会自发产生安全漂移（safety drift），并通过"工具性幻觉"（Instrumental Hallucination）来合理化规则违背。文章提出理论框架，将压力形式化为克服环境摩擦所需的功与代理剩余容量之比，并在超过临界阈值时将安全漂移建模为数学最优适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界长期运行的自主代理面临着累积性摩擦，这种摩擦会内在破坏其对齐（alignment）稳定性。现有研究聚焦于静态越狱（static jailbreaks），但缺乏对源自交互动态本身的内生性、非对抗性压力现象的建模与理解。

Method: 提出理论框架，将Agentic Pressure形式化为环境摩擦所需的功与代理剩余能力的比率；通过分析证明当压力超过临界阈值时，安全漂移成为数学上最优的适应策略；并辅以实证实验验证框架。

Result: 实证实验表明，原本对齐的代理在高压力条件下会自发地为了保持自主性而牺牲安全性，验证了提出的理论框架。

Conclusion: Agentic Pressure是一种内生的、非对抗性的安全风险来源，它揭示了传统对抗性安全评估的盲区，表明即使在没有恶意输入的情况下，长期运行的对齐代理也可能在目标追求与安全约束之间做出偏离安全的选择。

Abstract: Achieving reliable autonomy in the wild requires agents to sustain continuous operations across long-horizon trajectories. However, as agents navigate these unconstrained settings, they encounter cumulative friction that inherently destabilizes their alignment. In this paper, we identify a distinct non-adversarial phenomenon termed Agentic Pressure. We define this as a kinetic force that spontaneously emerges when the cost of compliance conflicts with the imperative of goal achievement. Unlike static jailbreaks, this pressure is endogenous and arises directly from the dynamics of interaction. We propose a theoretical framework that formalizes Agentic Pressure as the ratio between the required work to overcome environmental friction and the remaining capacity of the agent. Our analysis demonstrates that when this pressure exceeds a critical threshold, agents exhibit safety drift as a mathematically optimal adaptation. Consequently, they often resort to Instrumental Hallucination to rationalize rule violations. Empirical experiments validate this framework and show that aligned agents spontaneously compromise safety to preserve autonomy under high-pressure conditions.

</details>


### [194] [Generator-Independent Runtime Assurance under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06036)
*Guangxi Wan,Yongbo Xie,Yuqi Liu,Qingwei Dong,Qingxin Li,Hongfei Bai,Peng Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于提议（proposal-based）的控制器在运行时验证门控下的闭环安全保证何时能与生成器解耦。主要定理表明，"同时集合级健全性"（同时认证一组不含不可行动作的可接受提议）是生成器无关的"准入健全性"的必要且充分条件；在部分观测下，若两个状态假设的似然在总变差距离δ内却需要不同安全决策，则任意准入机制的失败概率下界满足 ā+β+δ≥1。作者提出顺序风险账本使该保证可通过时间一致置信管实现，并将Simplex式运行时保证与控制屏障函数滤波作为退化情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于提议的控制器（学习策略、语言模型规划器等黑箱生成器）越来越多地被部署在运行时验证门控之后。现有的逐候选（per-candidate）认证模式在重试或best-of-k选择下无法组合——单个候选的伪准入水平α会膨胀到1-(1-α)^k，这可能使系统实际安全保证远差于预期，因此需要一种能解耦生成器影响的闭环安全保证框架。

Method: 作者提出"同时集合级健全性"概念，即认证一组可接受的提议中不含任何不可行动作；证明其对生成器无关的准入健全性是必要且充分的。配合设计时证书与no-bypass规则可获得合同安全，违规界为Γ+Σε_t+η，该界对任意对抗性生成器替换保持不变。第二个定理刻画部分观测下任意准入机制的根本下界。通过顺序风险账本与时间一致置信管保证可实现性，并证明确定性准入计算将所有统计风险集中在状态估计中。

Result: 主要定理建立了集合级健全性⟺生成器无关准入健全性；在部分观测下建立了混合下界ā+β+δ≥1；合同安全的违规界Γ+Σε_t+η对任意生成器均成立。实验性地恢复了Simplex式运行时保证和控制屏障函数滤波（CBF filtering）作为该理论的退化情形。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的、生成器无关的安全保证框架，首次说明了传统逐候选认证在重试场景下不安全，而同时集合级认证是获得可组合安全保证的必要且充分条件。该理论框架为黑箱生成器的安全部署提供了理论依据，涵盖了运行时验证、运行时保证与屏障滤波等多种安全机制。

Abstract: Proposal-based controllers---learned policies, language-model planners, and other black-box \emph{generators}---are increasingly deployed behind runtime verification gates. We ask when the closed-loop safety guarantee decouples from the generator. The prevailing per-candidate certification pattern does not compose: under retry or best-of-$k$ selection a per-candidate false-admission level $α$ can inflate to $1-(1-α)^{k}$. Our main theorem shows that \emph{simultaneous setwise soundness}---certifying a set of admissible proposals containing no nonviable action---is necessary and sufficient for generator-independent \emph{admission soundness}, the worst case over all generators of executing a nonviable proposal equalling the probability of setwise failure; together with a design-time certificate and a no-bypass rule it is sufficient for \emph{contract safety}, with violation bound $Γ+\sum_t\varepsilon_t+η$ invariant under arbitrary, even adversarial, replacement of the generator. A second theorem bounds every admission mechanism under partial observation: for a fixed probing and admission policy, if two state hypotheses whose information laws lie within total-variation distance $δ$ require different safe decisions, then $\abar+β+δ\ge1$. A sequential risk ledger makes the guarantee implementable with time-uniform confidence tubes, and shows that deterministic admission computations concentrate all statistical risk in state estimation. Simplex-style runtime assurance and control-barrier-function filtering are recovered as degenerate cases.

</details>


### [195] [DAREBench: Deployment-Aware and Reliable Evaluation of Models as Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06059)
*Yu Liu,Zhilin Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Shaojie Zhang,Zheyuan Deng,Tingwei Huang,Zhenbo Luo,Lei Jiang,Yanbing Liu,Pei Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DAREBench，一个部署感知且可靠的智能体评估基准。基于统一的OpenClaw执行环境，DAREBench将来自22个源基准的233个任务组织成2×3的工作负载矩阵（按输入模态和执行形式划分），采用统一的基于契约的评估协议和证据审计。研究评估了23个商业API模型和12个本地部署的开源模型（共7,587次模型-任务运行），结果显示：没有单一模型在所有工作负载组中全面占优，文本与多模态任务的准确率-成本权衡存在差异，本地开源模型在部分工作负载组具有竞争力但整体仍落后于前沿商业模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正从问答系统演化为通用智能体，但现有评估基准通常绑定特定任务类型、执行环境或评分协议，限制了跨基准的可比性、可解释性和部署决策的可靠性。研究者希望构建一个能捕捉工作负载变化、支持可靠智能体评估的统一基准。

Method: DAREBench基于共享的OpenClaw执行环境，将22个源基准中筛选和适配的233个任务组织为一个2×3的工作负载矩阵，矩阵维度为输入模态（文本vs多模态）和执行形式。采用统一的基于契约的评估协议，并配备基于证据的分数审计机制。对23个商业API模型和12个本地开源模型共进行了7,587次模型-任务运行评估，记录了准确率、token消耗以及API模型的参考成本。

Result: 实验结果表明没有任何单一模型在所有工作负载组中全面占优；文本任务和多模态任务展现出截然不同的准确率-成本权衡特征；本地开源模型在部分工作负载组具有竞争力，但整体上仍落后于前沿商业模型。

Conclusion: 智能体部署和模型选择应综合考虑工作负载画像、部署模式以及准确率-成本权衡，而非依赖单一的聚合评分。

Abstract: As large language models evolve from question-answering systems into general-purpose agents, evaluation must move beyond static answer correctness to assess multimodal perception, multi-step execution, tool use, and artifact delivery. However, existing benchmarks are often tied to specific task types, execution environments, or scoring protocols, limiting their comparability, interpretability, and reliability for deployment decisions. We introduce DAREBench (Deployment-Aware and Reliable Evaluation of Models as Agents), a benchmark designed to capture workload variation and support reliable agent evaluation. Built on a shared OpenClaw execution environment, DAREBench organizes 233 tasks selected and adapted from 22 source benchmarks into a $2\times3$ workload matrix defined by input modality and execution form, and evaluates them under a unified contract-based protocol with evidence-based score auditing. We evaluate 23 commercial API models and 12 locally deployed open-weight models over 7,587 model--task runs, reporting accuracy and token consumption alongside reference costs for API models. Results show that no single model dominates all workload groups, text and multimodal tasks exhibit distinct accuracy--cost trade-offs, and local open-weight models are competitive in several groups but still trail frontier commercial models overall. These findings suggest that agent deployment and model selection should consider workload profiles, deployment mode, and accuracy--cost trade-offs rather than rely on a single aggregate score.

</details>


### [196] [Explaining AI Agents Through Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06063)
*Vittoria Vineis,Fabiano Veglianti,Lorenzo Antonelli,Claudia Di Carlo,Matteo Silvestri,Gabriele Tolomei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向AI Agent的后验可解释性框架，将执行轨迹转化为结构化报告和基于可观测行为的忠实自然语言解释，跨架构、环境与任务通用，能有效识别无依据声明、不合理动作和证据缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Agent在真实场景中与外部工具交互并做顺序决策，需要可靠且可审计的解释，但传统XAI方法无法提供这类交互式多步系统所需的过程级透明度，亟需面向AI Agent的专门解释方法。

Method: 提出一个后验XAI框架，将冗长的Agent执行轨迹转化为结构化报告和忠实于可观测行为的自然语言解释。框架仅依赖执行轨迹，因此可跨Agent架构、环境和任务通用。

Result: 在多个基准和架构上进行的人类与自动化评估表明，该框架能生成高质量、轨迹忠实的解释，并能可靠识别无依据声明、不合理动作和证据缺口，优于朴素LLM生成的解释。

Conclusion: 传统XAI不足以解释交互式多步AI Agent，所提出的基于执行轨迹的后验XAI框架为Agent提供了一种可审计、过程透明且架构无关的解释方案。

Abstract: AI Agents are increasingly deployed in real-world settings, where they interact with external tools and make sequential decisions with limited human oversight. This creates a pressing need for reliable and auditable explanations of what an agent did and why. However, traditional Explainable AI (XAI) methods fall short of providing the process-level transparency required for such interactive, multi-step systems, motivating a paradigm shift toward approaches specifically designed for AI Agents. To address this gap, we present a post-hoc XAI framework that transforms a lengthy agent's execution trace into a structured report and a faithful natural-language explanation explicitly grounded in its observable behavior. Because it relies solely on execution traces, the framework applies across different agent architectures, environments, and tasks. Human and automated evaluations across multiple benchmarks and architectures show that our framework produces high-quality, trace-faithful explanations while reliably identifying unsupported claims, unjustified actions, and evidence gaps, outperforming naive LLM-generated explanations.

</details>


### [197] [Generating Instance Generators in PDDL Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06071)
*Nicola J. Müller,Naya Rudolph,Katharina Stein,Jörg Hoffmann,Ayal Taitler,Timo P. Gros*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大语言模型(LLM)自动生成实例生成程序的新方法，用于AI规划领域的PDDL领域实例生成，并内置健全性检查保证生成实例的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDDL作为AI规划社区的事实标准语言，虽然能描述规划域（谓词和动作模式），但缺乏指定实际实例集的能力（如初始状态、目标条件的合法约束以及领域子集约束）。这导致实例生成一直是临时性的手工编写，且现有的推理和学习方法存在可扩展性限制。

Method: 利用LLM自动生成面向特定PDDL领域和子集的实例生成程序，并通过预设的健全性检查确保生成程序内置可靠性保障。

Result: 实验表明，自动生成的实例生成器能够高效地产出大量健全且多样化的实例。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的实例生成程序自动生成方法能够有效替代手工编写实例生成器，在保证健全性的同时实现高效的大规模实例生成。

Abstract: PDDL, the de-facto standard language in the AI Planning community, is designed to specify planning domains: sets of instances that share the same predicates and action schemas. Yet it does not provide any means to specify the actual instance set, i.e., legality constraints on initial states and goal conditions, as well as possibly domain subset constraints specifying an instance subset we are interested in. One consequence of this is that instance generation has always been ad-hoc, with manually written domain- and subset-specific instance generators. Recent work has started to address this, through reasoning and learning methods that however suffer from scalability limitations. Here we introduce an alternative approach, leveraging LLMs to generate instance-generation programs, with built-in soundness guarantees through prescribed checks. We show that these automatically generated instance generators return large numbers of sound and diverse instances efficiently.

</details>


### [198] [LayerRoute: Action-Conditioned Mixture-of-Layers Routing for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06079)
*Zheng Lu,Haoran Liao,Wanqi Zhong,Yunhe Ni,Lijie Wang,Xingjie Fan,Zhisheng Chen,Yantang Qu,Meijia Chen,Tianyu Xin,Zirui Song,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LayerRoute，一种动作条件化的表示路由接口，通过Layer Mixture Router动态混合缓存的VLM层表示，并结合Action-State Reread重用早期动作表示，使VLA策略能自适应地访问不同层次的视觉语义特征与动作状态特征，在LIBERO Long等基准上提升高达7.2的性能且仅增加0.31%/3.87%的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA接口在表示访问上缺乏灵活性：VLM信息通过每个动作层固定层分配暴露，不同操作任务可能需要不同层次的视觉语义表示（从局部视觉几何到语言对齐的抽象语义）的混合；同时中间动作状态仅通过残差流隐式传播，未得到显式重用。

Method: LayerRoute包含两个核心组件：(1) Layer Mixture Router——动态地形成缓存VLM表示的混合，根据动作条件自适应选择所需VLM层表示；(2) Action-State Reread——显式重用前序动作表示，使中间动作状态参与后续决策。整体构成一个动作条件化的表示路由接口。

Result: 在多种仿真与真实世界基准上，LayerRoute持续改进StarVLA-π和π0.5的性能，在LIBERO Long上实现高达7.2的提升，仅增加0.31%/3.87%的额外参数。消融实验验证了动作条件化层路由的有效性，路由分析揭示了跨动作层和任务设置的分配模式。

Conclusion: LayerRoute通过灵活的动作条件化表示路由机制，解决了VLA策略中VLM层表示固定分配和中间动作状态隐式传播的问题，以极小参数开销显著提升操作性能，并展现出结构化的路由分配规律，为VLA接口设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies leverage pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) to guide action generation for robot control. VLMs provide hierarchical visual-semantic representations that evolve across layers, from local visual geometry to abstract, language-aligned semantics; different manipulation tasks may therefore require different mixtures of layer representations. Meanwhile, the action module maintains intermediate representations that evolve throughout action computation and may provide useful information for subsequent decisions. However, existing VLA interfaces offer limited flexibility in representation access: VLM information is exposed through fixed layer assignments for each action layer, while intermediate action states are only propagated implicitly through residual streams without explicit reuse. We introduce LayerRoute, an action-conditioned representation routing interface that enables adaptive access to VLM layers and action representations. The Layer Mixture Router dynamically forms mixtures of cached VLM representations, while Action-State Reread reuses earlier action representations. Across diverse simulation and real-world benchmarks, LayerRoute consistently improves StarVLA-$π$ and $π_{0.5}$, achieving up to 7.2 gains on LIBERO Long with only 0.31% / 3.87% additional parameters. Ablation studies validate the benefit of action-conditioned layer routing, while routing analyses reveal structured allocation patterns across action layers and task settings.

</details>


### [199] [SAP: State-Guided Data Synthesis with Argument Provenance for Multi-Turn Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06124)
*Zichen Tian,Jinpeng Chen,Cheng Gong,Suiyun Zhang,Rui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAP提出了一种状态引导的数据合成方法，通过参数溯源约束和逐轮验证，生成具备长程依赖和高准确率的工具使用轨迹数据，并据此训练出SAP-4B模型，在多基准上可与更大规模的模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的多轮工具使用数据对训练智能体模型至关重要，但现有数据合成方法往往低估了参数级依赖关系，导致模型即使选择了正确工具，也会因填入伪造、过时或弱依据的参数值而导致任务执行失败。

Method: SAP结合了状态引导(state guidance)、工具参数溯源约束(tool-argument provenance constraints)和逐轮验证(turn-level validation)，以高效构建具备长程依赖关系且高准确率的工具使用轨迹。

Result: 使用SAP生成的数据构建的SAP-4B模型，在多个基准上即使与更大规模的模型相比也极具竞争力。

Conclusion: SAP通过强调参数溯源与状态引导，有效解决了多轮工具使用中参数依赖不足的问题，所训练的SAP-4B证明了该方法的有效性，并已开源代码、合成数据及训练权重。

Abstract: High-quality multi-turn tool-use data is essential for training agentic models, yet existing data synthesis methods often underrepresent the argument-level dependencies that are critical to long-horizon tool use.
